vaṭivaṅkaḷ










cāttaṉkuḷam
a. irākavaṉ










amiḻtam patippakam
cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ nūṟkaḷañciyam tokuti  aintu
iṟivaṉiṉ eṇvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷ | cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ | 
patippāḷar : i. vaḷarmati | mutal patippu : 2006 |  
tāḷ : 18.6 ki mēplittō | aḷavu : 1/8 temmi | eḻuttu : 10.5 puḷḷi | 
pakkam : 12+260 = 272 | nūl kaṭṭamippu : iyalpu (cātāraṇam) | 
vili : urupā. 255 | paṭikaḷ : 1000 | nūlākkam : caravaṇaṉ, malar
aṭṭi vaṭivamippu : i. iṉiyaṉ, pāvāṇar kaṇiṉi, tiyākarāyar nakar, 
ceṉṉi - 17 | acciṭṭōr : veṅkaṭēcuvarā āpceṭ piriṇṭar āyiram viḷakku, ceṉṉi - 6 | veḷiyīṭu : amiḻtam patippakam, 
pi-11, kulmōkar kuṭiyiruppu, 15, teṟku pōkku cāli, tiyākarāyar nakar, ceṉṉi - 600 017 | kiṭikkumiṭam : tamiḻmaṇ patippakam, 2 ciṅkāravēlar teru, tiyākarāyarnakar ceṉṉi - 600 017, to.pē: 2433 9030
innūlākkattiṟku utaviyavarkaḷ :
pērā. vī. aracu maṟṟum āyvāḷar, ira. piruntāvati.

patippuri
empatippakam tamiḻmoḻi, iṉam, kali, nākarikam,  paṇpāṭu, ici, nuṇkalikaḷ, tolliyal āyvu  toṭarpāṉa ariya ceytikaḷ aṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷit tēṭiyeṭuttu itukāṟum veḷiyiṭṭu varukiṟatu.  kaṭanta kālattil nūlāciriyar cāttaṉkuḷam a.irākavaṉ  eḻutiya eṭṭu nūlkaḷi amiḻtam patippakam vāyilāka veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻum iṟivaṉiṉ eṇvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉum innūl kiyeḻuttup paṭiyāka nūlāciriyar irākavaṉ avarkaḷiṉ makaṉār irā.mativāṇaṉ avarkaḷiṭam iruntatu. eṅkaḷuṭiya vēṇṭukōḷi ēṟṟu maṉamuvantu kiyeḻuttuppaṭiyiṉi  koṭuttu utaviṉār.  itaṉi  mutaṉmutalāka veḷiyiṭu
kiṉṟōm.   
moḻikkum iṉattiṟkum perumi cērkkiṉṟa vakiyil ariya nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭuvarum eṅkaḷ tamiḻppaṇikku ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻakat tamiḻilakkiyat
tuṟiyiṉ talivar pērā. vī. aracu avarkaḷ  tōṉṟāt tuṇiyāka iruntu varukiṟār. avaruṭiya vaḻikāṭṭutaliṉ pēriltāṉ nūlāciriyariṉ iṉṉapiṟa nūlkaḷum veḷi
varukiṉṟaṉa.  inta nūlkaḷ ceppamākavum nalla vaṭivamip
pōṭum varuvataṟku   pallāṟṟāṉum tuṇi iruntu utaviyavar. mēlum, innūlukku muṉṉuri aḷittuc ciṟappu ceytuḷḷa pi.irāmanātaṉ avarkaḷukku em neñcārnta naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatākum.  
nuṇkalic celvar irākavaṉ avarkaḷ eḻuti avaruṭiya kālattil veḷivanta nūlkaḷiyum, veḷi
varāmal kiyeḻuttup paṭiyāka iruntavaṟṟiyum , kuṭiyaracu, jaṉacakti, aṟivu, tamiḻmuracu itaḻkaḷil veḷivanta avaruṭiya kaṭṭurikaḷiyum tokuttu nūl kaḷañciyaṅ
kaḷi   poruḷ vāriyākap pirittu  patiṉāṟu tokuti
kaḷāka uṅkaḷ kikaḷil tavaḻaviṭṭuḷḷōm. marapu karuti mūla nūlil uḷḷavārē veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. 
celvi ira. piruntāvati, pērā. aracu avar
kaḷiṉ āyvu māṇavar. ivar pērāciriyariṉ vaḻi kāṭṭutalōṭu palvēṟu vakiyil paṅkāṟṟiyum in nūlkaḷ piḻiyiṉṟi varuvataṟku meyppup pārttum utaviṉār. celvi piruntāvati avarkaḷi naṉṟi yuṇarvōṭu pārāṭṭukiṟēṉ.
tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattic cērnta pulavar mutturāmaliṅkam avarkaḷ pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ veḷiyiṭṭapōtu pallāṟṟāṉum tuṇiyirunta perumikkuriyavar. avar aṇmiyil maṟintu viṭṭār. maṟivukku muṉpāka  nūlāciriyariṉ taṅki vīralakkumi ammiyāriṭamum, makaṉ irā. mativāṇaṉiṭamum urimiyuri vāṅki utaviyatōṭu it tokutikaḷ veḷivaruvataṟkup peritum tuṇi iruntavar. avarukkum em naṉṟi. innūl tokutikaḷ nalla vaṭivamippōṭu veḷivaruvataṟku utaviya tirumati. celvi (kuṭvil kaṇiṉi) avarkaḷukkum, meyppup pārttu utaviya ki. kuṇat tokiyaṉ, celvi piruntāvati, tirumati kaliyaraci, celvi kōkilā, celvi aru.apirāmi  ākiyōrkkum,  nūlkaḷ naṉmuṟiyil varuvataṟku mikavum utaviyāka irunta araṅka. kumarēcaṉ, mu.na. irāmacupramaṇiya rācā, ciṟanta vakiyil vaṭivamittu oḻuṅkupaṭuttiya kaṇiṉi iyakkunar malar, mēlaṭṭiyi aḻakuṟa vaṭivamippu ceyta iṉiyaṉ maṟṟum piṟa vakikaḷil tuṇi irunta  vē. taṉacēkaraṉ,ila.tarumarācu, 
re. vijayakumār  ākiyōrkku em naṉṟiyum, pārāṭṭum.
innūl tokutikaḷ tamiḻ āyvāḷarkaḷukkum tamiḻ uṇarvāḷarkaḷukkum peritum payaṉpaṭattakka ariya nūlkaḷākum. itaṉi aṉivarukkum payaṉpaṭat takka vakiyil veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷōm. vāṅkip payaṉ peṟuvīr.

	patippāḷar

urimiyuri
nuṇkalic celvar cāttaṉkuḷam a. irākavaṉ avarkaḷ tamiḻkkalikaḷ paṟṟi virivāka āyvu 
ceytu nūlkaḷ eḻutiyavar. avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ ippoḻutu mīṇṭum accāvatu eṅkaḷukku mikunta makiḻcciyi aḷikkiṟatu. innūlkaḷ mīṇṭum accākumā? eṉṟa cantēkattil irunta eṅkaḷukku ic ceyal mikunta makiḻcci aḷikkum ceyalākum. tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭu varalāṟu, kali varalāṟu, toḻil nuṭpa varalāṟu, tolporuḷāyvu varalāṟu ākiya pala tuṟikaḷil nuṇkalic celvar irākavaṉār eḻutiya nūlkaḷit tamiḻulakam pōṟṟip pārāṭṭum eṉṟa nampikki eṅkaḷukku uṇṭu.
pala nūlkaḷ kiṭittum cila nūlkaḷ kiṭik kāmalum iruntatik kaṇṭu kavali aṭinta eṅkaḷukku amiḻtam patippakattār mūlam in nūlkaḷ veḷi varuvatu eṅkaḷ kuṭumpattiṟkum tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkum perum ciṟappu eṉṟē karutukiṟōm. tamiḻmaṇ patippaka urimiyāḷar kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ avarkaḷukku eṅkaḷatu naṉṟi eṉṟum uriyatu. amiḻtam patippakattiṉ mūlam innūli veḷiyiṭum tiru iṉiyaṉ avarkaḷi nāṅkaḷ peritum pōṟṟip pārāṭṭukiṟōm. amiḻtam patippakattār nuṇkalic celvar nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭuvati vāḻtti varavēṟṟu makiḻkiṟōm.

irā. mativāṇaṉ
tirunelvēli	(aṟiñar a. irākavaṉiṉ makaṉ)
nāḷ	kā. vīralaṭcumi ammiyār
30.12.2005	(aṟiñar a. irākavaṉiṉ taṅki)

muṉṉuri
cāttaṉkuḷam a.irākavaṉ paṇṭit tamiḻar tamiḻaka varalāṟu paṟṟiyum, av varalāṟu cārnta araciyal, camūkam, poruḷiyal, paṇpāṭu. kali, camayam pōṉṟa palvēṟu uṭpulaṅkaḷ paṟṟiyum āḻntu paṭittu ārāyntu ariya nūlkaḷi eḻutiyuḷḷār. innūlil civaperumāṉiṉ eṇ vaki eḻiṟkōlaṅkaḷ (aṭṭa mūrttaṅkaḷ) , antantak kōlac ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, vaḷarcci, aṭṭa mūrtta vaṭivaṅkaḷil muṉṉōr kaṇṭa aḷavilāta tattuvaṅkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟi eḻutiyuḷḷār. eṭṭuk kōlaṅkaḷāvaṉa:
teṉmukakkaṭavuḷ (taṭcaṇāmūrtti)
maṇavaḻakar(kaliyāṇacuntara mūrtti)
cōmakanta cukācaṉa mūrtti
cantiracēkara mūrtti
kālacammāra mūrtti
tiripura cammāra mūrtti
kacacammāra mūrtti
piccāṭaṉamūrtti
eṉpaviyām. ovvoru kōlattiyum -akkōlac ciṟpam  cārnta toṉmam, ākamakkūṟṟukaḷ, ciṟpanūl viḷakkam, paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ viḷakkam, tattuva viḷakkam, ilakkiyac cāṉṟukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟuṭaṉ, niralpaṭa viḷakkiyuḷḷār. iyalkaḷ 2-7 il civacamayam, ākamaṅkaḷ, iliṅkaṅkaḷ, mūrttaṅkaḷ, civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu, paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam, paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavimuṟi, aṭṭamūrttaṅkaḷ paṟṟiya potucceytikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟit tarukiṉṟaṉa. iyalkaḷ 8-13 aṭṭamūrttaṅkaḷit taṉittaṉiyē viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. civaṉiyak koṇmuṭivu paṟṟip payilpavar
kaḷukkum, tamiḻaka ciṟpa, paṭimakkali paṟṟi aṟiya virumpu
pavarukkum itu ciṟanta  karuvi nūlākum.
2) innūlil āṅkāṅkē irākavaṉ valiyuṟuttum paṇṭit tamiḻar camayam paṟṟiya potuc ceytikaḷil mutaṉmi
yāṉavi: tamiḻar camayamē piṟṟiya intu camayattukku aṭippaṭiyāṉatu; civavaḻipāṭē intiyāvilēyē ēṉ ulakilēyē kālattāl muntiyatu; cintuveḷi nākarikam tol
tamiḻa nākarikamē - moḻi, paṇpāṭu, camayam ākiya aṉittuk kūṟukaḷilum; tamiḻariṉ tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭum, civavaḻipāṭum cintuveḷi nākarika kālattiliruntē (ēṉ ataṟku muṉṉiruntē) toṭarntu varupavi;ākamaṅkaḷ tamiḻaruṭiyaṉa; mutalil tamiḻil irunta avikaḷum ciṟpa nūlkaḷum piṉṉar camakirutattil moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭaṉa; pōṉṟaviyām. ikkaruttuk
kaḷi āṅkāṅkē innūlil pala iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇalām. ivaṟṟukkāṉa ātāraṅkaḷuḷ irākavaṉ eḻutiyataṟku muṉṉamē veḷivantavi cila (mārṣal, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ, kā.cuppiramaṇiyapiḷḷi pōṉṟōr karuttukkaḷ); piṉṉar aṇmikkālattil veḷivantavi cila. ivviruvakiyilum mutaṉmi vāynta ātāraṅkaḷ kīḻē orucērat tarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa, innūlip paṭippavar naṉmi karuti .
3) iṉṟiya intu camayak kaṭavuḷarum vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷum muṟṟilum tamiḻiya (tirāviṭa) cārpuṭiyaviyē; āriyariṭamiruntu peṟṟavi alla eṉpati pi.ṭi.cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ pōṉṟōrum, ciṟanta mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarum niṟuviyuḷḷaṉar. intu camayak kōṭpāṭukaḷil aṭippaṭiyākavum ciṟantaṉavākavum uḷḷaviyellām vaṭanāṭṭil paravalāka 3000 āṇṭukaṭku muṉ vāḻnta tamiḻ mēṉmakkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟappaṭṭavi eṉpatiyum, avaṟṟi āriyaruṭiyaviyākak koḷvatu aṟiyāmi eṉpatiyum maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ tamatu mullippāṭṭu ārāycciyuriyilum piṟṟi nūlkaḷilum niṟuviyuḷḷār. kā.cuppiramaṇiyapiḷḷi (tamiḻar camayam 1940), tēvanēyap pāvāṇar (tamiḻar matam 1972) ākiyōrukkum akkaruttu uṭaṉpāṭēyākum. kaḷum puṭkaram, mirukēntiram mutalāṉa cila civākamaṅ
kaḷum piṟavumām eṉpatu aṭikaḷ muṭivu. im muṭivukkāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷāka aṭikaḷ tantuḷḷa palavaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmiyāṉa cila varumāṟu:
irukku vētattiṟ paratar mutaliya peyarkaḷāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa paṇṭit tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷē ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum muttī vaṭivil vittu muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa civa pirāṉi vaḻipaṭum nuṭpa muṟiyik kaṇṭōrāvar (1930:māṇikkavācakar varalāṟum kālamum)
vicuvāmittirar eṉṉum tamiḻaraca muṉivarāl ceytu cērkkappaṭṭa irukku vēta  mūṉṟām maṇṭilattil uḷḷa kāyattiri mantiramāṉatu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ kaṇ muḷittu viḷaṅkum parkkaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭiya civapirāṉ mēṟṟāy viḷaṅkukiṟatu (1923:tamiḻar nākarikam)
kollā aṟatti mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṇṭaṟintu ataṟkēṟpat tamatu immi vāḻkkiyi naṭattiṉavarkaḷ tamiḻaril mēṉ makkaḷāy irunta perumpakutiyārē... maṟṟi makkaṭpiriviṉari
luḷḷa enta mēlōrum ivvaru maruntaṉṉa uṇmiyiṉik kaṇṭaṟintilar... vaṭakkirunta camaṇar, cākkiyar, cāṅkiyar, yōkar mutalāyiṉāruṅkollā vaṟattiṉi viṭāp piṭiyāyk koṇṭu oḻukiṉarallarō veṉil; camaṇa cākkiyar mutalāṉa avarellām āriyar varutaṟku muṉṉamē imayamali variyiṟ paravi nākarikattiṟ ciṟantu uyir vāḻnta vaṭanāṭṭut tamiḻmēṉ makkaḷiṉ marapil vantavarē (1941 tamiḻar matam)
camaṇa, puttak karuttukkaḷ āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭanāṭṭut tirāviṭa meyyiyalil piṟkāla vaḷarccikaḷē eṉpar car jāṉ mārṣalum (1934 mokañcotarōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum)
4. aṭikaḷiṉ mēṟcoṉṉa āyvuk karuttukkaḷiṉ karuvip piṉvarumāṟu kā.appātturiyār 1956il (1800 āṇṭukaṭku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻakam tamiḻākkattiṉ aṭik
kuṟippil) curuṅkak kuṟittār:
tamiḻar paṇṭiyac camaya vāḻvu iṉṟiya pala camayaṅ
kaḷiṉ uruvākā mūlakkaru mutalit taṉṉiṭamākak koṇṭa vāḻvēyākum. atil ellāc camaya mūlamumkāṇalām...ò¤j, camaṇa, civa, viṇavak karuttukkaḷ uruvākumuṉ,a¢camaya¥peyarkaŸvēṟuvēṟāka¤tamiḻa®neṟiyiṉiṉWpiriyumuṉ,āriya«ilniṉWtamiḻari‹oUneṟiyi¥palavāWpirikkumuṉ,avvellahneṟikaḷukku«vaḻikāṭṭiamifmuṟpaṭlkāla¤tamiœaṟiva®maraòcārnjaṟiñaruŸoruvutiruvaḷḷuva®eṉpaJuyttuṇara¤takfuṇmiyākum”
5(i) ipporuḷ kuṟittu mēlum cila mēṉāṭṭaṟiñar kūṟṟukkaḷum maṉatiṟ koḷḷattakkaṉa:-
heṉric cimmar: vaṭa mēṟkiliruntu nuḻinta āriyarkaḷāl makatam mutaliya kīḻttici nāṭukaḷilirunta āriyarallāta mēṉmakkaḷ muḻumiyāka iṭam peyarntilar. attakiya mēṉmakkaḷ kuṭumpaṅkaḷ pala toṭarntu iruntaṉa. āriya āḷum kuṭumpaṅkaḷ valuviḻantu vīḻat toṭaṅkiya piṉ muntiya uḷḷūr āḷum kuṭumpaṅkaḷ mīṇṭum valuppeṟṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka cantirakupta muriyaṉ ittakiya kuṭumpattic cārntavaṉ. puttarum avvāṟē. (The Philosophies of India 1951)
leviṉ: intu camayattil yāṇṭum kāṇappaṭum uruva vaḻipāṭu intiyāvil irunta āriyar allātār vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷil iruntē pirāmaṇīyattukku vantatu. (G.M.Bongard Levin/ A complex study of Ancient India 1986)
vālparṭ: mokañcōtarō muttiriyil kāṇappaṭupavar pōṉṟa (āriyarkaḷukku muntiya intiya) yōkikaḷ vantēṟikaḷ moḻiyic cila kālattukkuḷ kaṟṟuk koṇṭu tamatu camaya maṟi poruṭkaḷi (vētap pāṭalkaḷi iyaṟṟiya) pirāmaṇarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉarō? (Stanley Wolpert: A New History of India 1982)
vālparṭ: āriya nāṭōṭikaḷiṉ muraṭṭup paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, avarkaḷuṭiya kaṭumiyāṉa perumpōrkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟukkum ahimcikkum (uyirkaḷukku ūṟu ceyyāmi) neṭuntolivu ākiyāl, yōkam, tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭu pōṉṟavaṟṟip pōla ahimciyum āriyarkaḷukkum muṟpaṭṭa (cintu veḷit tamiḻ) makkaḷiṭamiruntē avarkaḷ peṟṟatāka nām karuta vēṇṭiyuḷḷatu.
civaṉ vaḻipāṭu vaṭa intiyāvi viṭa teṉṉāṭṭil tāṉ makkaḷiṭam mutaṉmi peṟṟuḷḷatu. teṉṉāṭṭut tirāviṭa makkaḷ āriyarkaḷukku neṭuṅkālattukku muṉṉarē aṅku nilipeṟṟu iruntamiyi itu cāṭṭuvatākalām. tāyt teyva vaḻipāṭum teṉṉāṭṭiltāṉ mikuntu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ( Wolpert: An Introduction to India 1991)
rāparṭ: iṉṟu vaḻipaṭappaṭum intuk kaṭavuḷarkaḷil talimic ciṟappu vāyntavarkaḷil oruvaṉ civaṉ. tolpaḻaṅkālattil iruntaviyum, makkaḷiṉap perukkam avarkaḷ celvap perukkam ākiyavaṟṟi nōkkiyaviyumāṉa vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷ pala civaṉ vaḻipāṭṭil iṇintuḷḷaṉa. toṉmiyāṉa civaṉ vaṭivam pōṉṟa oṉṟi mokañcōtarō muttiri oṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷatu. civaṉ aṭiyāḷamākak koḷḷappaṭuvatum taṟkālak kōyilkaḷil vaḻipaṭap paṭuvatum āṉa liṅkam pōṉṟa kaṟkaḷ hārappā nākarika eccaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. eṉavē iṉṟu ulakattil niṉṟu nilavivarum camayaṅkaḷil civaṉvaḻipāṭē aṉittiyum viṭat toṉmi vāyntatu eṉa uyttuṇara ōraḷavu cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  (J.M.Roberts: History of the World  1992) itē karutti ār.eṉ. tantēkar ( Vedic. Mythological Tracts 1979) avarkaḷum terivittuḷḷār. 
 (ii) mēṟkaṇṭavaṟṟiṉ āṅkila mūlaṅkaḷ varumāṟu:-
Zimmer (1951):  The Pre-Aryan nobility of the north-eastern States, therefore were not all swept off their thrones [by the incoming Aryans] Many of the families survived, and when the dynasties of the invading race began to show symptoms of exhoustion the scions of these earlier native lines were able to assert themselves agin. Chandragupta Maurya, for example, stemmed from a family of this kind. So did the Buddha”(The Philosophies of India:1951)
Levin Bongard (1986): The worship of “images of dieities”- a  practice extremely wide-spread in Hinduism-came to Brahministic tradition from local non-Aryan cults.
Wolpert (1982) : Had pre-Aryan Yogis (such as the one depicted on the seal from Mohenjodaro) learned by now the language of their conquerors and began teaching their own secrets in turn to Brahmin bards?
Wolpert (1991): Non-violence is so remote from the rough and ready mores of Aryan nomads and their epic battles that we assume it has, like yoga and Mother Goddess worship, pre-Aryan roots.
xxxx Shiva is more popular in South India than in the North, which may reflect the deeper pre-Aryan roots of Dravidian people in that region where Mother-Goddess worship is also most intense.
J.M.Roberts(1992): One of the greatest cult figures of the Hindu pantheon today is Siva, in whose worship many early fertility cults have been brought together. A seal from Mohenjodaro already shows a figure who looks like an early Siva, and stones like the lingam found in modern temples, the phaliic cult-object which is his emblem have been found in the Harappan culture. There is some presumptive evidence therefore for the view that worship of Siva may be the oldest surviving religious cult in the world.
6. cāttaṉkuḷam a.irākavaṉ kiyeḻuttup paṭiyāka viṭṭuc ceṉṟa innūli aritiṉ peṟṟut tiṟampaṭa acciṭṭu veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷa  amiḻtam patippakam pārāṭṭukkuriyatu.

iṟivaṉiṉ eṇvaki 
vaṭivaṅkaḷ


1. tōṟṟuvāy
pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻ nāṭṭil pala kalikaḷ tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa.  ulakilē tamiḻakam kalikaḷiṉ ūṟṟāka ēṟṟamuṟṟu uyarntu niṉṟatu.  āya kalikaḷ aṟupattu nāṉkiṉiyum, ēya uṇarvikkum eṉṉammi eṉṟu arum perum kalikaḷ aṟupattu nāṉkiyum peṟṟa tamiḻt tāykku oru tamiḻp pulavaṉ vāḻttuk kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ.  āyirakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻnāṭṭil eṇṇaṟṟa eḻiṟkalikaḷ eḻuvataṟkuk kāraṇam pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē kali kaḷi uruvākkuvataṟkuriya irumpuk karuvikaḷ arumpiviṭṭatē yākum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.  putiya kaṟkālattiṉ iṟutikkālattilē tamiḻakattil irumpu ūḻi (iron Age) arumpi viṭṭatu.  putuk kallūḻi (Neolithic age) - il makkaḷ irumpuk karuvikaḷi uruvākki, atip perum aḷavil payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.  inta ūḻimāṟṟattiṉ kālattit tiṭṭa vaṭṭamākak kuṟippiṭa muṭiyavilli, eṉṟālum teṉ intiyāvil irumpu ūḻi toṭaṅki, ēṟattāḻa ēḻāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ irukkalām eṉṟu akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭeṭutta toṉmiyāṉa poruḷkaḷik koṇṭu oru cila aṟiñarkaḷ muṭivukaṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar.1
irumpu ūḻiyik kaṇṭa tamiḻ makkaḷ mikat toṉmi yāṉa kālattilē eḻuttāṇiyi uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar.  tam paṭṭaṟivāl payiṉṟa palakalikaḷiyum paṉiyōlikaḷiṉāl ceyta ēṭukaḷil pativu ceytuḷḷaṉar.  avarkaḷ ātiyil kaṇṭa varivaṭivam eḻuttu eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟatu.  eḻuttu eṉṟa patattiṟku ōviyam eṉṟu nikaṇṭukaḷil viḷakkantarap peṟṟuḷḷatu.  eṉavē paṇṭiya tamiḻarkaḷ tam ilakkiya nūṟkaḷiyum kali nūṟ kaḷiyum ōviya eḻuttu (Pictograph) mūlam ēṭukaḷil patintu payiṉṟu vantaṉar eṉṟu eṇṇappaṭukiṟatu.  iṉṉa palappala eḻuttu nili maṇṭapam eṉṟu paripāṭal kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.  eḻuttu nili maṇṭapam eṉpataṟkuc cittiramāṭam eṉṟē uriyāciriyarkaḷ poruḷ viḷakkam tantuḷḷaṉar.  iḵtaṉṟi, cilappatikārattil kaṇṇeḻuttu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭuvatiyum, yāpparukkala viruttiyil  mēṟkōḷ uruveḻuttu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭuvati yum, piṟa, tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil oli eḻuttu, uruveḻuttu, ōviya eḻuttu eṉṟu kāṇappaṭuvatiyum āyntāl paṇṭiya tamiḻakattil ōviya eḻuttē mutalil tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu naṉku teriyalām.  tamiḻar kuṭiyēṟiya cintuveḷi, cumēriyā, ekiptu, kirīṭ, īṭar tīvu pōṉṟa pala iṭaṅkaḷil ōviya eḻuttup payilap paṭṭu vantatu eṉpatu namatu karutti araṇ ceyvatākum.  
eṉavē, ēḻāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷ ōviyakkali, eḻuttukkali, paṭimakkali, cilikkali, kaṭṭiṭak kali, maruttuvakkali, āṭaṟkali, pāṭaṟkali, kappaṟkali, neytaṟkali, vāṉiyal kali, eṇṇiyal kali, camiyaṟ kali iṉṉōraṉṉa eṇṇilāk kalikaḷip payiṉṟu avikaḷit tiṟam peṟap paṭikkum aṟivum āṟṟalum peṟṟiruntaṉar.  ataṟku iṉṟum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟaṉa.  paṇṭik kālattil palvēṟu kalikaḷip paṭitta kalivallunarkaḷiṉ marapu vaḻiyil vanta cantatikaḷ iṉṟum tamiḻakattil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  paṇṭiya kaliñarkaḷiṉ tirukkōyilkaḷum, veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷum cilivaṭivaṅkaḷum, icit tūṇkaḷum, cuvar ōviyaṅkaḷum poṉ aṇikalaṅkaḷum, poṟ kalayaṅkaḷum, veḷḷik kuṭaṅkaḷum, vaki vakiyāṉa viḷakkukaḷum, eṇṇaṟṟa icik karuvikaḷum, āṭikaḷum, nakar amippukaḷum vaṇṇan tīṭṭiya maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷum, irumpuk karuvikaḷum, nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ niṟinta tērkaḷum, nilikaḷum cāḷaraṅkaḷum poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, īyam mutaliyavaṟṟāl ceyta kācukaḷum mūṉṟu, nāṉku nilikaḷiyuṭiya vīṭukaḷum pattukku mēṟpaṭṭa māṭaṅkaḷiyuṭiya vimāṉaṅkaḷum, kattikaḷum, eḻuttāṇi kaḷum, taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷkaḷum, mayiriṉiyum vakuttup piḷakkum aṟuvik karuvikaḷum, mutumakkaḷtāḻikaḷum, eḵku iḻippuḷikaḷum, taccu uḷikaḷum, vāḷkaḷum piṟavum iṉṟum uḷa.  āṉāl kaṭṭiṭak kali, paṭimak kali, ōviyakkali, āṭaṟ kali, icikkali, maruttuvakkali, kappaṟkali, vāṉiyaṟkali, uṇavuk kali, neytaṟ kali, cāyamūṭṭum kali, ākamaṅkaḷ, camayam, tattuvam pōṉṟavikaḷi viḷakkik kūṟum tamiḻ nūṟkaḷ mikavum kuṟivu.  tamiḻil ōviyam, kaṭṭiṭam, cilivaṭittal, tāṇṭavam āṭutal, ilāciyam āṭutal pōṉṟavikaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟum mūla nūṟkaḷ oṉṟu kūṭat tamiḻil illi.  cila ariya kalikaḷukku mūla nūṟkaḷ vaṭa moḻiyiltāṉ uḷḷaṉa.  vaṭa moḻiyil uḷḷa ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ nāṭṭiya nūṟkaḷ civākamaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavi kūṭat tamiḻil moḻiyākkam ceyyap peṟavilli.  tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ākamaṅkaḷiyum kalikaḷiyum tamiḻarallāta piṟa moḻiyāḷarkaḷum aṟiya vēṇṭum eṉpataṟkākap pala kali nūṟkaḷi paṇṭiya tamiḻar vaṭa moḻiyil yāttaṉar.  āṉāl, vaṭa moḻiyāḷarkaḷ, itaṉit tamakkuc cātakamāka eṇṇit tamiḻar neṟiyum, tamiḻar paṇpāṭum, tamiḻar kalikaḷum vaṭa moḻiyāḷarkaḷiṭamiruntu iravalākap peṟṟavi eṉṟu iṉṟu kūṟa muṉvantuḷḷaṉar.  intak kūṟṟukku eḷḷaḷavum cāṉṟu kāṭṭa muṭiyātu.  vaṭavarkaḷiṉ vātam nilittu niṟka muṭiyātu.  paṭimakkali, ōviyakkali, kōlakkali, pūttoṭukkuṅkali, āṭaṟkali, pāṭaṟkali pōṉṟavaṟṟit tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tirukkōyilil vittu vaḷarttu vantaṉar.  antak kalikaḷit tamiḻ makaḷir meyvanta teyvat tiruppaṇiyākac ceytu vantaṉar.  atu piṟkālattil potu makaḷir kikkup pōyviṭṭatu.  cumār 45 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ teṉ intiya nala urimik kaṭciyiṉ āṭciyiṉ pōtu tirukkōyilil makaḷir āṭuvatum pāṭuvatum caṭṭattiṉ mūlam taṭukkappaṭṭatu.  appāl tamiḻnāṭṭup pārppaṉarkaḷ tamiḻar kaḷiṭamiruntu āṭaṟ kaliyik kaṟṟu iṉṟu pērum, perumiyum, poṉṉum, poruḷum tēṭivarukiṉṟaṉar.  vāyk koḻuppu cēliyil vaṭintatu eṉṟa mutumoḻik koppat tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṭalum, pāṭalum payiṉṟa pārppaṉa makaḷir cilar ikkali vaṭanāṭṭil vaṭavarkaḷāl uruvākki vaḷartta kali eṉṟu kūṟa muṟpaṭṭatu avarkaḷiṉ aṟiyāmiyik kāṭṭuva tākum.  mēlum itu naṉṟi maṟanta ceyalumākum.  āṭavallāṉit tamiḻarkaḷ teyvamākak koṇṭu civaṉiṉ tāṇṭavamum umiyiṉ ilāciyamum tamiḻarkaḷiṉ taṉi urimi peṟṟa kaliyākak karutivarukiṉṟaṉar.  eṉavē āṭaṟkaliyi evarum taṅkaḷukkē uriya kali eṉṟu contappaṭuttiviṭa muṭiyātu.  atu eḷitāṉa kāriyam aṉṟu.  tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷiṉ ariya āṭaṟ kaliyi aṉṉiyarkaḷukkuk kaṟpikka muṉvanta payaṉ akkaliyit tamiḻar iḻakka nēriṭṭuviṭṭatu.  itit tamiḻarkaḷ iṉṟu naṉkaṟintu viḻippuṟṟu eḻat toṭaṅki viṭṭaṉar.  tamiḻk kalikaḷip pātukākkavum toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar.  iṉit tamiḻk kaliyi evarum eḷitil kavarntu viṭa muṭiyātu.  
tamiḻ nāṭṭup pārppaṉap peṇkaḷ tamiḻ nāṭṭu aṇṇāvikaḷiṭamiruntu āṭaṟkaliyip payiṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ eṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmi.  āṉāl vaṭa nāṭṭu āriyarkaḷ tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṭalō, pāṭalō iṉṟuvari aṇuvaḷavum aṟiyārkaḷ.  atōṭu, tamiḻ nāṭṭup pārppaṉarkaḷi vaṭa nāṭṭu āriyarkaḷ tam iṉattic cērntavarkaḷāka matippatum, ēṟṟuk koḷvatum illi.  ēṉeṉil ivarkaḷ tamiḻ moḻiyit tāy moḻiyākak koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷ. tamiḻarkaḷip pōla āṭi aṇikaḷ aṇintuḷḷārkaḷ.  tamiḻ nāṭṭuc civarkaḷip pōl yākam ceyvatum, cōmapāṉam ceyvatum illi.  kaṭal puṣpam eṉṟu mīṉ cāppiṭuvatum illi.  tamiḻarkaḷ nāṭoṟum nīriṟ mūḻkit tirunīṟu aṇintu civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṟārkaḷ.  ivar kaḷiṉ kurumārkaḷ caṅkarāccāriyar tamiḻnāṭṭut tirumaṭaṅkaḷil vāḻum civa canniyatāṉaṅkaḷ pōl nīṟaṇintu ceṅkāvi āṭi aṇintu civa civa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟārkaḷ.  tamiḻar nākarikatti tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭi ivarkaḷ vaḷarttu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  uṭal amippil mukattōṟṟattil tamiḻarkaḷiṉiṉṟu ivarkaḷi vēṟupaṭuttik kāṇa muṭiyātu eṉṟu vaṭanāṭṭu āriyarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.  āṉāl, tiru. vi. ār irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ kūṟukiṟapaṭi ivarkaḷiṭam paṇpāṭṭu vēṟṟumikaḷ, cila uṇṭu eṉpati maṟuppataṟkilli, nām maṟappataṟkum illi.
niṟka, mēlum ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉ vaṭa intiyarukkut tamiḻarkaḷiṉ ciṟpam, nāṭṭiyam, ici pōṉṟavi kaḷip paṟṟi oṉṟum teriyātu.  paratamuṉi1 eṉpavaṉtāṉ ki.mu. 3ām nūṟṟāṇṭil piṟantu, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ āṭaṟ kaliyip payiṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyil nāṭṭiya nūlip paṭittuḷḷāṉ.   (parataṉuṭiya kālam mikap piṟpaṭṭateṉṟu kūṟuvārumuṇṭu) parataṉ, tamiḻnāṭṭi tirāmiṭam eṉṟāṉ. nila nūl pulavaṉ tālamiyum, tirāviṭa nāṭṭit tam nūlil kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷāṉ.  vaṭa nāṭṭu āriyaṉ, cāraṅkatēvaṉ tamiḻ iciyi vaṭamoḻiyil cīriya nūlākkam - moḻiyākkam ceytuḷḷāṉ.  āriyarkaḷ eṉṟu eṇṇi niṟkum tamiḻnāṭṭup pirāmaṇarkaḷ, tamiḻ ici kūṭa kāṣmīril piṟantatu eṉṟu kūṟum nāḷ aṇmiyil varalām eṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟārkaḷ.
nāṉ, muppatu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, pañcāp vaṭamoḻit tokuti eṉṉum peyaril ciṟpa cāttirak koḷki (Principles of Silpa Sastra) eṉṉum nūl oṉṟippārttēṉ; payiṉṟēṉ.  atil tamiḻar kaṭṭiṭak kali, ōviyak kali, paṭimakkali ākiyavikaḷ vātu cātirā, citralakcaṇam, piratimālaṭcaṇam eṉṉum peyarkaḷil, vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷāṉa 1. pirahat camhiti, 
2. viṣṇutarmōttiram, 3. cukkira nīti, 4. maccaya purāṇam, 5. akkiṉi purāṇam, 6. māyā cātiram, 7. piratimā māṉa laṭcaṇam eṉṟa nūṟkaḷiṉ mēṟkōḷkaḷuṭaṉ naṉku viḷakki eṭuttuk kāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  itip paṭittavarkaḷ ittakiya nūl oṉṟu kūṭat tamiḻil illi eṉpatiyum mūla nūṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil irup patiyum eṇṇi ikkalikaḷ āriyar kalikaḷē eṉṟu mayakkam uṟuvārkaḷ.
annūṟkaḷip paṭittatum eṉakku nāṉ tamiḻaṉ eṉṟa uṇarvu unti eḻuntatu.  nāṉ intiyaṉ eṉatu tāymoḻi tamiḻ, eṉatu camayam civaneṟi, eṉakkeṉṟu oru taṉik kaliyum, paṇpāṭum, nākarikamum, aṟa neṟiyum, oḻukkamum, uṇavum, uṭi aṇiyum muṟiyum uṇṭu eṉa eṇṇattoṭaṅkiviṭṭēṉ.  inniliyil cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu.  cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvil īṭupaṭṭavarkaḷum, atip paṟṟi ārāycciyil pukuntavarkaḷum, iṟutiyāka aṅkukkiṭitta ōviya eḻuttip paṭittavarkaḷum iṅkilāntu, peyṉ, piṉlāntu, hālantu, uruciyā mutaliya veḷināṭṭavarkaḷum vaṭintiyar kaḷumēyāvar.  inta akaḻ āyvilō, iṅkukkiṭitta poruḷ kaḷip paṟṟiya ārāycciyilō, iṅkuḷḷa muttirikaḷilum ceppuppaṭṭayaṅkaḷilum uḷḷa ōviya eḻuttukaḷukku maṟikuṟi eḻuttu poruḷ viḷakkam kāṇpatilō tamiḻarkaḷō allatu tirāviṭarkaḷ eṉpōrkaḷō īṭupaṭavē illi.  intiya miya araciṉ tolporuḷ āyvuttuṟi iyakkunarāṉa car cāṇ mārcal, avarukkup piṉ appataviyi vakitta carmārṭṭiyar uyilar, mokañcatārō nakari akaḻntu āynta pērāciriyar ār.ṭi. pāṉarcci, arappāvi akaḻntāynta em. e. vāṭcu, mēlum inta āyvil īṭupaṭṭa ṭākṭar mekkēy ār. kē. mukarcci, eṉ.ji.macumatār, kē. eṉ tīkcit, ṭākṭar. ji.ār. haṇṭar, eyc, ār. hāl lāṅṭaṉ, el. ē. vaṭal mārivālā, tanti eyc hirā, piṉlāntu ṭākṭar akō pārpōlā, leṉiṉ kirāṭ pērāciriyar kericōv mutaliya paṉṉāṭṭu aṟiñarkaḷum cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭiṉar.  intac ceyti veḷivantatum āriya ulakamē kiṭukiṭuttatu.  vaṭa intiyāvil uḷḷa intiya āriyarkaḷum teṉṉintiyāvil uḷḷa tamiḻaka āriyak kurukkaḷāṉa caṅkarāccāri mutal, nintapūṇūlit taritta cātāraṇa ēḻip pārppaṉar uḷpaṭa aṉivarum kotitteḻuntaṉar.  neñcaṅkumuṟi alaṟiṉar.  pattirikiyil maṟuppu eḻutiṉar.  intiya aracil uyar patavi vakikkum pārppaṉarkaḷ maṟuppu nūṟkaḷ eḻuti veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷaṉar.  irāma kiruṣṇā micaṉic cārnta pārppaṉattuṟavikaḷ kūṭa maṟuppuk kūṟiṉar.  caṅkarāccāriyār, āriyarkaḷ ayal nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu iṅku vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉatilli.  āriyarkaḷ aṉāti kālantoṭṭu intiyāvil tōṉṟi vāḻntu varupavarkaḷ eṉṟu (tūttukkuṭiyil) poyyuri kūṟi varalāṟṟup puraṭṭar eṉṉum peyarukku ilakkāṉār.  āṉāl intiyāviṉ mutal amiccarum intiya tēcīya kāṅkiraciṉ talivarum, varalāṟṟaṟiñarumāṉa paṇṭitar cavaharlāl nēru avarkaḷ taṉatu intiyāviṉ kaṇṭupiṭippu eṉṟa nūlil (Discovery of India) cintuveḷi nākarikam tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉpatiyum āriyarkaḷ ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantu intiyāvil kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ eṉpatiyum ēṟṟuk koṇ ṭuḷḷār.
cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu: āriyarkaḷ intiyāviṟkuḷ pukuntu tirāviṭa makkaḷik koṉṟu intiyāvi ākkiramittu, tirāviṭa nakaraṅkaḷi aḻittu, avarkaḷiṉ paḻamperum paṇpāṭṭi aḻippataṟkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē, teṉintiyat tirāviṭa makkaḷ uyariya kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷi kaṭṭi eḻuppi nāṭu nakaraṅkaḷi niṟuvi ulakilē oppaṟṟa uyariya paṇpāṭṭi uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu aṟiñar ulakam muṭivu kaṭṭiyuḷḷatu.
itik kaṇṭu, tamiḻarkaḷō, tirāviṭa iṉamō uḷam makiḻntu, mukam malarntu kutitteḻuntu viḻāveṭukkavō illi  makkaḷ makiḻccip perukkāl āravāram ceyyavō illi.  celvarkaḷ cintuveḷi ceṉṟu tamatu muṉṉōrkaḷ vāḻntu muṭinta arappā, mokañcatārō lokuñcatārō, caṅkutārō, lōtal, nāl, kulli, kāḷi paṅkaṉ pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷik kaṇṭu kaḷikkac cellavumilli.  āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cintu veḷiyil vāḻnta civavēḷāḷar vaḻipaṭṭu vanta civaliṅkatti, civaṉ ciliyi, pārvati tēviyiṉ paṭimatti, āṭavallāṉ aruḷ uruvattit tericikka -  tiruvāṭutuṟi, tiruppaṉantāḷ, tarumapuram, kuṉṟakkuṭi, maturi peruṅkuḷam (ceṅkōl maṭam) pōṉṟa civa maṭātipatikaḷ kaṇṭukaḷikka - vaḻipāṭiyaṟṟa muṉintucellavilli.  tamiḻ eḻuttāḷarkaḷ, pērāciriyarkaḷ cintuveḷip paṇpāṭṭip paṟṟik kaṭṭuri tīṭṭavō nūl eḻutavō muṉvaravilli.  eṉṟālum cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu 1922-lē eṉ pōṉṟa oru cila ciṉṉañciṟu, anāmatēya eḻuttāḷarkaḷi īrttuḷḷatu.  ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvi maṭṭumalla arikkaṉmēṭu, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu, koṟki, maturi akaḻ āyvukaḷip paṟṟiyum cintikkac ceytuviṭṭatu eṉpati iṅku kuṟippiṭāmal irukka muṭiyavilli.  paṇṭik kālattil tamiḻarkaḷ āḻkaṭal kaṭantu, upitiyā, ellam, cumēriyā, ekiptu, kirīṭtīvu, īṭartīvu, amerikkā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟi payirt toḻili vaḷarttu, kit toḻili uruvākki kaṭal vaṇikattip perukki civaneṟiyi nilināṭṭip paṇpāṭṭip parappiya ceytiyi uṉṉippāka uṟṟunōkki uṇarac ceytatu.  mēṉāṭṭu āciriyarkaḷ palarāl amerikkāvil araṉāriṉ kōyil, ekiptil ēṟūrntacelvaṉ tiruuruvam, ellam nāṭṭil eḻiṟkuṉṟil oḷik kaṭavuḷ, kirīṭṭil nantīcaṉ eṉṉum teyvavaṭivam, ciṉṉa āciyāvil civaṉ oḷi, pacipik tīvukaḷiṉ civa neṟi eṉṉum peyarāl -  veḷi vanta kaṭṭurikaḷ itayatti īrttaṉa.  namnāṭṭu kōṭarik kāmpukaḷ pōṉṟa cilar namatu kalikaḷik kavarntu āriyak kaliyākka iṭiyaṟātu muyaṟcittup paṇiyāṟṟi varuvatu eṉ uḷḷattiṟku uṇar vūṭṭiyatu.  nāṭṭup paṟṟi maṟantu, camayappaṟṟit tuṟantu, teyva paktiyi ikaḻntu, moḻip paṟṟip puṟakkaṇittu niṉṟa, eṉpōṉṟa cilar uḷḷattil namatu nāṭu, namatu moḻi, namatu camayam, namatu paṇpāṭu, namatu nākarikam namatu kali eṉṟa uṇarvi eḻuppiyatu.
itaṉ viḷivāka eṉakku, tamiḻ makkaḷi avarkaḷiṉ paṇpāṭu nākarikam, moḻi mutaliyavaṟṟi uṇarac ceyvatōṭu atik kākkavum tūṇṭavum vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇamum eḻuntatu.  anta uṇarvē iṟivaṉiṉ eṇvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷ - ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum vaḷarcciyum eṉṟa innūli eḻutat tūṇṭiyatu.  innūlukku aṭippaṭiyāṉa karuttukkaḷ aṉittum vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa civākamaṅkaḷilum, ciṟpa nūṟ kaḷilumiruntu peṟappeṟṟaṉa.  teyvattiruuruvaṅkaḷ aṉittum namatu kivaṇṇam vanta tapatikaḷāl vitippaṭi aimpoṉṉālum, kallālum, vārkkap peṟṟum cetukkappeṟṟum tirukkōyilil iṭam peṟṟirunta uyariya oppaṟṟa kalik karuvūlaṅkaḷiṉ niḻal uruvappaṭaṅkaḷēyākum.  oṉṟiraṇṭu varipaṭaṅkaḷākum.
civākamam kaṇṭa cāṉṟōrkaḷ civaṉ uruvi ātiyil - ciṟpa aṟivu arumpiya toṭakka kālattil ammi appaṉ uruvi - civaliṅka vaṭivil ceytu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.  appāl ulōka ūḻiyil ciṟpak kali vaḷarcci peṟṟa niliyil iṟivaṉiṉ tirukkōlatti mūrttaṅkaḷāka - civa perumāṉiṉ muḻu uruvaṅkaḷākac ceytu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.  ātiyil tamiḻarkaḷ karaṭumuraṭāṉa kallil civaliṅka uruvic ceytu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.  appāl iṟivaṉ tamiḻnāṭṭil teṉpāṇṭi nāṭṭil uḷḷa mantiramaliyil (makēntirakiriyil) kallāla marattiṉ kīḻ tamiḻ muṉivarkaḷākiya caṉakar, caṉātaṉar, caṉaṟkumārar, caṉāntarar ākiyavarkaḷukkuk kurumūrttamākak kāṭci aḷitta kōlattit taṭcaṇāmūrtti vaṭivam (teṉmukak kaṭavuḷ uruvam) eṉṟu mutaṉmutalākac ciliyilum ulōkappaṭimattilum ceytu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar.  appāl aṭṭamūrttaṅkaḷ eṭṭum, makātēva mūrttaṅkaḷ irupattintum civamūrttaṅkaḷ aṟupattu nāṉkum eḻuntaṉa.  avikaḷiṉ viḷakkam aṭiyil varuvaṉavākum:

iḵtaṉṟi iṟivaṉuṭiya kōlaṅkaḷ eṇṇila uḷa.   tāṇṭavamūrttiyiṉ kōlaṅkaḷ 7eṉṟum 16 eṉṟum 108 eṉṟum 1008-eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  iṅku eṭuttukkāṭṭappeṟṟa civamūrttaṅkaḷ 64-il oṉṟu mutal 8 variyuḷḷa mūrttaṅkaḷ aṭṭamūrttaṅkaḷ eṉṟum oṉṟu mutal 25 eṇvariyuḷḷa kōlaṅkaḷ makātēva mūrttaṅkaḷ eṉṟum civa camayaccāṉṟōr kaḷāl vakuttut tokuttu namakku aḷikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ituvari namakku iṟivaṉiṉ kōlaṅkaḷip paṟṟi evarum nūl yāttaḷikkavilli.  civamūrttaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyō, makātēva mūrttaṅkaḷip paṟṟiyō ārāyavāyppilli.  aṭṭamūrttaṅ kaḷip paṟṟit tāṉ namakku aṟiya ārāya vāyppuk kiṭṭiyatu.  atuvum inta aṭṭamūrttiyiṉ tiruuruvaṅkaḷiyum ataṉ uṟuppukaḷiyum, uṭikaḷiyum, kikaḷil tāṅkiya karuvi kaḷiyum aṇikaḷiyum uṭiyavariṉ kāliṉ kīḻkkiṭantu nintu uḻalum muyalakaṉiyum (apamāra purucaṉiyum) civacamaya tattuva ñāṉak kaṇṇōṭu alaci ārāyntu kaṇṭa uṇmi kaḷiyum uṇarttavilli.  ataṟkuriya niṟivāṉa āṉmañāṉat teḷivu eṉakku illi.  civañāṉa pōta meyyaṟivum eṉakkuk kiṭṭavilli.  eṉavē eṉakkuḷḷa mikak kuṟinta camaya uṇarvi yum ciṟiya ciṟpa aṟiviyum vittum - aṭṭamūrttiyiṉ vaṭivaṅ kaḷi viḷakkac civaliṅkaṅkaḷiyum ataṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyi yum viḷakkiyum - mēṟpōntavāṟāka nāṉpeṟṟa ākama aṟivik koṇṭum innūli yāttaḷikka muṉ vantuḷḷēṉ.
nāṉ eṉ pattu vayatil - paḷḷip paruvattil cāttaṉkuḷam ci.em.e uyartarappāṭacālikku āṅkilak kalvi payilvataṟku eṉ peṟṟōrkaḷāl aṉuppap peṟṟēṉ.  aṅku karttāvē eṅkaḷ nalvēntaṉik kāppāṟṟumē eṉṟa - āṅkila kiṟittava āṭciyik kāppāṟṟumē eṉṟa - irāca vāḻttum, appāl, paramaṇṭalaṅkaḷil irukkiṟa eṅkaḷ pitāvē eṅkaḷik kāppāṟṟumē eṉṟa kiṟittu nātari vaḻipaṭum jepamum kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa.  aṅku āciriyar eṉṉi aññāṉap piḷḷi eṉṟē kūṟuvār.  eṉṉi avvāṟu kūṟumāṟum kaṟpittār.  appāl pipiḷ pāṭam ārampippār.  civaperumāṉ nintikkap paṭuvār.  umātēvi iḻitturikkappaṭuvār.  aṅku neṟṟiyil tirunīṟu pūciyirukkum māṇavarkaḷ cāmpalil puraṇṭu vantirukkum kaḻuti pōlvar eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  kiṟittava maṟiyiṉ pala pakutikaḷ, pārāmal oppuvikkumāṟu kaṭṭāyappaṭuttappaṭṭatu.  nāḷaṭivil nāṉum eṉṉōṭu payiṉṟa kiṟittavarallāta civap piḷḷikaḷum kiṟittuvi kāli māli vaḻipaṭat toṭaṅ kiṉōm, kōyilukkum pōṉōm.  kiṟittava camaya pāṭap parīṭciyil kiṟittavap piḷḷikaḷiviṭa naṉku payiṉṟu atikamāṉa tērvu eṇkaḷ peṟṟup paricum peṟṟu vantōm.   kālam cintikkat tūṇṭiyatu.  nāṉ payiṉṟirunta ciṟpa aṟivu innūli eḻuta pakkapalamāka, eṉatu paḷḷit tōḻar muṉṉāḷ kalekṭar amarar to.mu. pākarat toṇṭamāṉ pala aṭṭamūrttiyiṉ niḻalurup paṭaṅkaḷit tantu eṉatu naṉmuyaṟcikku ākkamum ūkkamum aḷittār.
nāṉ eṉatu iḷavayatil kiṟittavap paḷḷiyil payiṉṟu vantēṉ.  aṅku nāṉ kiṟittava camayatti aṟintēṉ.  intu camaya uṇarvi iḻakkumāṟu ceyyap peṟṟēṉ.  nāṉ peyaraḷavil intu eṉṟu aḻikkap peṟṟālum, uḷḷam kiṟittava camaya uṇarvip peṟṟuviṭṭatu.  1922ām āṇṭu tirunelvēli āciriyar payiṟcik kallūriyil payiṉṟu piṉṉar  kamāliyā mulim pāṭacāliyil āciriyarākap paṇiyāṟṟiṉēṉ.  appoḻutu ilāmiya camayattip paṟṟiyaṟiyum vāyppip peṟṟēṉ.  nāṉ 1923ām āṇṭu tirunelvēliyil āciriyar payiṟcip pāṭacāliyil payilumpoḻutu civacittānta nūṟ patippuk kaḻakattōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭēṉ.  appoḻutu tiruvāḷarkaḷ kā.cuppiramaṇiyapiḷḷi, tiru.vi.kaliyāṇacuntara mutaliyār, ī.ve.irāmacāmi nāyakkar mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ naṭpip peṟṟēṉ.  ikkālattil eṉakkut tamiḻ payilum ārvamum, ōviyam eḻutum uṇarcciyum eḻuntaṉa.  civatīṭciyum peṟṟuk koṇṭēṉ.
eṉṟālum 1926ām āṇṭu periyār ī.ve. irāmacāmi avarkaḷiṉ pēccum eḻuttum eṉṉip peritum kavarntaṉa.  avaratu cuyamariyāti iyakkattil paṅku koṇṭēṉ.  1932ām āṇṭu periyāriṉ utaviyōṭu pakuttaṟivu nūṟ patippuk kaḻakam limiṭeṭ eṉṟa niṟuvaṉatti niṟuvi cumār 20-kku mēṟpaṭṭa nūṟkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭēṉ.  nāṉ cila nūṟkaḷi eḻutiṉēṉ.  tirāviṭaṉ, kuṭiaracu, tārulilām pōṉṟa pattirikikaḷil kaṭṭurikaḷ pala eḻutiṉēṉ.  periyāriṉ pēccum eḻuttum piṭikkātu avaratu toṭarpi aṟuttuk koṇṭu kāṅkiracilum, cōcaliṭkaṭciyilum paṅku peṟṟēṉ.  mūṉṟu tiṅkaḷ ciṟivācamum ceytēṉ.  ī.ve.irāmacāmi avarkaḷ araciṉ etirppiyum kiṟittavarkaḷ mulimkaḷ etirppiyum tāṅka muṭiyātu cuyamariyāti iyakkatti vāpa peṟṟu, araciṭam maṉṉippup peṟṟu tirāviṭa iyakkam niṟuviṉār.  periyāriṉ ceyali veṟuttu aṇṇāturi ti.mu.kaḻakam amittut tamiḻaka makkaḷiṉ uḷḷattik kavarntār.  periyār tirāviṭar kaḻakam amittu poruḷ tiraṭṭum pōkki maṟuttār.  tirāviṭa iṉattiṉ varalāṟu, tirāviṭa moḻi, tirāviṭar neṟi, tirāviṭar teyvam, tirāviṭar paṇpāṭu, tirāviṭar kalikaḷ, tirāviṭar nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟi aṟiyāmal iruppatōṭu tamiḻarkaḷ kāṭṭumirāṇṭi eṉap pitaṟṟi varuvatiyum, tirāviṭa iṉattil cātit tirāviṭa iṉam, ātittirāviṭa iṉam eṉap pirittatiyum, tirāviṭa iyakkattiṉ peyarāl nāttikap piraccāramum, camaya etirppup piraccāramum ceytu  -  tirāviṭa makkaḷi oṉṟu cērttu āṅkila āṭciyiṭamiruntu mulimkaḷ, pākkitāṉ peṟṟatip pōl tirāviṭa makkaḷ tirāviṭatāṉ peṟa muṭiyātu - periyār tōlviyuṟṟatiyum kaṇṭu tirāviṭa muṉṉēṟṟak kaḻakam amittār.  oṉṟē kulamum oruvaṉē tēvaṉum eṉṉum tirumūlar tiruvākkik kūṟittamiḻar iṉatti oṉṟu cērttut tamiḻaka aracit tērtalil kip paṟṟit tamiḻ moḻiyi vaḷarkkavum, tamiḻkkalikaḷip pēṇavum muṉ vantār - avar eṉṉic cantittu tamiḻakak kalikaḷi vaḷarkka utavumāṟu aḻittār.  nāṉum oruppaṭṭēṉ.  atōṭu avar tamatu kaṭcikkup periyāriṉ ātaraviyum tēṭik koṇṭār.  tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ turatirṣṭam 1969ām āṇṭu aṇṇā uyir tuṟantār.  tiru. mu. karuṇāniti mutal amiccarāṉār.  ivar oru nalla tiṟami mikka araca tantiri eṉṟu peyar peṟṟuk koṇṭār eṉṟālum akkaṭciyiṉariṉ naṉmatippip peṟavilli.  tamiḻ vaḷarkka, tamiḻk kali vaḷarkka aṇṇāturi aḷavu ārvamō aṟivō peṟavilli.  ivar mītu makkaḷ kūṟiya ūḻal kuṟṟaṅkaḷi maṟukkum valuvum, etirkkum āṟṟalum peṟavilli.  periyār ī.ve.rā, karuṇānitiyiṉ ceyali ātarittār.  ataṉāl karuṇānitiyi etirttavarkaḷic camātāṉappaṭutti ti.mu. kaṭciyik kākkamuṭiyātu pōyviṭṭatu.  a.i.a.ti.mu.kaḻakam tōṉṟiyatu. em.ji. irāmaccantiraṉ eṉṉum oru maliyāḷi akkaṭciyiṉ talivarāki mutal amiccar pataviyiyum peṟṟuḷḷār.  ivaratu aracu tamiḻ moḻiyik kākkum eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭālum ivarāl tamiḻ moḻikākkappaṭum, tamiḻar camayam, tamiḻar paṇpāṭu, tamiḻarkali kākkappaṭum eṉṟa nampikki eṉakku illi.  tamiḻarkaḷiṭiyē orumippāṭu nilavum eṉṟa nampikki kūṭilli.  ataṟkuriya āṟṟalō, aṟivō, eṇṇamō ivariṭam illi.
eṉavē tamiḻ iṉam taṉṉiṭam uḷḷa vēṟṟumikaḷi maṟantu - oṉṟu tiraṇṭu tamiḻ moḻi, tamiḻc camayam, tamiḻp paṇpāṭu, tamiḻ nākarikam paravavum, vaḷaravum - iṉapētam, kaṭci pētam, camaya pētam mutaliyavikaḷi maṟantu oṉṟupaṭavēṇṭum,  oṟṟumiyuṟavēṇṭum eṉṟa uṇarvōṭu iṟivaṉiṉ eṇvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷ eṉṟa nūli eḻuta muṉvantēṉ.  ivvaḻiyil pala nūṟkaḷum eḻutiyuḷḷēṉ.  araciyal kaṭcikaḷi etirpārātu tamiḻ iṉam oṉṟupaṭṭut taṉṉik kākkum; uyarttum; vāḻum eṉṟu etirpārkkiṉṟēṉ.
niṟka, tamiḻ nāṭu campantappaṭṭa maṭṭil inta nāṭṭil enta valuppeṟṟa camayamum namatu tēcīya neṟiyākiya civatti - civaneṟiyi aḻittu viṭa muṭiyātu.  varalāṟu ati meyp pikkiṟatu.  tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ aracarkaḷik kivacappaṭutti civaṉ eṉṟu oruvaṉ taṉṉik kūṟi nimirntu naṭakka muṭiyātu innāṭṭik kavarntu niṉṟa aṉittulaka neṟi eṉṟu pukaḻpeṟṟa puttamum, camaṇamum irunta iṭam teriyātu - atu tamiḻakattil nilavi iruntatu eṉṟu kūṟum oru aṭiccuvaṭukūṭa kāṇamuṭiyāmaṟ pōyviṭṭatu.  tamiḻar araci aḻittu avarkaḷ camayatti urut teriyātu māṟṟi avarkaḷatu teyvat tiru uruvaṅkaḷi - aimpatu aṭi āḻattiṟkuk kīḻē putikkappaṭṭa civaṉ uruvaṅkaḷum, civi uruvaṅkaḷum, civaliṅkac cilikaḷum mīṇṭum veḷiyē vantu vaṇaṅkappaṭavum āriya makkaḷ vaḻipaṭavum iṉṟu viṇṇil ulavum kōḷ oṉṟukku viṇveḷi āyvāḷar eṉṟu peyarpeṟavum oru nili eḻuntuḷḷatu.
civa neṟiyi utaṟittaḷḷiya eṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷ  - kiṟittava camaya uṇarcciyum ilāmiya camayappaṟṟum, putta mata paktiyum koṇṭavarkaḷ -  mīṇṭum nāṭṭuppaṟṟum civa camaya uṇarvum, civapaktiyum koṇṭu, tamiḻarneṟi, moḻi, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam mutaliyavikaḷi vaḷarkka - pēṇikkākka nāṉ peṟṟa piṟa camaya ārāycciyum, nāttika aṟivum payaṉpaṭum nilikku māṟṟiyuḷḷatu.  itu namatu moḻiyiṉ, camayattiṉ, pārampariyattiṉ valuviyum, oḷiyiyum kāṭṭuvatākum eṉṟu uṅkaḷukku uṇarttak kaṭamip paṭṭuḷḷēṉ.

camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷ
intukkaḷ eṉpavar yār?
iṉṟu civar, viṇavar, cumārttar ākiya mūṉṟu camayi kaḷiyum intukkaḷ eṉṟu aḻikkiṉṟaṉar.  itu tavaṟu. civam civaperumāṉi muḻu mutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭa neṟi. uyarnta naṉṉeṟi.  itu iṉṟiyac camayaṅkaḷ aṉittilum paḻamiyāṉa camayam. inta camayattil cērvataṟku mutalil oruvar civatīṭci peṟavēṇṭum, tirunīṟu iṭavēṇṭum.  civaperumāṉi muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷum iccamayattiṉ maṟi nūṟkaḷāka tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tirumantiram, tirukkuṟaḷ mutaliyavikaḷum viti nūṟkaḷāka ākamaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷi naṉku payiṉṟu ataṉvaḻi naṭakka vēṇṭum.
itēpōl viṇavattiṟku tirumāl muḻumutaṟ kaṭavu ḷākavum nālāyirap pirapantam mutaliyaṉa maṟinūlākavum koḷḷappaṭum.  inta camayattiṟ pukuvataṟku muttiri tāraṇam ceyyappaṭavēṇṭum.  tirumaṇ iṭavēṇṭum .  āṉāl civat tiṟkum viṇavattiṟkum neruṅkiya oṟṟumiyuṇṭu.
itē pōl cumārtta camayattiṟku piramaṉ, uruttiraṉ, viṣṇu ākiyavarkaḷ muḻumutaṟkaṭavuḷkaḷ.  irukku, yacur, cāmam, atarvaṇam ākiya nāṉkum vētaṅkaḷ.  immatattil cērvataṟku kāyattiri mantiram cepikkavēṇṭum.  pūṇūl tarikka vēṇṭum.  yākam puritalum cōmapāṉam ceytalum accamayattiṉ iṉṟi yamiyāta koḷkikaḷākum.
civattiṉ taṉipperum ciṟappu
civam taṉip perum ciṟappuṭiya puṉita neṟi.  inneṟi intiya maṇṇil tirāviṭap peruṅkuṭimakkaḷāl kāṇappaṭṭa taṉittaṉmi vāynta neṟi.  inta camayam āriyarkaḷiṉ camayamākiya cumārttam karuvil iṭam peṟuvataṟkumuṉpē tuḷirttu vaḷarnta tūya koḷkikaḷiyuṭiya camayam.  ulakil uḷḷa camayaṅkaḷuk kellām tāy neṟi eṉṟu kūṟiṉālum poruntum.  intac camayam viṇavam, puttam, camaṇam, ilām, kiṟittavam, yūtarkaḷiṉ ekōvāmatam mutaliya camayaṅkaḷuṭaṉ peṟṟirukkum orumip pāṭukūṭa cumārtta matattuṭaṉ peṟṟirukkavilli.  iṭik kālattil putta camayattiyum camaṇacamayattiyum etirkka cumārttam, viṇavam, civam ākiya camayaṅkaḷil oru aikkiya muṉṉaṇi niṟuvi atu  intumatam eṉṟu aḻikkap paṭṭatu.  anta aikkiya muṉṉaṇiyiṉ kōrikki niṟivēṟiyatum cumārttar, civaneṟiyiyum viṇavaneṟiyiyum aḻittu cumārtta matamē (intu matamē) ellārukkum tāymatam eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.  cumārtta maṟikaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa vaṭamoḻiyē teyvīka moḻi eṉṟu kūṟiṉar.  araciṉariṉ celvākkil piṟa camayattiyum avarkaḷiṉ teyvattiyum maṟikaḷiyum, iṉattiyum moḻiyiyum oḻikka muṟpaṭṭaṉar.
inta irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilē civaneṟi ulakilē uyarnta taṉineṟi.  itaṟkeṉat taṉit teyvamum, maṟiyum, moḻiyum, paṇpāṭum uṇṭu eṉṟu maṉōṉmaṇiyam āciriyar tiru. cuntarampiḷḷi, maṟimali aṭikaḷ, kā. cuppira maṇiyapiḷḷi pōṉṟavarkaḷ civaneṟikku maṟumalarcciyaḷikka muṉvantaṉar.
civarkaḷiṉ tirukkōyilkaḷiṉ amippu muṟikaḷum vaḻipāṭukaḷum ākamaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.  ākamaṅkaḷ vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷaṉa.  upākamaṅkaḷum vaṭa moḻiyilē ākkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ākamaṅkaḷip paṟṟi aṟital innūliṉ uyirōṭṭamāka amintuḷḷatu.  tamiḻil ākamaṅkaḷukku nikarāka tirumantiram, tēvāram, tiruvācakam, tirukkuṟaḷ civañāṉa pōtam, civañāṉa cittiyār pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷ uḷa.
ākamam
ākamam eṉpati ā+kamam eṉṟu pakuttup poruḷ koḷkiṉṟaṉar.  uyirkaḷ iṉpam peṟa malanācam ceyvippatu eṉṟu itaṟku poruḷ tarappaṭum.  tōṟṟak kālattil iṟivaṉ, uyirkaḷ īṭēṟṟam eytum poruṭṭu makkaḷukkāka inta ākamaṅkaḷi aruḷic ceytār eṉṟu nampappaṭukiṟatu.  civa camayikaḷ civākamaṅkaḷi vitivētam eṉṟum tēvāram, tiruvācakam ākiyavikaḷit tutivētam eṉṟum kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.  ākamaṅkaḷ kōyilkaḷiṉ amippu, teyva vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ vakikaḷ, vaḻipaṭum muṟi pōṉṟa aṉittiyum kūṟuvaṉavākum.  ākamaṅkaḷē camayikaḷukku muṭivāṉa caṭṭanūṟkaḷākum.  ākamaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu vakippaṭum.  atāvatu (1) pāñca rātrā ākamaṅkaḷ, 
(2) civākamaṅkaḷ, (3) cākta ākamaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum.
inta mūṉṟu vakippaṭṭa ākamaṅkaḷum potuvāṉa vicayaṅkaḷil oruppōlak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  vēṟupaṭṭa karuttukkaḷum māṟupaṭṭa koḷkikaḷum illi.  āṉāl peyaraḷavil cila vēṟṟumikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  āṉāl aṭippaṭi yāṉa tattuvaṅkaḷil vēṟṟumikaḷ illi.  ivikaḷiṉ vivaram aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
pāñca rātrā (viṇava) ākamaṅkaḷ
pāñcarātrā ākamaṅkaḷ 108 vakippaṭum.  āṉāl inta ākamaṅkaḷ aṉittum kiṭippatu aritu.  iṉṟu muyaṉṟu pattu allatu patiṉmūṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷi aṟiñarkaḷ tēṭik kaṇṭu piṭittuḷḷārkaḷ.  ivi ātiyil nāṉmukaṉ, aimukaṉ, aṟumukaṉ, kutamaṉ, vaciṭṭaṉ, nārataṉ, kapilaṉ ākiya ēḻu muṉivarkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  inta 108-viṇava ākamaṅkaḷukku nāṉkulaṭcam culōkaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu pala mutuperum aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
2. civākamaṅkaḷ (civākamaṅkaḷ)
civākamaṅkaḷ, civākamaṅkaḷ eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭum.  civa camayikaḷiṉ kōyil vakikaḷ ataṉ amippu muṟi, pūci (pūcey) muṟikaḷ, paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ amippu ataṉ tattuvaṅkaḷ aṉittiyum viḷakkamākak kūṟum intac camaya vitikaḷip paṟṟic civa camayikaḷ ituvari akkari kāṭṭavilli.  civākamaṅkaḷ aṉittum vaṭamoḻiyil uḷḷaṉa.  civaṉi vaḻipaṭum aṭiyārkaḷiṟ palarum vaṭamoḻiyiyaṟiyātavarkaḷ; tirukkōyilkaḷil pūci naṭattum āti civarkaḷil palar inta ākamaṅkaḷi vācippārkaḷ.  culōkaṅkaḷi maṉappāṭam ceytu oppuvippārkaḷ.  āṉāl poruḷ terintōr cilarēyāvar.  eṅkaḷ kuṭumpattic cērnta cilar kāñcipurattiṉiṉṟu tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil kuṭiyēṟiya civavēḷāḷar āvar.  ōtuvār eṉṟum ākama civar eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum ivarkaḷil kuṟippiṭattakkōr tirunelvēli muttamiḻc caṅkat talimip pulami naṭāttiya tiru cālivāṭīcuvara ōtuvār, avaratu makaṉ cuntara ōtuvār avarkaḷum āvar.  ivarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ palarukku vaṭamoḻip payiṟciyuṇṭu.  ākamaṅkaḷi naṉku kaṟṟavarkaḷ; ākamaṅkaḷukkup poruḷ kūṟa vallavarkaḷ.  ivarkaḷi ākama civarkaḷ eṉṟum aḻippar.  itaṟku cācaṉac cāṉṟukaḷ uṇṭu.  ivarkaḷukku - inta ākama civarkaḷukku maṉṉarkaḷ, āti civarkaḷ, kurukkaḷ, tēcikar mutaliyavarkaḷukku aḷitta mariyātiyiviṭa atikamāṉa mariyāti aḷittu māṉiyaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅki ciṟappuc ceytu vantuḷḷaṉar.
civam pōṟṟum civākamaṅkaḷum ataṉ peyarkaḷum aṭiyil varumāṟākum:
1. acitam
2. aṉalam
3. añcumāṉ
4. iruram
5. ilaḷitam
6. kāmikam
7. kāraṇam
8. kīraṇam
9. cattiram
10. cantirañāṉam
11. cantāṉacaruvōttam
12. cittam
13. cintiyam
14. cuppirapētam
15. cuvāyampuvam
16. cūkkumam
17. tīpattam
18. pāramēccuram
19. purōṟkītam
20. pētam
21. makuṭam
22. yōkacam
23. vātuḷam
24. vimalam
25. vicayam
26. vicuvācam
27. vimapam
28. vīram
ivikaḷ aṉittum iṉṟu kiṭippatu aritu.  ivi kaḷil cila accēṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cila tamiḻil moḻiyākkam ceyyap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷ cemmiyākak kāṇappaṭavilli.  pāṇṭiccēriyil uḷḷa pirañcuk kaḻakam (Institute Francis D’ Indalogie) perum muyaṟci ceytu acittākamam (VOL I 1964, Vol II 1967) iruravākamam (Vol 1961) mirukaṇṭarākamam (1962) mūlanūṟkaḷi acciṭṭuḷḷatu.  ēṉiya ākamaṅkaḷiyum acciṭṭup piṉ pirañcu moḻiyil moḻiyākkam ceytu acciṭṭuppaṭittup payaṉaṭiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa āval 1972ām āṇṭu vari oru civapaktarukkum piṟakkavilli.  āṉāl pirañcu nāṭṭil uḷḷa pirañcukkārar kaḷāṉa kiṟittavarkaḷum nāttikarkaḷum pallāyiram rūpāy celaviṭṭu pirañcil moḻiyākkam ceytu paṭittu iṉpuṟṟu varukiṉṟārkaḷ.  ceṉṉiyil kāmikākamam moḻiyākkam ceyyap peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟum moḻiyākkam ciṟappāka illi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  civākamaṅkaḷukku 113 upākamaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avikaḷi nām kaṇṭum kēṭṭum aṟiyōm.
3. cāktākamaṅkaḷ
cāktākamaṅkaḷ 64. ivikaḷil kūṭa ippoḻutu ellām kiṭippatu aritu.  ākamaṅkaḷ nāṉku pirivāka uḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷ muṟiyē (1) ñāṉapātam (2) yōkapātam (3) kiriyāpātam (4) cariyāpātam eṉappaṭum.
1. ñāṉapātam
ñāṉapātattil tatvattirayam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa mupporuḷ uṇmi viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  civākamaṅkaḷil pati, pacu, pācam eṉpaṉavum viṇavākamaṅkaḷil cittu, acittu, īcuvaraṉ eṉpaṉavum cāktākamaṅkaḷil civam, cakti, āṉmā eṉpaṉavum mupporuḷāyk koṇṭu vivarikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟi viḷakkum vakiyil uṟpatti oḻuṅku urikkappaṭukiṉṟatu.  ulakam eppaṭit tōṉṟiyatu eṉṟu taṟkāla aṟiviyalvātikaḷum viyakkum muṟiyil uṟpatti viḷakkam paṭippaṭiyāy ēṟṟam peṟṟa ceyti viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
civākamaṅkaḷiṉ ñāṉapātam cantāṉa kuravar eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum meykaṇṭa tēvar, aruṇanti civāc cāriyār, maṟiñāṉa campantar, umāpati civāccāriyār eṉṟa nālvarāl 14 nūlkaḷāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭu civacittānta camayattiṉ aṟivuk kaḷañciyamāka miḷirkiṉṟatu.  civa neṟikku ippatiṉāṉku nūlkaḷum ātāramākat tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.  inta nāṉku kuravarkaḷil meykaṇṭa tēvar civa vēḷāḷar. aruṇanti civāccāriyārum, umāpati civāccāriyārum āticivarkaḷ. maṟiñāṉa campantar pirāmaṇar.  ivar civatīṭci peṟṟu civar āṉatu maṭṭumaṉṟi cantāṉa kuravarākavum uyarntār.  vaṭamoḻi ākama nūlkaḷiṉ ñāṉapātattip paṭikka muṭiyātavarkaḷ ippatiṉāṉku nūṟ kaḷiyum paṭittup payaṉ aṭiyalām.
tirumūlar iyaṟṟiya tirumantiram tamiḻmoḻiyil ākkap paṭṭa ākamam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  itu vaṭamoḻi ākamatti viṭa kuṟinta nūlaṉṟu. cila vicayattil uyarntatu eṉṟum urikkalām.
2 yōkapātam 
yōka pātattil pirāṇāyāmam mutaliya cātaṉikaḷum āṟu ātāraṅkaḷiṉ niliyum kuṇṭaliṉiyiṉ ilaṭcaṇamum, ātma cutti, antar yākam mutaliyaṉavum pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  civa, viṇava, cākta ākamaṅkaḷ ākiya mūṉṟil cākta ākamaṅkaḷē inta yōkap payiṟciyi virivāyk kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ataṟkākap pala nūṟkaḷum upācaṉā mārkkaṅkaḷum koṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
3 kiriyāpātam
kiriyāpātattil karṣaṇam mutal piratiṣṭi variyuḷḷa kiriyikaḷum avaṟṟiṟkuriya mantiraṅkaḷum utcavaṅkaḷ, pirāyac cittaṅkaḷ, nittiya nimittiya pūcā vivaraṅkaḷ, camaya vicēca nirvāṇa tīṭcāk kiramaṅkaḷ, atikāri pētaṅkaḷ, varṇāccirama tarmaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  avikaḷ kiriyāpāta mantiram, kiriyi, pāvaṉi eṉa mūṉṟu pakutikaḷākap pirikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  mantiraṅkaḷiṉ pākupāṭu aṭcaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam vivarikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
mantiraṅkaḷi (1) cittam (2) cātyam (3) vacitam (4) ripu eṉa nālu pirivukaḷākap pirittu (1) cittam eṉpati cepippatālē palaṉit tarattakka pañcāṭcaram, caṭāṭcaram, aṣṭāṭcaram, pañcataci pōṉṟa mantiraṅkaḷāka vimarcikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  
(2) cātyam eṉpavi kiriyiyōṭu cērttu mantiram ōtappaṭa vēṇṭum itik kāraṇākamam,
mantiram vinākriyā nāti
karṣaṇān yarccaṉā ntakakā
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
karṣaṇam mutal piratiṣṭi vari ovvoru paṭiyilum mantiraṅkaḷ ōtappeṟal vēṇṭum.  karṣaṇam eṉṟāl kiruṣi ceytal atāvatu ālayam amikkappaṭavēṇṭiya iṭatti uḻutal eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.  intap pakutiyil kōyil nirmāṇaṅ kaḷukku uriya nilattiṉ taṉmi, aḷavu, paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ pariṇāmam amippu mutaliyavikaḷ pēcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  cilā cantira haṇam eṉṟa pakutiyil uruvam ceyya vēṇṭiya kalliṉ taṉmi āṇ, peṇ, ali eṉṟa ataṉ pākupāṭukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  maṇi ōci pōṉṟa nātamuṭiya kal peṇ eṉṟum ataṉāl peṇ uruvaṅkaḷ cetukkappaṭavēṇṭum eṉṟum nātam illāta kal ali eṉṟum ataṉāl pīṭaṅkaḷ camikka vēṇṭum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.  ivikaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.  ākamaṅkaḷē muṭivāṉa nūl;  atikāra pūrvamāṉa caṭṭa nūl.
ciṟpikku vēṇṭiya payiṟcikaḷaḷikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.  mēlum cilikaḷiṉ amippu, aḷavi muṟi, pāvaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  ākamaṅkaḷil teyvaccili vaṭivaṅkaḷ eppaṭi irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.  itaṉ viḷakkaṅkaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ākamaṅkaḷukkuc ciṟpa cāttiraṅkaḷ muraṇpaṭṭirukka muṭiyātu.  eṅkāvatu muraṇpāṭu kāṇappaṭṭāl  ciṟpanūlit taḷḷiviṭṭu ākama vitikaḷi mēṟkoḷḷa vēṇṭum.  ākamaṅkaḷ civaneṟiyāḷarkaḷiṉ uyarnta karuvūlam, vilimatikka voṇṇāta karuvūlam.  ākamaṅkaḷip peṟṟiruppatē civarkaḷiṉ perumpēṟu.  ciṟpa nūṟkaḷi vicuvakarmā pōṉṟa teyva kammiyarkaḷum mayaṉ pōṉṟa acura kammiyarkaḷum yāttuḷḷaṉar.  ciṟpa nūṟkaḷ 32.  avikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
1. aṉumāṉ
2. ariṭirukam
3. āticāram
4. intiram
5. kaṟpāriyam
6. kāpilakālayūkam
7. cayittakam
8. cavumam
9. cāttikam

11. cittiram
12. taravaṭṭam
13. naḷam

32. vicālam
iliṅkam
iliṅkam eṉṟa patattiṟkuk kuṟi - aṭiyāḷam eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum.  civa ākamaṅkaḷil iṟivaṉ āṇ peṇ vaṭivāka irukkiṟāṉ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  eṉavē camaya nūṟkaḷ teyvatti ammiyappaṉ eṉṟu pōṟṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ammiyiṉ ciṉṉamāka pīṭamum appaṉiṉ ciṉṉamāka uruṇṭi vaṭivilāṉa ciṉṉamum iṇittu civaneṟiyiṉ puṉitac ciṉṉamākat tamiḻarkaḷum tirāviṭarkaḷum piṟarum avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaḷum putiya kaṟkālantoṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṟārkaḷ.  putiya kaṟkālattil, eḵku āyutaṅkaḷ illāta kālattil orumuka vaṭṭa vaṭivamāṉa kalliyum ataṉ mītu nīṇṭa kuḻavikkal pōṉṟa vaṭivattiyum vittu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar.  ituvē civaliṅkam eṉap piṟkālattil eḻuntatu.
piṟkāla aṟiñarkaḷ aḷaviṭaṟkariya āṟṟal vāynta āṇṭa vaṉukku aṭiyāḷamāka ōr aḷaviyil vippatu iliṅkam ākum eṉṟaṉar.  iṉṟu iliṅkam eṉṟāl civālayaṅkaḷil uṇṇāḻikiyil irukkum aruvuruvat tirumēṉi tāṉ kuṟippākak koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu.
iliṅkavakikaḷ
ātiyil pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ putiya kaṟkālattil orēoru vaki iliṅkamtāṉ iruntatu. appāl tāvara iliṅkam caṅkamaliṅkam eṉa iruvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.  tāvara liṅkam niliyākat tirukkōyilil iruppatu, caṅkama iliṅkam aṉparkaḷ akattil iruppatu eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
itaṉpiṉ iliṅkaṅkaḷ mūṉṟu vakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭaṉa.  1.  viyaktaliṅkam, 2. avviyakta iliṅkam 3. viyaktā viyakta liṅkam eṉap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pīṭamum iliṅkamumāka iruppatu av viyaktaliṅkam. iliṅkattilē mukamum tōḷkaḷum veḷip paṭāmal iruppatu viyaktā viyaktaliṅkam.  iṟivaṉuṭiya tattuva nilikaḷi viḷakkum kurumūrttamāṉa viyākkiyāṉa (ñāṉa) taṭciṇāmūrtti vaṭivaṅkaḷi uḷḷaṭakki niṟkum aṣṭamūrtti vaṭivaṅkaḷum, civaperumāṉiṉ aṉpu neṟiyi aruḷ tiṟaṅkaḷi  irupattintu eḻiṟkōlaṅkaḷi viḷakkum  iliṅkōṟpa mūrttatti uḷḷaṭakkiya makēcuvara vaṭivaṅkaḷum, civaperumā ṉuṭiya paṭittal, kāttal, aḻittal, aruḷal, maṟittal ākiya toḻilkaḷik kāṭṭum āṭavallāṉ aruḷ uruvi aṭakkiya aṟupattiṉāṉku uruvaṅkaḷiyum aruṭkōlaṅkaḷiyum viḷakkum civamūrttaṅkaḷum viyakta iliṅkaṅkaḷ ākum eṉṟu civākamaṅkaḷ ceppukiṉṟaṉa.  iṟivaṉiṉ eṇvakik kōlaṅ kaḷāṉa (aṭṭamūrtti vaṭivaṅkaḷāṉa) teṉmukak kaṭavuḷ (taṭcaṇāmūrtti) maṇaaḻakar (kaliyāṇa cuntara mūrtti), ammi appar, makaṉuṭaṉ amarntirukkum kōlat teyvam (cōmākanta cukācaṉa mūrtti), cantira cēkara mūrtti, kālacammāra mūrtti, tiripurāntaka mūrtti, piṭcāṭaṉa mūrtti, kacacammāramūrtti, tiripuracammārar mūrtti vaṭivaṅkaḷ ellām viyakta iliṅkaṅkaḷākum.  ivi civaperumāṉiṉ aṉpi, aruḷi, āṟṟali, irakkatti, uṇmiyi, eḻili, ēṟṟatti, oppaṟṟa taṉmiyik kāṭṭum kōlaṅkaḷākum.  
cila aṟiñarkaḷ civiliṅkatti 1. acalaliṅkam 2.  calaṉa liṅkam 3.  calācalaliṅkam 4.  acalacalaliṅkam eṉṟu nāṉku vaki kaḷākap pirittuḷḷārkaḷ.  kōpuram, vimāṉam mutaliyaṉa acala liṅkam.  itayattil kaṟpikkappaṭuvatu calaṉaliṅkam.  vāṇaliṅkam, irattiṉa liṅkam mutaliyaṉa calācalaliṅkam.  vētikiyil maṇṭalamiṭṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭuvatu acalacalaliṅkam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.  vāṇaliṅkam mutaliyaṉa iṣṭa liṅkamākavum, parārtta pūciyil civacannitiyil aṅkaliṅkamākavum pūcikkappaṭṭāl atu calaliṅkam eṉavum, niṟṟalāl calācalaliṅkam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.  tīṭcikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu vētikiyiliṭṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭiṉ acalam eṉavum, iṣṭaliṅkamāka nāṭōṟum vaḻipaṭappaṭṭāl calam eṉavum niṟṟalāl acalam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
acala liṅkatti vimāṉam kōpuram mutaliyavikaḷ mūlam vaḻipaṭalām.  inta vimāṉaṅkaḷum kōpuraṅkaḷum aṭiperuttu nuṉi ciṟuttu irukkavēṇṭum.  āṉāl itu kōyilkaḷil uḷḷa iliṅkattiṟku muṟṟilum māṟupaṭṭatu.  cila vimāṉaṅkaḷ ari vaṭṭavaṭivil, ōr ari uruṇṭiyāṉa kiṇṇattik kaviḻtti ṉāṟ pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum.  teḷivākak kūṟiṉāl uṭikkappaṭṭa pātit tēṅkāy pōṉṟu kāṇappaṭum.  cila vimāṉaṅkaḷ catura vaṭivil irukkum. vimāṉaṅkaḷ palvēṟu ciṟpa vaṭivilē tikaḻum. ciṟpattil yāṉi mutal eṟumpu īṟāka vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, ūrvaṉa, makkaḷ, tēvar, maram, ceṭi koṭikaḷ aṉittum kāṇappaṭum. ekiptu, mekcikkō, ayarlāntu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil eṭuppikkap peṟṟa paṇṭikkālak kōyilkaḷum piṟkālak kiṟittavak kōyilkaḷum civaliṅka vaṭivil aṭi peruttu nuṉi ciṟuttuk kāṇappaṭum eṉṟu muṉivar pavul em.veṭ. pi.ec.ṭi avarkaḷ palvēṟu nāṭṭuk kōyilkaḷiyum paṭam pōṭṭu viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār.1
tamiḻ ākamam eṉappaṭum tirumantirattil, vimāṉam tūlaliṅkam eṉṟum vimāṉattiṉ aṭiyil tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ uḷḷē irukkum iliṅkam cūkkumaliṅkam eṉṟum palipīṭam pattiraliṅkam eṉṟum muṉivar kūṟiyuḷḷār.2
iṉṉum cila camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ, iliṅkatti aintu vakiyākap pirittup pañcapūta makāliṅkaṅkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟi yuḷḷārkaḷ.  ivi nilam, nīr, neruppu, kāṟṟu, vicumpu eṉṟu aintu pūtaṅkaḷip piratipalikkum.  pirutivi (nilam) liṅkam (ēkāmpara liṅkam) appu nīrliṅkam (campukēcuvaraliṅkam), akkiṉi (neruppu) liṅkam (aruṇācala liṅkam), vāyu (kāṟṟu) liṅkam (cīkāḷatti liṅkam), ākāya (vicumpu) liṅkam (tirumūlaliṅkam) eṉṟu aintu liṅkaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉpar. ivi muṟiyē kāñcipuram, tiru vāṉikkā, tiruvaṇṇāmali, cīkāḷatti, citamparam pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil uḷḷaṉa.  pañca liṅkaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭuvatu ciṟappāṉatu.
vimpam, kāmikam, vātuḷam, kāraṇam, cuvāyampu, cantāṉam, carvōttamam ākiya civākamaṅkaḷ 1.  parārtta liṅkam 2.  cuyampu liṅkam 3.  kāraṇaliṅkam 4.  teyvīka liṅkam 5.  āriṭaliṅkam 
6.  māṉuṭaliṅkam 7.  iccaṇi liṅkam eṉa ēḻu iliṅkaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
inta ēḻu iliṅkaṅkaḷil uyarntatu māṉuṭa iliṅkam.  ataṉiṉum uyarntatu teyvīka liṅkam. ataṉiṉum uyarntatu karaṇaliṅkam.  ataṉiṉum uyarntatu cuyampuliṅkam.  (iyaṟkiyāy eḻunta maliyum, kuṉṟum, ciṟu pāṟiyumākum) cuyampu liṅkam tāṉē tōṉṟiyatu.  karuṇi kaṉintu aṉparkaḷukku iṉpam aruḷum iṟivaṉ tāṉē taṉṉi veḷippaṭuttum uyarnta teyvīkak kuṟiyākum;  cuyampuliṅkam anātiyāṉatu.
curāṭṭirattil cōmanātaliṅkamum ciricilattil mallikārccuṉa liṅkamum ucciṉiyil makākāḷaliṅkam ōṅkārattil paramēcuvaraliṅkamum imācalattil kētāraliṅkamum iṭākiṉiyil pīmacaṅkaraliṅkamum vāraṇāciyil vicuvēcara liṅkamum kōtāvariyil tiriyampaka liṅkamum citāpurattil vittiya nātaliṅkamum tārukāvaṉattil nākēcuvaraliṅkamum cētuvil irāmēcuvaraliṅkamum civālayattil kucumēcaliṅkamum ākap paṉṉiraṇṭu cōtiliṅkamum uḷateṉṟu kōṭiruttira caṅkiti kūṟukiṟatu.
mukaliṅkam
ituvari eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya iliṅkaṅkaḷ ciṟpat toḻil ciṟappuṟṟu eḻāta kālattil ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.  ciṟpat toḻil ciṟappuṟṟu eḻuntatum iliṅkavaṭivil oru putiya māṟṟam eḻuntatu.  iliṅka vaṭivil oru maṉitaṉiṉ mukam pōṉṟa vaṭivam cetukkap peṟṟatu. itu mukaliṅkam eṉappaṭṭatu. itu civaperumāṉiṉ mārpiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pākamākak kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
cilakālam ceṉṟu iṟivaṉ muṉpakkam maṭṭum pārkkak kūṭiyavaṉ allaṉ, piṉpuṟamum pārkkak kūṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu iliṅkattiṉ taṇṭil muṉpuṟamum piṉpuṟamum iru uruvaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.  itu tuvimukiliṅkam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu.  appāl; mūṉṟu, nāṉku pakkaṅkaḷilum mukaṅkaḷ cetukkit tirimukaliṅkam caturmukaliṅkam amittaṉar.  iṟivaṉ nāṉku ticikaḷiyum pārkkak kūṭiyavaṉ eṉpati viḷakkuvataṟkuc catur muka liṅkam amittaṉar.  piṉṉar iṟivaṉ nāṉku ticikaḷiyum viṇṇiyum pārkkak kūṭiyavaṉ eṉpati viḷakkum poruṭṭup pañcamuka iliṅkatti uruvākkiṉar.  iliṅkattaṇṭiṉ nāṟ puṟamum nāṉkumukamum ataṉ ucciyil oru mukamāka aintu mukaṅkaḷ amittaṉar.  ip pañca mukaliṅkam iṟivaṉiṉ aintu kōlaṅ kaḷāṉa vāmatēva, taṟpuruca, akōra, caṭiyōcaṭa, īcāṉa eṉpavi kaḷi viḷakkum eṉṟu kūṟuvar.  tiruuṇṇāḻikiyil oru vāyil iruntāl ēkamukaliṅkamum, iru vāyilkaḷ iruntāl tuvi mukaliṅkamum, mūṉṟu vāyil iruntāl tirimukaliṅkamum nāṉku vāyilkaḷ iruntāl caturmukaliṅkamum irukkavēṇṭum.  nāṉku vāyilkaḷ uḷḷa uṇṇāḻikiyil pañca mukaliṅkamum irukkalām.  inta iliṅkaṅkaḷil taṟpuruṭa mūrttam kiḻakku nōkkiyum akōra mūrttam teṟku nōkkiyum caṭiyōcaṭa mūrttam mēṟku nōkkiyum vāma tēvamūrttam vaṭakku nōkkiyum īcāṉa mūrttam viṇṇi nōkkiyum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
makkaḷ nākarikamuṟṟu vaḷara vaḷarac ciṟpa aṟivum āṟṟalum vaḷarntaṉa.  iliṅka vaṭivilum māṟṟaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa.  toṭakkat til irunta iliṅka uruvam mukaliṅkamāki iliṅkattiṉ taṇṭil civaperumāṉiṉ mārpiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakuti iṭam peṟṟiruntatu.  appāl civaperumāṉiṉ muḻaṅkāliṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakuti iliṅkattiṉ mītu cetukkappaṭṭatu.  inta vaṭivum iliṅkōṟpa mūrttiyiṉ uruvam eṉappaṭum.  ataṉpiṉṉar iliṅkattiṉmītu civaperumāṉiṉ muḻu vaṭivamum cetukkappaṭṭatu.  inta vaṭivam ciṟpattuṟiyiṉi ārāyum aṟiñarkaḷukku ituvari teriyā tiruntatu.  ippoḻutu anta vaṭivam āntira nāṭṭic cērnta kuṭimallam eṉṟa iṭattil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.  itaṟkup piṉṉarē civaperumāṉiṉ uruvam taṉiyākac cetukkappaṭṭu mūrttaṅkaḷāka uruvakappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.  mutaṉ mutalākac civaperumāṉ (teṉmukakkaṭavuḷ taṭcaṇāmūrtti) makēntiramali yil tamiḻ nāṭṭic cērnta nāṉku muṉivarkaḷukkuk kallāla marattiṉ kīḻ aṟamuritta taṭcaṇāmūrttam uruvākkappaṭṭatu.  ciṟpa aṟivum ciṟpikaḷiṉ kali nuṇukkamum āṟṟalum camaya aṟivum vaḷara vaḷarap palvēṟu mūrttaṅkaḷum iṭampeṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻ makkaḷ aṟiviṉ mutircciyilē camayattik kaṇṭaṉar.  avarkaḷ mikat toṉmiyāṉa iṉattavarkaḷāy iruntatiṉāl avarkaḷiṉ camayat toṭakkamum vaḷarcciyum naṉku kāṇak kūṭiyatāy irukkiṟatu.  makkaḷiṉ camutāyam toṭakka kālattilē camaya uṇarvu peṟṟut teyva nampikki koṇṭu teyvaṅ kaḷukkut taṅkaḷiṉ aṟiviṟkēṟpa uruvam amittu, appāl aṟivuvaḷarcciyiṉ nilikaḷukkēṟpat teyvaṅkaḷiṉ ciṉṉamāṉa uruvaṅkaḷum paṭippaṭiyāy vaḷarcci peṟṟu vantuḷḷataṟku uriya meyccāṉṟukaḷāka iliṅkaṅkaḷum mūrttaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
ulakil oru aṅkulantoṭṭu nūṟuaṭi vari uyaramuḷḷa iliṅkaṅkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.  imayamaliyi cuyampuliṅkamāka eṇṇi vaḻipaṭalām.  inta nīṇilattiyē iliṅkamāka eṇṇi vaḻi paṭalām.  mikanuṇṇiya aṇuttoṭṭu periya malikaḷ vari ellām iliṅka vaṭivil uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  intap pirapañcamē iliṅka vaṭivil amintuḷḷatu.  kaṅki mutal taṇ poruni - paḵṟuḷiyāṟu vari ellā āṟukaḷum iliṅkamayamāy iruppatāka eṇṇi vaḻipaṭalām.  ulakil uṟiyum uyirkaḷi uyvikkum poruṭṭu umāpati iliṅka vaṭivil tikaḻkiṟār.  kaṉṟi niṉittatum pacuviṉ maṭiyil pāl curappatu pōl, pirapañcam muḻuvatum paravi niṟkum iṟivaṉ uyirkaḷ mītu karuṇi koṇṭu iliṅka vaṭivil tōṉṟi aruḷkiṉṟār eṉṟu civañāṉa cittiyār pōṉṟa civa camaya tattuva nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cakala iliṅkaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭuvaṉa aṟupattu nāṉku uruvattirumēṉikaḷ (civamūrttaṅkaḷ) eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ kūṟu kiṉṟaṉa.  ivaṟṟi aṣṭāṭṭa vikrahaṅkaḷ eṉṟum ivi yāvum niṣkaḷa liṅkattiṉiṉṟu āvirppavittavi eṉṟum kūṟap paṭṭirukkiṟatu.  aṣṭāṭṭa vikrakaṅkaḷic curukki 25 makātēva mūrttaṅkaḷāka kāraṇākamam kūṟukiṉṟatu.  mūṉṟu aṭṭamūrttaṅ kaḷum oru iliṅkōṟpa mūrttamum kūṭi makātēvamūrttam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.  ivikaḷic curukkiyē aṭṭamūrttam - eṇvaki vaṭivaṅkaḷ amikkappaṭṭaṉa.

iḵtaṉṟi iṉṉum cila civamūrttaṅkaḷum uṇṭu.  avikaḷil cila inta aṟupattu nāṉkil iṭampeṟṟum mēl uḷḷa cila mūrttaṅkaḷ nīkkappaṭuvatum uṇṭu.
itaṉ mūlam tamiḻarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭuvanta iliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷ āṇ peṇ kuṟikaḷ eṉṟu pakivarkaḷ kūṟiya iḻic coṟkaḷ aṭipaṭṭup pōkiṉṟaṉa.  āṉāl tamiḻ makkaḷ toṭakka kālamutal vaḻipaṭṭu vanta tāyiṉ ciṉṉamum appāl vaḻipaṭṭu vanta tantiyiṉ ciṉṉamum iṇintu tāyt teyvattiṉ ciṉṉamākavum tantit teyvattiṉ ciṉṉamākavum civaliṅkam viḷaṅkukiṟatu.  iṟutiyāka makkaḷiṉ aṟivu mutircciyil eḻunta ammiyappar allatu maṅki paṅkaṉ (arttanārīcuvarar) vaṭivattip pirati palikkum civaliṅkavaṭivam tōṉṟiyatu eṉpatu maṟukka muṭiyāta uṇmiyākum.  iṟivaṉ aṉpum aruḷum uḷḷavaṉ eṉpati uṇarttuvataṟku iliṅka vaṭivam eḻuntatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.  ulakamāṉatu caṭam cakti eṉṟa iru perum uṇmikaḷi aṭippaṭiyāk koṇṭu amintu niṟpatu pōla iliṅka vaṭivamāṉatu aṭiyil āvuṭiyārum mēlē iliṅkamum peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.  iṉṉum ivikaḷukku eṇṇaṟṟa tattuvaṅkaḷ kaṟpippārum palar.
iliṅkattiṉ tattuvam
civaperumāṉiṉ cōtit taṉmiyi viḷakkik kāṭṭuvataṟku vaṭṭamākat tūṇ pōṉṟa oruvaṭivic civaliṅkam eṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ kūṟiṉar.  muṟkālattil ekōvāvi vaṇaṅkum kiṟita varkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāṉa epirēyarkaḷum ittūṇ vaṇakkam purintaṉar eṉṟu kiṟitava mata nūlāṉa pipiḷ  kūṟukiṟatu.1  muṟkālat tamiḻarkaḷum mutaṉ mutalāka maraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil  taṟi eṉṉum kal tūṇi naṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar eṉṟu namatu paḻantamiḻ nūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.  ittūṇ vaḻipāṭē civaliṅka vaḻipāṭāka malarntatu.
civaliṅkam eṉṉum col, liṅ - layam; kam - tōṟṟam, eṉṟu poruḷ paṭuvatāl ulakam tōṉṟi oṭuṅkuvataṟku uriya iṭam eṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭum.  iliṅkam oḷi eṉṟa poruḷil aintu toḻilkaḷāl ulakukku oḷi ceyvatāl, iliṅkam eṉṟa peyar eḻuntatu.
iḵtaṉṟi iliṅkam eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷam eṉṟu poruḷkoṇṭāl itu oru poruḷi uṇarttuvataṟku uriya kuṟiyākak koḷḷalām.  cuṭṭik kāṭṭamuṭiyāta oṉṟi oru kuṟiyiṉ mūlam atu ittaṉmiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkip pōl civam ettaṉmiyuṭiyatu eṉṟu kāṭṭamuṭiyātatāl atu kaltūṇik koṇṭu civaliṅkam eṉṟa peyarāl kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
civaliṅkam piraṇava corūpam. ñāṉam karuvi karaṇam mutaṟkoṇṭu pala poruḷkaḷic civaliṅkam eṉṟu kūṟuvar. ācāraliṅkam kuruliṅkam civaliṅkam caṅkaraliṅkam eṉṟu pala iliṅka pētaṅkaḷ uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟuvar civar. tirumūlar
malarnta ayaṉmāl uruttiraṉ makēcaṉ
palantarum aimukaṉ paravintu nātam
nalantaruñ catti civaṉvaṭi vākip
palantaru liṅkam parānanti yāmē	(tirumantiram 1745)
eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. itaṉāl paracivattiṟku iliṅkam ātāra maṉṟu. muḻuuruvattirumēṉi  iliṅkam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuva tilli. iliṅkam pīṭam avviyaktaliṅkam cantiracēkarar mutaliyaṉa mūrttaṅkaḷ - atāvatu viyaktaliṅkaṅkaḷ.

ulaka uṟpattiyum makkaḷiṉ tōṟṟamum
ulakiṉ tōṟṟam
nām vāḻum ivvulakam, iṉṟu eppaṭik kāṇappaṭukiṟatō appaṭip pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kāṇappaṭṭilatu; pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukkup piṉṉum atu paḻiya niliyil kāṇappaṭavilli; palvēṟu māṟṟaṅkaḷi eytiyuḷḷatu. palvēṟu ūḻikaḷil untiyeḻunta kaṭaṟperukkukaḷāl nilap parappu nīrpparappākavum, nīrpparappu nilapparappākavum māṟutal aṭintuḷḷaṉa. inta māperum  maṇṇulakam palvēṟu kālaṅ kaḷil peṟṟirunta vaṭiva māṟṟaṅkaḷi aṟiviyal aṟiñarkaḷ āyntu pala ariya uṇmikaḷi ulakiṟku uṇarttiyuḷḷaṉar.
nilanūl cāṉṟukaḷ
iṉṟiya nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulam vāynta aṟiviyal viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ paḻaṅkālattu ippārulakil paraviyirunta kaṇṭaṅkaḷiyum kaṭalkaḷiyum aṟiviyal pāṅkāka āyntu aṟintu pala uṇmikaḷi viḷakkik kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. avarkaḷ āyntu kaṇṭa muṭiviṉpaṭi, kiḻakkut tīvukaḷ, ātirēliyā 
(Australia) intiyā, āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ ulakil oru perum nilapparappāka nīṇṭa kālam nilavi niṉṟatu.  apperiya nilapparappu kōṇṭvāṉā (Gondwana) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. immāperum kōṇṭvāṉāk kaṇṭam, 300-kōṭi āṇṭukaḷ vari. intiyā, teṉamerikkā, ātirēliyā, āppirikkā, aṇṭārṭṭikkā mutaliya nilapparappukaḷ oṉṟiṇinta oru māperum nilap parappāka nilaviyatu. immāperum kaṇṭam cumār 15 kōṭi āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ citiyat toṭaṅkiyatu.  ittoṉmiyāṉa kaṇṭattil cila uyiriṉaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āṉāl atil mutalil makkaḷ tōṉṟavilli. mikat toṉmiyāṉa ikkaṇṭattiṉ varalāṟu, iṉṉum teḷivāka viḷakkampeṟa vēṇṭumāṉāl pariṇāma vaḷarccit tattuvattiṉit tuṇikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. putiya kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷ varāta akkālattilēyē maṇivācakap perumāṉ inta uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṉum aṟiviyal koḷkiyi ulakiṟku uṇarttiṉār. uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa pariṇāma vāta cittāntattiṟkuc civa camaya maṟumalarccikku ākkam aḷitta nāṉku camaya kuravarkaḷil oruvarāka matikkappeṟum māṇikkavācakar aṭippaṭi iṭṭār eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl atu oru ciṟitum mikiyākātu.
pullākip pūṭāyp puḻuvāy maramākip
palviruka mākip paṟaviyāyp pāmpākik
kallāy maṉitarāyp pēyāyk kaṇaṅkaḷāy
vallacura rāki muṉivarāyt tēvarāyc
cellāa niṉṟit tāvara caṅkamattuḷ
ellāp piṟappum piṟantiḷittēṉ emperumāṉ					(tiruvācakam)
eṉṟu civa camayikaḷ maṟinūlākap pōṟṟap peṟṟuvarum tiruvācakattil kūṟiyiruppatu cintikkat takkatu. nāṭṭuppaṟṟu mikka nāṇayamāṉa talivarum, tamiḻppulavarum, civa cintānta aṟiñaru māṉa mayili muṉivar tiru. vi. kaliyāṇa cuntara mutaliyār uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṉum koḷki civa camaya neṟikku uṭaṉpāṭāṉa koḷki eṉpati ēṟṟu murukaṉ eṉṟa tam nūlil1 ataṟku viḷakkamum aḷittuḷḷār. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum :
viṇava camayam, tirumāl ātiyil mīṉākavum (maccā vatāram) appāl āmiyākavum (kūrmāvatāram) piṉṉar paṉṟi yākavum (varāka avatāram) piṟaku ciṅkamākavum (naracimma avatāram) piṟaku kuḷḷa maṉitaṉākavum (vāmaṉa avatāram) iṟutiyāka  maṉitaṉākavum (paracurāma avatāram) tōṉṟiṉār eṉṟu kūṟiyatu. civa camayam, maṉita iṉam nīril vāḻum uyirp pirāṇiyāyt tōṉṟi nilattil vāḻum vilaṅkākap pariṇamittu iṟutiyil maṉitaṉāka vaḷarcci peṟṟuḷḷatu eṉṟu kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. eṉavē viṇavamum civamum uḷḷatu ciṟattal eṉṟa pariṇāmavāta cittāntatti aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭaṉa eṉpatu naṉku pulappaṭukiṉṟatu.
uyiriṉam  nīril tōṉṟi, appāl nīrilum nilattilum vāḻap payiṉṟu, piṉṉar nilattil vāḻat toṭaṅkiyatu. appāl kuṉṟil vāḻat toṭaṅkiyatu. puḻuvāyt tōṉṟiya uyiriṉam vaḷarcci peṟṟu, ūrvaṉavāy, naṭappaṉavāy, paṟappaṉavāy, paṭippaṭiyāy kuraṅkiṉamāy māṟi iṟutiyil maṉita iṉamāka māṟṟam peṟṟu uyarnteḻuntuḷḷatu!
makkaḷ malikaḷil vāḻat toṭaṅkiṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷ āṭiyiṉṟi vāṭiyil vataṅkiṉar. uṟiyuḷ iṉṟi, kāṭṭilum mēṭṭilum uṟintu vantaṉar. pōtiya uṇaviṉṟi arip paṭṭiṉiyāy avala niliyil avatiyuṟṟaṉar. veyyilum, maḻiyum, kāṟṟum, paṉiyum kuḷirum avarkaḷi vāṭṭi vatittaṉa. yāṉiyum, pūṉiyum, eliyum, puliyum, nāyum, nariyum, kāṭṭu māṭum, karaṭiyum makkaḷik koṉṟu kuvittaṉa. makkaḷāl, vilaṅku kaḷōṭu pōriṭa muṭiyavilli. avarkaḷiṉ uṭalum, uṟuppu kaḷum vilaṅkukaḷōṭu pōrāṭuvataṟku ēṟṟatāka illi. eṉavē maṉita iṉam, cintaṉi ceyya muṟpaṭṭatu. aṟivip payaṉpaṭuttat toṭaṅkiyatu. ‘kÅj ïd« thœtjh - khŸtjh? eṉṟa cūḻnili avarkaḷ aṟivi vaḷappaṭuttiyatu - vāḻviṟku vaḻikōliyatu; kār iruḷil kāṭārnta kallum, muḷḷum, putarum niṟinta malip pakutikaḷil vāḻkkiyil tōlvi mēl tōlvi kaṇṭa makkaḷ iṟutiyil oḷiyik kaṇṭaṉar. kalliyum, villiyum makkaḷ tam vāḻkkip pōrāṭṭattil payaṉpaṭutti iṟutiyil vilaṅku ulaki veṟṟi koṇṭaṉar. neruppi uṇṭākkap payiṉṟu vilaṅkiṉatti viraṭṭavum, pāmpu, tēḷ, pōṉṟa koṭiya nacciṉaṅ
kaḷic cuṭṭup pocukkavum, uṇavup poruḷkaḷi tīyiṉāl cuṭṭu - vēka vittu uṇṇavum toṭaṅkiṉar. āṉāl malikaḷil eḻunta taṭpa veppanili, uṇavup poruḷ taṭṭuppāṭu peṟac ceytatu. mēlum, maṉitaṉ kūrmiyāṉa kaṟ karuvikaḷip peṟap
peritum virumpiṉāṉ. eṉavē maliyiṉiṉṟu kīḻē iḻintu varum āṟṟiṉ karikaḷiṉ vaḻiyāyk kīḻē iṟaṅki vara makkaḷiṉam muṉ vantatu. makkaḷ iṉattiṉ oru pakuti maliyi viṭṭuk kīḻē iṟaṅki vara oruppaṭavilli. āṉāl perum pakuti makkaḷ kīḻ nōkki vantu mali aṭivārattil uḷḷa kāṭukaḷil taṅkiṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ oru cilar, āṟṟiṉ kari vaḻiyāka mīṇṭum ceṉṟu kaḻaṉikaḷum, ēri, kuḷam pōṉṟa nīr nilikaḷum niṟinta camaveḷikaḷil taṅka muṟpaṭṭaṉar. oru cilar mīṇṭum naṭantu ceṉṟu kaṭaṟkariyi aṭintu aṅku vāḻa muṟpaṭṭaṉar. makkaḷ ātikālattil taṅkaḷ nalvāḻkkikkākat tērnteṭukkappaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷ muṟiyē kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉappaṭṭaṉa. mali vāḻkkiyilē makkaḷ tāy āṭciyik kaṇṭaṉar. koṟṟavi eṉṉum tāyt teyvamum, pacupati eṉṉum tantit teyvamum tōṉṟiviṭṭaṉa. malimakaṉ, malimakaḷ eṉṟa peyaril teyva vaḻipāṭu toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. appāl makaṉ teyva vaḻipāṭum, aṇṇaṉ teyva vaḻipāṭum vantuviṭṭaṉa. paḻam perum tamiḻ ilakkaṇa ēṭāka ēṟṟam peṟum tolkāppiyam -
māyōṉ mēya kāṭuṟi ulakamum
cēyōṉ mēya mivari ulakamum
vēntaṉ mēya tīmpuṉal ulakamum
varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇal ulakamum
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytaleṉac
colliya muṟiyāl collavum paṭumē	(tol.poruḷ: 252))
eṉṟu kūṟiya cūttirattāl, kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal ākiya nāṉku tiṇi nilaṅkaḷum, cēyōṉ, māyōṉ, vēntaṉ, varuṇaṉ ākiya nāṉku nilat teyvaṅkaḷum iṅku kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. piṟkālattil kuṟiñcikkum mullikkum iṭip paṭṭa pakutikaḷ iyaṟkik kōḷāṟukaḷiṉāl varaṇṭu pāli eṉṟoru putiya tiṇi nilamum, paḻiyōḷ eṉṟa nilat teyvamum eḻuntatu. paḻiyōḷ eṉṉum tāyt teyvam ellā nilattiṟkum uriya periya teyvamāka eḻuntu piṉ pāli nilattiṉ taṉit teyvamāka māṟiyatu. maruta nila vaḷarcciyil civi eṉṉum peyarāl civaṉōṭu iṇinta teyvamāka ammi appaṉ eṉṟa peyarāl oppaṟṟa teyva vaḻipāṭu eḻuntatu.
aivaki nilappākupāṭu
tamiḻar peṟṟa camūka vaḷarccip perukkāl makkaḷ taṅkaḷ uṇavup piracṉiyit tīrkkap palvēṟu tiṇi nilaṅkaḷil kuṭiyēṟi, vayiṟāra uṇṭu uvakiyuṟṟu, āṭippāṭi iṉpuṟṟu vāḻa muṟpaṭṭaṉar. inta nāṉilap -  pirivukaḷum, nāṉku nilaṅkaḷukkeṉa eḻunta maram, pū, puḷ, vilaṅku, yāḻ, muracu, teyvam mutaliyaṉavum nūṟkaḷil kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. āriyar innāṭṭiṟku vantu cumār 4000ām āṇṭukaḷ ākiṉṟaṉa. avarkaḷ intiyāvil pukum poḻutu āṭumāṭu mēyttu mēccal tarikkāka alintu tiriyum nāṭōṭi makkaḷāy iruntaṉar. avarkaḷukku oru camayamō, perunteyvamō, tiruntiya moḻiyō, poruntiya eḻuttō, ki vanta kalikaḷō, pāriya paṇpāṭō, cīriya maṟi nūṟkaḷō illi. āṉāl avarkaḷ intiyā eṉum puṉita pūmiyi ākkiramippataṟku patiṉāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē tamiḻarkaḷum avarkaḷ vaḻivanta tirāviṭa makkaḷum nāṉilatti mēṉilamākki viṭṭaṉar. moḻi, eḻuttu, oviyam, ilakkiyam, ciṟpam, vīṭu, māḷiki, kōyil, aracu, camayam, teyvam maṟi nūṟkaḷ, aṟaneṟi, paṇpāṭu, nākarikam mutaliyavaṟṟi uruvākkiyuḷḷaṉar. tamiḻnāṭṭil pattāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarē eḵkup paṇpāṭu eḻuntu viṭṭatu. ērum cīrum kalappiyum koḻuvum eḻuttāṇiyum vāḷkaḷum kattikaḷum maṟi nūlkaḷum aṟaneṟikaḷum nāvāykaḷum kaṭalvaṇikamum eḻuntu viṭṭaṉa. poṉ aṇikaḷum, navamaṇikaḷum poṟcarikinūl iḻitta paṭṭāṭikaḷum pālāṭiyeṉṉa melliya āṭi kaḷum pacci, civappu vaṇṇantōynta paṉṉiṟa āṭikaḷum, pūntukilkaḷum paṭṭāṭikaḷum eṇṇaṟṟa kūlapporuḷkaḷum palvēṟu kiviṉip poruḷkaḷum arumpiviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻarkaḷ āḻkaṭalik kalaṅkaḷiṉ mūlam  kaṭantu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa kaltolivil uḷḷa nāṭukaḷilum tīvukaḷilum kuṭiyēṟi nākarikam parappiṉar. civa neṟiyi vaḷarttaṉar. ulakaṉittum civamaṇaṅ kamaḻumāṟu ceytaṉar.
nāṉilaṅkaḷilum kuṟiñci nilat teyvam cēyōṉ, (murukaṉ) mulli nilat teyvam māyōḷ, māyōṉ, marutanilat teyvam vēntaṉ, neytal nilat teyvam varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl civaṉ eṉṉum peyarik kuṟippiṭavilliyē eṉṟu palar viṉavukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiya ṉār, nilat teyvaṅkaḷi maṭṭum iṅkuk kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārē yoḻiya vēṟu teyvaṅkaḷiyō, allatu perunteyvaṅ kaḷiyō kuṟippiṭavilli. mēlum maruta nila vēḷāḷar tam nilat teyvamāka vēntaṉiyē vittup pōṟṟi yuḷḷaṉar. tol kāppiyar kālattil aracaṉōṭoppa, piṟanilat teyvamākac cēyōṉum māyōṉum, varuṇaṉum vittu eṇṇappaṭṭiruk kalām eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. mēlum tol kāppiyattil vēṟu cila teyvaṅkaḷum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭiruppati nām iṅku maṟantu viṭa muṭiyātu.
koṭinili kantaḻi vaḷḷi eṉṟu
vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ maṉṉiya mūṉṟum
kaṭavuḷ vāḻttoṭu kaṇṇiya varumē					(tolporuḷ. puṟa. 27)
eṉṟa tolkāppiyac cūttira uriyāl ñāyiṟu, neruppu, tiṅkaḷ eṉṟa mūṉṟi vāḻttuvatum kaṭavuḷ pōlavē eṇṇappaṭṭu varum eṉṟu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭu varuvatum cintikkat takkatu. koṭi nili - ucciyil niṟpatu : ñāyiṟu (cūriyaṉ). kantaḻi - paṟṟuk kōṭṭi aḻippatu: neruppu. vaḷḷi - kuḷircciyit taruvatu : tiṅkaḷ (cantiraṉ) eṉap poruḷpaṭum.
ivaṟṟāl tamiḻarkaḷiṭam nilat teyvaṅkaḷākiya cēyōṉ, māyōṉ, vēntaṉ (aracaṉ), varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) ākiya teyvaṅkaḷ tavira ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, neruppu mutaliyavikaḷum kaṭavuḷākak karutappaṭṭu vantaṉa eṉpatu naṉku teriyavarum. kaṭavuḷ eṉpatu ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantu niṟpatu; kuṇam kuṟiyaṟṟu, maṉam, moḻi, meykaḷukku eṭṭātatu eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. kaṭavuḷ kaṭantu niṟpatu. poruḷum cariyākattāṉ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu.
maṟaṅkaṭi kūṭṭiya tuṭinili, ciṟanta
koṟṟavi niliyum attiṇip puṟavē (tol.po. puṟa. 4)
veṟi aṟiciṟappiṉ vevvāy vēlaṉ
veṟiyāṭṭu ayarnta kāntaḷum	(to.po.puṟa. 5)
eṉṟa tolkāppiya cūttiraṅkaḷ mūlam koṟṟavi, vēlaṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷum nāṉilattil iruntu vantaṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu. 
cintu veḷi muttirikaḷil kāṇappeṟṟa ōviya eḻuttu kaḷukku maṟikuṟi eḻuttup poruḷ viḷakkam (Deceipher) tanta piṉlantup pērāciriyar ṭākṭar akō pārpōlā cintu veḷit tirāviṭa makkaḷ ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, caṉi mutaliya kōḷkaḷi vaṇaṅki vantaṉar. appāl avarkaḷ ñāyiṟṟic civaṉākavum, tiṅkaḷic civi ākavum tāyt teyvamākavum, cevvāyi murukaṉākavum, putaṉi nāṉmukaṉākavum, viyāḻaṉit tirumālākavum, caṉiyik kiruṣṇa ṉākavum vaḻipaṭṭirukkalām eṉṟum itteyvaṅkaḷukku civappu, pacci, mañcaḷ, nīlam, karuppu mutaliya niṟaṅkaḷ kaṟpikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟum poruḷ viḷakkam tantuḷḷaṉar.1
inta āyvu viḷakkattāl tamiḻarkaḷiṉ mūtātiyarkaḷiṭam ātiyil ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, putaṉ, viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, caṉi mutaliya kōḷvaḻipāṭu tanti, tāy vaḻipāṭākavum iruntu appāl civaṉ cakti vaḻipāṭākavum malarntirukkalām eṉṟu karuta iṭam aḷikkiṉṟatu. cevvāy murukaṉ vaḻipāṭākavum, viyāḻaṉ tirumāl vaḻipāṭākavum, veḷḷi varuṇaṉ (vāraṇaṉ) eṉṉum kaṭal teyva vaḻipāṭākavum pariṇamittuḷḷatu eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ikkaruttukkaḷi araṇ ceytāṟpōṉṟu civa camayamalarccikkum, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ciṟappiṟkum tamiḻ iṉattiṉ taṉittaṉmikkum ākkam aḷitta civa cittānta vittakarum paṉmoḻippulavarumāṉa tiru. kā. R¥ãukÂa ãŸis v«.V., em.el. tam nūlil viḷakkam tantuḷḷār. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
tamiḻil eḻunta aintu vaki nilattiṟkum uriya teyvaṅkaḷ vevvēṟākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. avvav nilattil uḷḷavarkaḷ vāḻkkip payaṉkaḷi virumpi atteyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. mutalil makkaḷ iṟanta tāy tantiyarit toḻat toṭaṅki avarkaḷi aṭakkam ceyta iṭattil kalli nāṭṭi akkalliṉ kaṇ, muṉṉōrkaḷ uyirōṭiruntu taṅkaḷ vaṇakkatti makiḻvuṭaṉ ēṟṟu, taṅkaḷukku nalaṉ taruvatāka nampi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. nāḷaṭiviṟ kiṭitta palaṉukku ēṟṟavāṟu vaḻipāṭu vaḷarcci aṭintu vantatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil potuvāka mukkaṇ teyva vaḻipāṭu nilavi iruntatu. ... tamiḻar paṇṭiya muṉṉōr vaṭivit teyva vaṭivamākak karutiṉar.
mukkaṇṇuṭiya teyvam, civam eṉṟa collāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. civam eṉṟa col tamiḻccol  eṉṟu ṭākṭar kiriyarcaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷār.1
civa eṉṟa col, cemmi eṉpataṉ aṭiyākap piṟantatu. atu civappu eṉṟum naṉmi, maṅkalam eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. tamiḻ makkaḷ vēṭṭuva vāḻkki niliyilirunta pōtu civaṉi vaḻipaṭṭamiyāl tamakkuḷ ariya ceyalāy matikkappaṭṭa pulikkoliyiyum pāmpu vaciyattiyum apperumāṉukku ēṟṟi vaṇaṅkiṉar pōlum. pulittōlāṭiyum pāmpu vaṇakkamum civaperumāṉukku uriyaṉavāyiṉa.
kūriya muṉiyiṉiyuṭiya maḻu akkālattuc ciṟanta paṭikkalamākum. vīrat tantipāl accamum, aṉpuṭiya tāyiṉ pāl nēcamum civaṉāratu tiruvaṭivil kalantuḷḷaṉa. civa vaṭivam pāti āṇum pātippeṇṇum kūṭiya vaṭivamākum.
tāy āṭciyum tanti āṭciyum
mutaṉ mutalāka ulakam aṉittilum tāy kuṭumpat taliviyāy iruntu avaḷ taṉṉantaṉiyāka makkaḷip peṟṟu vaḷarttu varum niliyil mutalil tāy āṭciyum tāyt teyvamum tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu camuka iyal āyvāḷarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iyaṟki niyatiyum ataṟku ēṟpuṭittāy amintuḷḷatu. tāyiṟ ciṟantatoru kōyilum illi eṉum paḻamoḻiyum ammi nī appaṉ nī eṉṟu teyva vaḻipāṭṭil tāyi muṉ vittup pōṟṟuvatum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil peṇṇi (tāyi) illāḷ eṉṟu ciṟappippatum, tamiḻil illāḷukku ēṟṟa etirppatam illā tiruppatum tamiḻnāṭṭil mutalil tāy āṭciyum appāl tāyt teyvamum tōṉṟiya taṟkāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ eṉalām. piṉṉarē tanti āṭciyum tantit teyva vaḻipāṭum nilaviyuḷḷatu eṉpatu ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil nilaviya nāṉilaṅkaḷilum naṭunilamākiya naṉcey nilattil (marutanilattil) kal ūḻiyilē camūkavaḷarcci uyar niliyuṟṟatu. inta nilattilētāṉ, mutaṉ mutalāka āṇum peṇṇum iyintu iṇintu illaṟam naṭattum kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkki uṟutiyuṟṟatu. tirumaṇam, tiruntiya camūka vāḻkkiyāka arumpiyatu. kaṇavaṉ, maṉivi eṉṉum kaṭṭuk kōppu malarntatu. ammi appaṉ eṉṟa patamē uruppeṟṟatu. kuṭumpat talivarkaḷāy tanti tāy eḻuntu camūkavaḷarcciyil aracaṉ araciyāy ūrāḷum muṟiyum, nāṭāḷum muṟiyum, araca kaṭṭilum araṇmaṉiyum, maṇi muṭiyum, ceṅkōlum, malarttārum araṇkaḷum, paṭikaḷum, koṭikaḷum, aracaciṉṉaṅkaḷum eḻuttum, eḻuttāṇiyum moḻiyum, ēṭukaḷum, ilakkiyaṅkaḷum, ōviyaṅkaḷum, āṭalkaḷum, pāṭalkaḷum, kavitikaḷum, kali
kaḷum paṇpāṭum, camayamum, teyvamum piṟantaṉa. kallūḻiyilē, tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē tamiḻakattil aracu eḻuntu viṭṭatu. tamiḻc caṅkam tōṉṟiviṭṭatu. camayam naṉku tali tūkkiyuḷḷatu.
ammi appaṉ āṭciyil amintuḷḷa aracu, araca ciṉṉaṅkaḷāṉa koṭippaṭi, koṭi, kuṭi, kutiri, yāṉi, tār, muracu mutaliyaṉa uṭiyaṉavāy aivakit toḻilum ceytu, āṟiloru kaṭamiyum peṟṟu uyarntu niṉṟatu eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyam cāṉṟu tarukiṉṟatu.
uruva vaḻipāṭu
iliṅka vaṭivil teyvatti vaḻipaṭum ciṟanta muṟi, paḻiya kal ūḻiyilē, kāl kōlap peṟṟuḷḷatu. appāl anta āḻnta ariya poruḷ aṭaṅkiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟi, putiya kal ūḻiyil niṟivu peṟṟu arumpiyatu. putiya kal ūḻiyiṉ toṭakka kālat tilē tuḷirtteḻunta irumpu ūḻiyil iliṅka vaḻipāṭu, āḻnta poruḷ aṭaṅkiya (artta puṣṭiyuḷḷa) tattuvañāṉac ceḻumi yuṭaṉ maṟumalarcci peṟṟu malarntatu. iliṅkattiṉ arukil kālkaḷi maṭakkip paṭuttirukkum kāḷiyiṉ vaṭivam iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu.
paḻaṅkal ūḻiyil eḻunta tāy āṭci tamiḻakattil taṉicciṟappuṭaṉ viḷaṅkiyatu. tāy iṟantatum avaḷ uṭalip puṉita nīrāṭṭi, maṇantarum malarkaḷit tūvi, maṇṇit tōṇṭip putittu, mēlē tāyiṉ niṉivuc ciṉṉamāka oru kalli nāṭṭiṉar. appāl akkalliṉ mītu nīrvārttu malar cūṭi vaḻipaṭum muṟiyum tōṟṟamuṟṟatu. appāl arumpiya tanti āṭciyil tanti iṟantapiṉ avar uṭal putikkappaṭṭa iṭattil avaratu niṉivuc ciṉṉamāka oru nīṇṭa kal naṭappaṭṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantatu. irumpu ūḻi eḻuntatum tāy āṭciyum tanti āṭciyum iṇintatu. tirumaṇa vāḻkki muṟi, tiṭṭamiṭṭu eḻuntatu. ammiyappaṉ vaḻipāṭu arumpiyatu. tāy tantiyar iṟantatum avarkaḷiṉ uṭal aṭuttaṭuttup putikkap paṭṭatu. tāy tantiyar peṟṟōrkaḷāka matippaṭṭaṉar. kaṇavaṉum, maṉiviyum orāviṟku iru kōṭu pūttāṟ pōṉṟu iruvarum iṇi piriyātu vāḻa vēṇṭiyavarkaḷ eṉṟa uyariya karuttu uruppeṟṟeḻuntatu. iṟivaṉum iṟiviyum iṇi piriyātu iṇintu ilaṅkupavarkaḷ eṉṟa tattuvam tōṉṟiyatu. eṉavē tanti civaṉ eṉṟum, tāy avaṉatu āṟṟal (cakti) eṉṟum karutappaṭṭatu. tamiḻar teyvam, pāti āṇum pāti peṇṇumāy pirikka muṭiyātu iṇintu piṇintu ilaṅkum tattuva cōti yāyk kāṇappeṟṟatu. katiravaṉil kāṇum ēḻuvaṇṇaṅkaḷil pāti cenniṟamākavum pāti nīlavaṭivilum kāṇappaṭuvati āyntu, civam cenniṟa vaṭivilum cakti karuniṟa vaṭivilum iruppatāy uṇarappaṭṭatu. eṉavē, ciṟpakkali vaḷarcci peṟāta paḻaṅ kālattil, tāyiṉ ciṉṉa vaṭivāka - kuḻivāṉa oru kalli aṭiyil vittu ataṉ mītu kuḻavikkal pōṉṟa oru nīṇṭa kalli vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. irumpu ūḻiyil tamiḻakattil kallic cetukkum irumpu āyutaṅkaḷ eḻuntatum, aṭiyil oru pīṭamum ataṉ mītu ammiyiṉ ciṉṉamāka oru vaṭṭamāṉa vaṭiva amippum ataṉmītu vaṭṭamāṉa oru ciṟu tūṇ vaṭivil oru amippum orē kallil amittu, civaliṅkam uruvākkap paṭṭatu. ivvaṭivi eṉṟum tamiḻar, āṇ - peṇ kuṟiyāka eṇṇiyatu illi. āṇ, peṇ kuṟikaḷi vaḻipaṭṭatum illi. ātiyil tāyiṉ ciṉṉamākavum, tantiyiṉ ciṉṉamākavum uruva vaḻipāṭṭi amittu appāl acciṉṉam civaṉ cakti vaṭivil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. aṟivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ aruḷākavum, āṟṟalākavum civaliṅkattik kaṇṭaṉar eṉpatu varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muntiya makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟikaḷi āyntu aṟintu ēṭukaḷ eḻutiya aṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭa uṇmiyākum. varalāṟṟaṟivō tattuva ñāṉamō, iṟiyiyalō aṟiyāta paḻaṅkāla āriyarkaḷ tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭip pārttu itu ciciṉa tēvar vaḻipāṭu (āṇ kuṟi peṇ kuṟi vaḻipāṭu) eṉṟu ēḷaṉam ceytaṉar. appāl avarkaḷē, iliṅka vaḻipāṭu ēṟṟam mikkatum;  tattuva ñāṉam potinta uyariya teyva vaḻipāṭu eṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar. piṉṉar ivvaḻipāṭṭi ēṟṟu iṉṟu vari iliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. ivvaḻipāṭṭi ātiyil tamiḻt tirunāṭṭil vāḻnta maruta nila vēḷāḷar kaṇṭamiyāl avarkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ ēṟṟattiṟkum, uyirōmpum uḻavut toḻiliṉ veṟṟikkum uṟutuṇiyāyk koṇṭa kāḷiyic civaṉukku vākaṉamāy aḷittaṉar. civaliṅkam arukil kālkaḷi maṭakkip paṭuttirukkum kāḷiyiṉ (nantiyiṉ) uruvi iṭam peṟac ceytuḷḷaṉar. kāḷi uruvam uḻippiṉ ciṉṉam. uḻippavarkkē ivvulaka vāḻvu maṭṭumiṉṟi maṟuulaka vāḻvum kiṭṭum eṉpati atu aṟivuṟuttukiṟatu. civaperumāṉ uṟiyum kayiliyampatiyil kāḷi mukamum maṉita uṭalum vāynta nantīcaṉ vāyil kāval ceyyum teyvamāka irukkiṟār. avar vāyilākac civaperumāṉ āṭaṟkali, pāṭaṟkali, ōviyakkali, kāviyak kali, eṇ kali, eḻuttukkali, moḻikkali, oḷik kali, viṇkali, maṇkali mutaliyavikaḷi makkaḷukku veḷip paṭuttiyuḷḷār eṉṟu tamiḻp purāṇaṅkaḷ (Myths) mūlam nām aṟikiṉṟōm. purāṇattuṟiyil (Mythology) pala marapurik koḷkikaḷum, kaṟpaṉip paḻaṅkatikaḷum niṟintuḷḷaṉa eṉpati nāmum pala akaḻāyvut tuṟi vallunarkaḷum aṟivōm. eṉṟālum avaṟṟil pala ariya uṇmikaḷi viḷakkap pala puṉinturikaḷ pukuttappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉpati maṟantu viṭak kūṭātu. mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ āyvukaḷil purāṇakkatikaḷukku ēṟṟa iṭam aḷittiruppati nām puṟakkaṇittuviṭakkūṭātu. purāṇak katikaḷi aṭippaṭiyāka vittē aṉittulakak kali mētiyāka viḷaṅkiya tamiḻp perumakaṉ āṉanta keṇṭiṣ kumāracāmi, iṟivaṉuṭiya tāṇṭavaṅkaḷi āyntu civa naṭaṉam (Dance of Shiva)” eṉṟa nūli eḻuti ulakiṟkaḷittup perumiyuṟṟār. annūl pirañcup pēraṟiñar urōmeyṉ urōlaṇṭu avarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattik kavarntatu. āṇṭavaṉiṉ tāṇṭava tattuvam avari āṭkoṇṭatu. ulakappukaḻ peṟṟa uyarnta viññāṉa mēti alparṭ īyṉṭiṉiṉ itayatti īrttatu.
paṇṭik kāla makkaḷiṉ civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu
paḻaṅkaṟkālattil kallic cetukkum kaviṉ kali uruppeṟavilli. makkaḷ maṇṇik kuvittuk kuviki vaṭivil (Cone) vaḻipaṭṭatuṇṭu. oru pārppaṉac ciṟuvaṉ (caṇṭēcuvarar) maṇṇik kuvittuk kūmpu uruvil civaperumāṉi vaḻipaṭṭu uyarniliyuṟṟa katiyic civa naṉmakkaḷ aṟivar. civaliṅka vaṭiviṉ uṟpattiyiyum ataṉ tattuvanuṭpattiyum aṟiyāta paḻaṅkāla āriyarkaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ, pālūṭṭum koṅkiyik kūṭa civaliṅkam eṉṟu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. ipperuntavaṟṟi vaṭamoḻippulavaṉ kāḷitācaṉ kūṭac ceytuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu nām aṟintuḷḷōm. intiya nāṭṭu urimik kaviñar pāratiyār, koṅkikaḷē civaliṅkameṉṟu kūṟik kōkkaviñaṉ kāḷi tācaṉum pūjittāṉ eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār1. itaṟkuc civa camayattil ātaravu etuvum kāṇappaṭavilli.
caṅka kāla vaḻipāṭu
caṅka kālat toṭakkattil cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyta civaliṅka uruva vaḻipāṭu, tāytantit teyva uruva vaḻipāṭākat tuḷirttu viṭṭatu eṉṟu cila aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. caṅka nūṟkaḷ ātiyil taṟi vaṇakkamum, kantu vaṇakkamum naṭipeṟṟaṉa eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil ūrtōṟum potu il amittu atil teyvam uṟiyum taṟiyākiya tūṇ nāṭṭappaṭṭu ālamuṟṟattil vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. intap potu il ampalam eṉṟu kūṟappeṟṟatu. ālamuṟṟam eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭuvatum uṇṭu. maruta nilattuc civa makaḷir nāṭōṟum atikāliyil tuyilviṭṭeḻuntu  puṉita nīril mūḻki uṭaṟṟūymi ceytu, uḷḷat tūymi yuṭiyōrāy ālamuṟṟatti aṇukuvar. ampalatti meḻukuvar, kōlantīṭṭuvar; malar paṟittu vantu tiruuruviṟku tamiḻil arccaṉipurivar. ampalattil āṭippāṭi ammi appari vaḻipaṭuvar. iṟivaṉi maṅkipaṅkaṉ, pākam piriyāḷ, mātorupākaṉ, civacakti eṉṟellām pukaḻntu vaḻipaṭuvar. antiyil ampalattil nantā viḷakkēṟṟi naṟumpuki yūṭṭi, naṉmalar tūvi iṟivaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟuvar. āṇkaḷilum, peṇkaḷ iṟivaḻipāṭṭil atika akkari koṇṭiruntaṉar. paḻantamiḻ ēṭāṉa paṭṭiṉappāli itaṉip paṭampiṭittu eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu pōl iṉṟum eṭuttukkāṭṭi niṟkiṉṟatu.
koṇṭimakaḷir uṇṭuṟi mūḻki
anti māṭṭiya nantā viḷakkiṉ
malaraṇi meḻukkam ēṟip palar toḻa
vampalar cēkkuṅ kantuṭip poḻil	(paṭṭiṉappāli) 
eṉṟu kūṟum paṭṭiṉappāliyiṉ paḻampāṭal paṇṭiya civa makaḷiriṉ vāḻkki varalāṟṟiṟku nērāṉa eṭuttukkāṭṭāka miḷirkiṉṟatu.
ālamuṟṟamum kantu vaḻipāṭum taṟivaḻipāṭum, tamiḻarkaḷ tirukkōyil vaḻipāṭṭiṟkum, tiruuruva vaḻipāṭṭiṟkum civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṟkum aṭippaṭiyāy amintuḷḷaṉa.
varalāṟṟil civaliṅkam
