civaṉ tamiḻmakkaḷiṉ paḻamperum teyvam. civaliṅkam tamiḻarkaḷ ammiyappar vaḻipāṭṭiṟkāka ātikālattil kaṇṭa puṉitac ciṉṉam. tattuvap poruḷ niṟinta iṟi uruvam. āṉāl civaṉ eṉṟa colli iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ evarum uccarittatākat teriyavilliyi; caṅka maruviya taṅka ēṭukaḷil etilum civaṉ peyar kāṇappaṭavilliyē; tamiḻnāṭṭil iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya civaṉ uruvamō civaliṅka uruvamō kāṇappaṭavilliyē, eṉṟu cilar kēṭkiṉṟaṉar. itu nalla kēḷvitāṉ. viṭi iṟukkappaṭa vēṇṭiya kēḷvi eṉpatilum evarukkum karuttu vēṟṟumi eḻamuṭiyātu.
tamiḻnāṭu paḻamperum nāṭu; atil vāḻum tamiḻarkaḷ toṉmiyāṉa kuṭimakkaḷ eṉpatilum palarkku uṭaṉpāṭu uṇṭu. āṉāl itaṟku ituvari nammāl varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷ kāṭṭa muṭiyātiruntu vantatu. āṉāl cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu intiya varalāṟu ki.mu. 4000ām āṇṭiṟku muṉṉiruntu toṭaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟa uṇmiyi uṟutippaṭuttiyuḷḷatu. attuṭaṉ aṅku naṭipeṟṟa akaḻ āyvil kiṭitta eṇṇaṟṟa civaliṅkaṅkaḷum, civaṉ uruvamum, cakti vaṭivamum cintuveḷip paṇpāṭu tamiḻar kaḷiṉ muṉṉōr kaṇṭa paṇpāṭu; civa neṟivaḷartta paṇpāṭu eṉpatiyum uṟutippaṭuttiyuḷḷatu. aṅkuc civaneṟiyi uruvākkiya civa vēḷāṇkuṭimakkaḷ āṭci nilavi iruntatu. ki.mu. iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ arumpiya āriya ākkiramippāl cintu veḷiyil civar āṭci aḻikkappaṭṭatu. atu varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟātu maṟikkappaṭṭatu. cintuveḷi akaḻ āyviṟkut talimi tāṅki naṭattiya intiya miya araciṉ akaḻ āyvuttuṟi iyakkunar taḷapati (Director General of Archeology) car.cāṇ mārcal eḻutiya mokañcatārōvum cintuveḷi nākarikamum eṉṟa nūlil, cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷāl ki.mu. 2000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ kaṭṭi eḻuppap peṟṟa ulakilē oppaṟṟa nākarikamāy miḷirkiṟatu; atu tirāviṭa nākarikam eṉpataṟku iṉṟu tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ tirukkōyilkaḷiṉ tiruuṇṇāḻiki yil vittu vaḻipaṭṭu varum civaliṅkaṅkaḷum civaṉ uruvamum tāyt teyva uruvamum ērāḷamākac cintu veḷiyil kiṭit tiruppatē evarum maṟukka muṭiyāta cāṉṟu kaḷāy uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.1 mokañcatārō akaḻ āyviṉi muṉṉiṉṟu naṭattiya vaṅkam tanta taṅkap pērāciriyar tirunelvēli māvaṭṭattil uḷḷa ātitta nallūr akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭa tamiḻarkaḷiṉ irumpu nākarikam arappā nākarikattiṟku aṭippaṭiyāy iruntatu. cumēriya nākarikam ekiptiya nākarikam pōṉṟavikaḷukku mūlakāraṇamāy iruntatu eṉṟu uṟutiyāy eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār.2  ivi iṉṟu ulakap pēraṟiñarkaḷ palarālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.3
cintuveḷi akaḻ āyvu tamiḻ makkaḷukku viḻippuṇarcciyi yūṭṭiyatu. ātitta nallūr akaḻāyvi ārāyntu, atu iṟṟikkup pattāyiram āṇṭu paḻami uṭiyatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟum uṇmiyi aṟiyac ceytatu.1 vaṭamoḻiyil vāṉmīki eḻutiya irāmāyaṇattip payiṉṟu - irāvaṇaṉ civapaktaṉ eṉṟum avaṉ aṉpuneṟi niṉṟu civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceyyum tūneṟiyāḷaṉ eṉṟum avaṉ cellum iṭaṅkaḷuk kellām poṉṉāl ceyta civaliṅkattip pūcip peṭṭiyil vittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ eṉṟum civaṉik kaṭavuḷāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷāta irāmaṉ, irāvaṇaṉik koṉṟa pāvattiṟkāka irāmēcuvarattil civaliṅkatti nilināṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭāṉ eṉṟum irāmaṉ vaḻipaṭṭa civaliṅkam irāmaliṅkam eṉṟum, avaṉ vaḻipaṭṭa iṭam irāmēcuvaram eṉṟum  kūṟappaṭuvatu2 civaṉ caṅkakālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa teyvam eṉpataṟku nalla cāṉṟāka uḷḷatu. caṅka kālattil aṭuttaṭuttu eḻunta kaṭaṟkōḷ, tamiḻnāṭṭiyum tamiḻ makkaḷiṟ palariyum tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum tiṉṟu ēppam viṭṭu viṭṭatāl civaṉ peyar kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa paḻiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kiṭikka ētuvillāmal pōy viṭṭatu. cintu veḷiyip pōl maṇmūṭip pōṉa tamiḻar nakaram etuvum ituvari kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭu akaḻ āyvu ceyyappaṭavilli eṉpatiyum nām maṟantu viṭa muṭiyātu. iṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷa caṅka nūṟkaḷil civaṉ eṉṟa peyar kāṇap peṟāviṭṭālum civaṉuṭiya pala peyarkaḷ caṅka nūṟkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpati ārāya vēṇṭum. periyōṉ kōyil, kaṇṇutaṟkaṭavuḷ, mukkaṇṇaṉ, makēntiramalivācaṉ, kāriyuṇṭik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟa pala pataṅkaḷ caṅka nūṟkaḷil iṭam peṟṟiruppatu namatu cantēkaṅkaḷi akaṟṟuvaṉavāy uḷḷaṉa.

civaliṅka vaḻipāṭum ataṉ tattuvamum
cemporuḷ
civaliṅkam eṉpatu civam eṉṟa cemporuḷiṉ ciṉṉam eṉṟu poruḷ tarum. iti virivāka viḷakkik kūṟuvatāṉāl civacakti eṉṉum ammi appar taṉmiyik kāṭṭum kuṟiyākum. iṟiyiṉ aṉpiyum aruḷiyum iṇittuk kāṭṭum tattuvap poruḷ niṟinta inta uruvattit tamiḻarkaḷ taṅkaḷ kōyiliṉ tiruuṇṇāḻikiyil naṭuvil vittu toṉṟu toṭṭu iṉṟu vari vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṟārkaḷ.
cintu veḷiyil untiya oppaṟṟa uyariya paṇpāṭu, ki.mu. 4000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ nilaviya viyattaku paṇpāṭu; varalāṟṟil iṭam peṟāta ariya perum nākarikam eṉṟu iṉṟu ulakam uṇarntuḷḷatu. iṟṟikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ cintuveḷi nantāp perumi vāynta naṉi ciṟanta nāṭāka viḷaṅkiyatu eṉṟu 1920ām āṇṭiṟku muṉ ulakil oruvarum uṇarār. cintu veḷiyil, ciṟappu mikka talinakarkaḷāy viḷaṅkiya arappāvum mokañcatarōvum viṇmuṭṭum vīṭukaḷum aḻakiya pala māḷikikaḷum kaviṉuṟu neṟkaḷañciyaṅkaḷum nīr niṟinta peruntaṭākaṅkaḷum (Great Bath) - paṇṭik kālattil vāḻnta tamiḻaṉ kaṭṭiyirukka muṭiyum eṉṟu evaṉum eṇṇip pārttatē illi. enta varalāṟṟāciriyaṉum kaṉavu kūṭak kaṇṭatilli.
āṉāl cintu veḷiyil kiṭitta civaṉ, kāḷi pōṉṟa teyva uruvaṅkaḷum āṭavallāṉ pōṉṟa kālit tūkki niṟkum ampalavāṇaṉiṉ aruḷ uruvamum timil perutta kāḷi uruvamum (nanti vaṭivamum) kuṟippāka palvēṟu pāṅkil paṭitta civaliṅka uruvamum cintu veḷi nākarikam tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōṭikaḷāṉa tamiḻar nākarikam eṉpati uṇarvataṟku - uṟutiyāka urippataṟku uyariya cāṉṟāka iruntaṉa. car. cāṇmārcal, tamiḻarkaḷ toṉṟu toṭṭu iṉṟu vari tam tirukkōyil aṉittilum tiruuṇṇāḻikiyil civaliṅkamāka vittu  vaḻipaṭṭu varuvatu, cintuveḷiyil kāṇappaṭum. tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷ uruvākki vaḻipaṭṭa uruvamē eṉpatil eṉakku eḷḷaḷavum aiyamilli; evarkkum aiyameḻa, enta vitamāṉa ētukkaḷum illi. mēlum iti evarum eḷitil poyppaṭutti viṭavō māṟṟi viṭavō muṭiyātu, eṉṟu kaṇṭār. āriyarkaḷ nālāyiram āṇṭiṟku muṉ paṭiyeṭuttu vantu cintuveḷi tirāviṭa āṭciyi vīḻtti nāṭu, nakaram, vīṭu, kāṭu, kōṭṭi, kottaḷam, kōyil, kuḷam, aṉittiyum aḻittu makkaḷi viraṭṭiṉar. avarkaḷik koṉṟu nāyum nariyum, kaḻukum kākamum tiṉṟu kaḷikkac ceytu nāṭṭic cuṭukāṭākki viṭṭaṉar. illi iṭukāṭākki viṭṭaṉar. tirāviṭarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷ irupatu aṭi āḻattiṟkum - ataṟku appālum putiyuṇṭu pōkumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭaṉar. iccampavam varalāṟṟilum iṭam peṟātu irukkumpaṭi ceytu viṭṭaṉar. eṉṉē, āriyar koṭumi! eṉṉē avaratu vañcakam!
oru tamiḻppulavaṉ tamiḻiṉ perumiyik kūṟa eḻunta poḻutu aṭiyil varumāṟu kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. ē! teyvamē civaperumāṉē ! tamiḻiṉ perumiyi ulakil yār aṟivārkaḷ eṉṟu cilar viṉavukiṉṟārkaḷ. uṉṉuṭiya mūtūrākiya maturiyiṉ kaṇ, tamiḻ ēṭṭi nīril pōṭṭum, neruppiliṭṭum avikaḷāl aḻikka muṭiyāta valimiyuṭiyatu tamiḻmoḻi eṉṟu meyppikkappaṭṭu viṭṭatu. āṉāl nilattiṟkuḷ putittu vittālum aḻikka muṭiyāta valimivāyntatu tamiḻ eṉṟu meyppikkappaṭavilli. nī niṉittāl tamiḻi nilamum viḻuṅka muṭiyātu eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷāṉ. avvaṭikaḷ piṉvarumāṟākum:
pāraṟiyum aṟiviṉum ippārmēl niṉṟa pacuñāṉattālum					eṭṭippārkkoṇāta
pēraṟivē ciṟṟaṟivōrk kiruḷi nīkkum pēroḷiyē 					aṅkayaṟkaṇ piriyātōṉē
āraṟivār tamiḻaṟivār tamiḻarumi ? eṉkiṉṟār niṉmaturi 				mūtūr
nīraṟiyum, neruppaṟiyum, nīyaṟivit tālaṟiyum nilamuntāṉē
ippulavaṉ pāṭiya pāṭṭi iṟivaṉ ceviyuṟṟuc cintu veḷiyil 4000ām āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ āriya ātikka veṟiyar kaḷāl putikkappaṭṭa tamiḻarkaṇṭa ōviya eḻuttukkaḷum, maturiyil kuṭi koṇṭa maṅki paṅkaṉākiya anta  iṟivaṉiṉ tiruuruvamākiya civaṉ cili uruvaṅkaḷum civaliṅkaṅkaḷum  mīṇṭum uyarē vantu ulakam kaṇṭu viyappuṟumāṟu ceytuḷḷāṉ, eṉṟu ulakat tamiḻ ārāyccik karuttaraṅkil āttikarum nāttikarum veḷḷiyarkaḷum, karupparkaḷum, ēṉ mañcaḷ niṟa maṅkōliyarkaḷum, cenniṟac civappu intiyarkaḷum, piṟa niṟamakkaḷum inta aṟputac ceyalip pārāṭṭi, cīrāṭṭiṉar. ēṉ? āriyarkaḷum, cīriyarkaḷum, nāriyarkaḷum, kōriyarkaḷum,  viyappuṟum vaṇṇam tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ ēṟṟamum civaliṅkat tōṟṟamum  ulakam etirpārātu nilattiṉ aṭiyiṉiṉṟu veḷiyē eṭukkappaṭṭu mīṇṭum pōṟṟappaṭum nili tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu niṉintu niṉintu iṉpuṟattakkatu. 
varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa vaḻipāṭu
iliṅka vaḻipāṭu mikat toṉmiyāṉa kālattil tōṉṟiya vaḻipāṭu. kuṟivaḻipāṭu, tamiḻnāṭṭil tōṉṟiya ammi appar vaḻipāṭṭiṉ ārampanili. kal ūḻiyil ciṟpak kali vaḷarcci yaṭiyāta kālattil tāyiṉ ciṉṉamāka oru vaṭṭa vaṭivamum (O) tantiyiṉ ciṉṉamāka oru ciṟukōṭum (|) tīṭṭi vantaṉar. appāl ikkuṟiyīṭukaḷ vaḷarcci peṟṟu vaṭṭattiṉ naṭuvil kōṭu tīṭṭappaṭṭu (   ) antac ciṉṉam piṉṉar (   ) vaḷarcci peṟṟu acciṉṉattiṉ aṭiyil oru mukkōṇam pīṭam pōl iṇikkap peṟṟu iṉṟiya civaliṅka vaṭivāka māṟiyirukkalām( ). ikkuṟiyi arapiyar alip eṉṟa tam eḻuttiṉ mutal varivaṭivāka 1 eṉṟum kiṟittavarkaḷ oru kōṭṭiṉ mītu maṟṟoru kōṭṭi porutti ciluvi eṉṉum oru kuṟiyiyum  cumēriyar intac ciluvik kuṟikkuc cila kālkaḷ iṇittuc cuvattikam ± pōlum teyva ciṉṉaṅkaḷ amittup pōṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. ivikaḷ tamiḻar kaṇṭa civaliṅka vaṭiviṉiṉṟu (  ) atika vēṟṟumi yuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭa muṭiyātu.1
civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu cīṉā, cappāṉ, ayarlāntu, piriṭṭaṉ, amerikkā, āppirikkā, aciriyā, cīriyā, yūtāyā, ciṉṉa āciyā (Asia Minor) papilōṉiya mutaliya nāṭukaḷil vāḻnta paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷiṭiyēyum paravi iruntatu eṉṟu kiṟittava maṟinūlāṉa paḻiya ēṟpāṭu cāṉṟu pakarkiṉṟatu.2 iṉṟu papilōṉ nāṭṭil pala civaliṅkaṅkaḷ akaḻnteṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. hōra carāpi ira pōṉṟa peyarkaḷāl ekiptil iliṅka vaḻipāṭu, nilavi yiruntatu. iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭuṭaṉ pulikaḷum, pāmpukaḷum, vaḻipaṭappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptiya camātic cuvarkaḷil civaliṅkam aḻakiya vaṭivil tīṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. airōppāvil iliṅka vaḻipāṭu paravi iruntatu. kiṟittavak kurumārkaḷ ati arumpāṭupaṭṭu maṟittu vantuḷḷaṉar. iṅkilāntilum, kāṭlāṇṭilum civaliṅka uruvaṅkaḷ pala akaḻ āyvil kaṇṭupiṭikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. urōmar nāṭṭil paravi irunta iliṅka vaḻipāṭu airōppāviṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷilum paravi irukkak kūṭum eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.1
cermaṉi, pirāṉcu, mekcikkō peru, kiṭṭi tīvu (Hijti) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum amerikkāviṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷilum iliṅka vaḻipāṭu nilaviyatu. mutaṉ mutalāka amerikkāvil peyṉ nāṭṭār kāl vittatum aṅku eṅkum kōyilkaḷil civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu paravi iruppatik kaṇṭaṉar. āppirikkāvil takōmi (Dahomi) eṉṉum iṭattil iliṅkam leṅpā (Lengba) eṉṉum peyaril vaḻipaṭappaṭṭu vantatu. iliṅka vaḻipāṭu acīriyāvil arumpi ataṉ aṇmiyiluḷḷa ayalnāṭukaḷil paravi irukkalām eṉṟu pala mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉ ṟārkaḷ. kirēkka nāṭṭil pakka (Bacchus) ṭayōṉica (Dionysus) eṉṉum peyarāl civaliṅkam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kirīcu nāṭṭil pakka viḻāvil civaliṅkam capparattil vittu nakar valam vantatu uṇṭu. ciṉṉa āciyāvil iliṅkam kemō (Khemos) mōlōc (Moloch) martūk (Merodock) ātōṉiyi (Adonis) capāciya (Sabazius) pakka pākēo (bagios) eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻipaṭap paṭṭu vantataṟku ilakkiya cāṉṟukaḷum akaḻ āyvuc cāṉṟukaḷum kāṭṭa muṭiyum. āppirikkāvil uḷḷa ṟoṭēciyāvilum ciriyāvilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭa civaliṅkaṅkaḷil pūmālikaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉiciyar tayar nakaril pakalavaṉ kōyilil marakatattiṉālum poṉṉālum ceyta iliṅkaṅkaḷi vittu vaḻipaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉar. kaṭavuḷ āṇ, peṇ iyinta vaṭiviṉaṉ eṉpataṉ peyarilē pipiḷil kūṟum elōkim eṉṉum teyvam vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. pipiḷ eṉṉum kiṟittava maṟinūliṉ paḻiya ēṟpāṭṭil tūṇ vaḻipāṭu naṭipeṟṟatu eṉpatu naṉku terikiṟatu. tūṇiṟku eṇṇey apicēkam ceyyap paṭṭu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu naṉku eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.2
paṇṭik kālantoṭṭup pār muḻuvatum paraviya iliṅka vaḻipāṭu, cila mutirāp paṇpuṭiya paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ uyirkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattiṟku mūlakāraṇamāka irukkum āṇ peṇ kuṟiyākac civaliṅkatti eṇṇi vaḻipaṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟiṉāl atik kaṇṭu nām viyappaṭivataṟkilli.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ viḷakkam
tamiḻmaṟi pāṭum iṟi vaḻipāṭu  -  civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu tirāviṭa nāṭṭilum, tirāviṭa makkaḷ kuṭiyēṟiya miya nilakkaṭaṟ pakutikaḷiluḷḷa paḻam perum nāṭukaḷilum, āppirikkā, amerikkā, airōppā pōṉṟa nāṭukaḷilum cīrpeṟa nilavi iruntatu eṉpataṟkup pala mēṉāṭṭaṟiñarkaḷ mutal taramāṉa cāṉṟu kāṭṭi iliṅka vaḻipāṭu oru kālattil ulakam muḻuvatum paravi iruntatu eṉṟa uṇmiyi nilināṭṭiyuḷḷaṉar. ellāvaṟṟiṟkum mēlāka, āriyarkaḷ muṟkālattil civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭi iḻitturittu vantaṉar eṉṟālum iṉṟu tamiḻnāṭṭup pārppaṉarkaḷ tirāviṭarkaḷiṉ tirukkōvilukku vantu civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ceytu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. vaṭamoḻi nūṟkaḷ palavaṟṟil civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu āriyarka ḷiṭiyē nilava ākkam aḷittavaṉ, ūkkam ūṭṭiyavaṉ irāmaṉ āvāṉ. appāl iliṅka vaḻipāṭu āriyar uvappuṭaṉ ēṟka naṉku aṭippaṭiyiṭṭavar āti caṅkarar eṉappaṭupavar āvār.
civa camaya tattuva cāttiramākap pōṟṟappaṭum civañāṉa cittiyār,
civaṉaru uruvumallaṉ, cittiṉō ṭacittumallaṉ
pavamutaṟ ṟoḻilkaḷoṉṟum paṇṇiṭuvāṉu mallaṉ
tavamutal yōkapōkam tarippava ṉallaṉṟāṉē
ivipeṟa iyintu moṉṟum iyintiṭā iyal piṉāṉē
eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu. civaperumāṉiṉ tiruuruviṟku uṇmiyāṉa viḷakkam aḷikkiṟatu. avaṉ evvita uruvamum uṭiyavaṉ allaṉ cittum acittum allaṉ eṉṟellām uyariya viḷakkam tarukiṟatu. avaṉ ēkaṉākavum ellāmākavum, eṅkum niṟintum, arūpiyākavum, niṉmalaṉākavum, ātiyum antamum aṟṟava ṉākavum viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. mutal, naṭu, īṟu, ākiya nilikaḷit taṉṉuḷ koṇṭavaṉ. aṉittum avaṉiṭamiruntu arumpi, avaṉiṭamē oṭuṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu civañāṉa cittiyārum, civañāṉa pōtamum ulakiṟku aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. (ivikaḷ civa camaya tattuvañāṉaṅkaḷi viḷakkum atikārap pūrvamāṉa ēṭukaḷākum)
iliṅka purāṇam, iliṅkamē aṉittiṟkum aṭippaṭi yāy, ātāramāy, mūlamāy mutaṉmiyāyt tikaḻkiṟatu; oli, uṇarvu, tōṟṟam, cuvi, maṇam eṉṟa pañca taṉmāttiri kaḷaṟṟatāy pirakirutiyāy (pārvatiyāy) viḷaṅkukiṟatu eṉṟu uṭporuḷ viḷakkam urikkiṉṟatu.
tamiḻil ākamam aruḷiya tirumūlar ākamamākiya tiru mantirattil civaliṅkattiṟku oru ariya viḷakkam aḷittuḷḷār. atu aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
malarnta ayaṉmāl uruttiraṉ makēcaṉ
palantarum aimmukaṉ paravintu nātam
nalantaruñ cakti civaṉvaṭi vākip
palantaru liṅkam parānanti yāmē	(tirumantiram - 1745)
itaṉ poruḷ, ayaṉ māl uruttiraṉ, āṇṭāṉ, aruḷōṉākiya aimmukaṉ ākiyavarkaḷum mēlāya vintu nātaṅkaḷum, naṉmi nalkum cakti civaṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷum ākip palaṉ tarum iliṅkamē parānanti ākum.
teyvac cēkkiḻār tiruttoṇṭar mākkatiyil ñāyiṟṟiṉ oppaṟṟa oḷiyiyum, veppattiyum, viyāpakat taṉmiyiyum nam kaṇṇāl muḻumiyāka uṇara muṭiyātu. uṟṟu nōkkak kaṇ kūcum. maṉattālum cintikka muṭiyāta niliyil irukkiṉṟōm. oru pūtak kaṇṇāṭiyiṉ mūlam katiravaṉ oḷiyi pañciṉ mītu paṭac ceytāl atu tīppaṟṟi erivatiyum atiṉiṉṟu ñāyiṟṟiṉ taṉmiyiyum iyaṟki iyalpiyum kāṇa muṭiyum. atē pōṉṟu aṟivum āṟṟalum āṉmīka aṉupavamum peṟṟu viḷaṅkum aṉupūtic cemmalkaḷ āṇṭavaṉiṭamiruntē aṉittum arumpi avaṉiṭamē ākirtiyil (iṟutiyil) ilayap paṭum kāraṇattāl civaperumāṉukku oru taṉippaṭṭa tiruuruvattik kaṟpaṉi ceyyāmal iliṅkākāra vaṭivattil amittu vaḻipaṭap peṟukiṟatu. civaliṅkam tirumālum nāṉ mukaṉum kāṇa muṭiyāta uruviṉaṉāy aḻaṟ piḻampāy uḷāṉ eṉṟu viḷakkiyuḷḷār.
kāṇāta varuviṉukku muruviṉukkuṅ kāraṇamāy
nīṇāka maṇintārkku nikarkuṟiyām civaliṅkam
nāṇātu nēṭiyamāl nāṉmukaṉum kāṇanaṭuc
cēṇārun taḻaṟ piḻampāyt tōṉṟiyatu teḷintārāy 					(tirut. māk 24. vār 8.)
eṉṟu civaliṅkam tēvarkaḷāl kūṭa ataṉ aṭiyum muṭiyum kāṇamuṭiyāta aḻaṟpiḻampu eṉṟu cēkkiḻār tiruttoṇṭar mākkatiyil viritturippatu cintikkat takkatu.
civaliṅkam eṉṟa collē nalla poruḷ niṟinta uyariya patamākum. civa eṉṟa collukku tukkātikaḷip pōkkuvatu eṉṟum iliṅkam eṉpataṟku kuṟi eṉṟum poruḷ koḷḷap paṭum. eṉavē civaliṅkam eṉṟāl tukkātikaḷi akaṟṟum maṅkalakaramāṉa nalantarum naṟkuṟiyākak koḷḷappaṭum. mēlum civa eṉṟa patattil ci kaṭavuḷiyum va aruḷ uruvāṉa caktiyiyum kuṟikkum. cāṉṟōrkaḷ civaliṅkam eṉpatu caktiyum civaṉum iṇintu piṇintuḷḷatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. iliṅkam eṉṟa collukkut tōṉṟi oṭuṅkal eṉṟu camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ viḷakkam aḷikkiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē ulakam uṟpattiyāvataṟkum oṭuṅkuvataṟkum kāraṇamāṉa cemporuḷik kuṟikkum tiruuruvam iliṅkamākum.
mēlum iliṅkam eṉṟa patam liki eṉṟa tātuviṉ aṭippaṭiyiliruntu piṟantatu eṉṟu moḻi iyal valluṉarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, itaṟkuc cittarittal, aṇi ceytal eṉṟu poruḷ tarutal poruntum. iṟivaṉ ulakip paṭittum kāttum aḻittum varuvatu oru ciṟu tiruviḷiyāṭṭākac cila aṟiñarkaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar.
civaliṅkattiṟku aṭi, muṭi, naṭu eṉṟu mūṉṟa pākaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē piramma pākam, uruttirapākam eṉṟu āriya camaya paktarkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. uruttirapākam eṉpatu tāṉ civapākam. ēṉiya irupākaṅkaḷum caktipākam eṉṟum kūṟuvar. ivviraṇṭu pākaṅkaḷum āvuṭiyār eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itu caṅkarar vaḻiyil vaḻipaṭṭu niṟkum civaṉ aṭiyārkaḷ kūṟum kūṟṟākum.
civaliṅkam civākama vitikaḷiṉ āṇippaṭi tirukkōvil kōpurattiṟku appāl koṭimaram, palipīṭam, nanti, civaliṅkam eṉṟa oru oḻuṅku - niyatiyi aṉucarittu amikkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟatu. tiru uṇṇāḻikiyil tīpa oḷiyil civaliṅkattik kaṇṭutericitta oru paktaṉ, tericitta māttirattil iṟivaṉ viṇṇum maṇṇum akaṇṭākaramāy viyāpittirukkiṟāṉ; pirapañcam aṉittum iliṅkamayamāy uḷḷatu eṉṟa uṇarcci avaṉ uḷḷattil unti eḻumpak kūṭum. atōṭu civaliṅkattiṭam vivarikka muṭiyāta apūrva cakti nilavac ceykiṟatu; uḷḷatti eḷitil layikkac ceytu uḷḷatti orumukappaṭutti ēkāk kiracittam eḻumāṟum ceykiṟatu. iliṅka vaṭivip paktaṉ kaṇṭutericitta māttirattil uḷḷam paṭippaṭiyāka iḷaki cintaṉiyaṟṟuk kuvintu cānti arumpukiṟatu. ataṉālaṉṟō aṟivarkaḷ tirukkōyilil civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭi ēṟpaṭutti yuḷḷaṉar eṉṟa uṇmi pulaṉākiṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iliṅka vaḻipāṭu
ellā vaḻipāṭṭilum iliṅka vaḻipāṭu ēṟṟam peṟṟatu. iliṅka vaḻipāṭu iṟivaḻipāṭṭiṟku aṭippaṭiyāy amin tuḷḷatu. iṟivaṉi iliṅka vaṭivil vaḻipaṭupavarkaḷ eḷitil iṟi uṇarvip peṟṟu iṉpa niliyi eytuvar. tiruvaruḷi aṭivar eṉṟu civa camayac cāṉṟōrkaḷ naviṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ.
iliṅka vakikaḷ
iliṅkam pala vakippaṭum. avikaḷ aṉparkaḷ aṟiviṟkum paktikkum, iṭaṅkaḷukkum, nāṭukaḷukkum ēṟpap pala vitamāka amintuḷḷaṉa.
ēkaliṅkam
paṇṭu toṭṭu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟuvarum niliyāṉa kuṟiyē tāvara liṅkam ākum. itu oṉṟē aṉittu iliṅka vaṭivilum mēlāṉatu. ivikaḷē tirukkōyilkaḷil tiruuṇṇāḻikiyil iṭam peṟṟiruppaṉavākum. civaliṅkam eṉṟu aṉpoḻukap paktarkaḷālum cāṉṟōrkaḷālum ēṟṟappaṭuvatu : itu ēka liṅkam. iliṅkattilē mukamum tōḷkaḷum veḷippaṭṭirukkum taṭciṇā mūrtti uḷpaṭa aṭṭamūrtti vaṭivāṉa irupattintu makēcuvara vaṭivaṅkaḷum piṟavum viyaktaliṅkaṅkaḷēyām.
iruvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷ
uṇṇāḻikiyil uṟintirukkum uṭiyār uruvatti  -mūlavar vaṭivip pārtta iliṅkam (tāvara liṅkam) eṉṟum aṭiyārkaḷ illattil vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭuvatu āṉmārtta liṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. iḵtaṉṟi paktarkaḷ itayattil vittu vaḻipaṭappaṭuvatu caṅkaramaliṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
mupperum iliṅkaṅkaḷ
tirukkōyiliṉ uṇṇāḻikiyiṉ mēl eḻuppap peṟṟirukkum iṟivaṉiṉ makuṭampōl viḷaṅkum vimāṉam tūya liṅkam eṉṟum tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ (karuvaṟiyiṉ) uṭpuṟam kāṇappaṭum iliṅkam cūṭcamaliṅkam eṉṟum ataṟ kaṇmiyil etirāka iṭam peṟṟirukkum palipīṭam pattiraliṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum. ivi muṟiyē viyakta liṅkam, avviyakta liṅkam, viyaktā viyakta liṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻākamam ākiya tirumantirattil, tirumūlar,
tūya vimāṉamum tūlama tākumā
lāya catāciva mākunaṟ cūkkumam
pāyapalipīṭam pattira liṅkamā
māya varaṉili yāyntu koḷvārkaṭkē	(tirumantiram. 1689)
eṉṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkam tantuḷḷār.
nālvaki liṅkaṅkaḷ
civaliṅkam (1) acalaliṅkam (2) calaṉaliṅkam (3) calācalaliṅkam (4) acalacalaliṅkam eṉa nāṉku vakippaṭum. vimāṉam acalaliṅkam eṉavum itayattiṟ kaṟpikkappaṭum civaliṅkam calaṉaliṅkam eṉavum varṇaliṅkam irattiṉaliṅkam mutaliyaṉa calācala liṅkam eṉavum vētikiyil maṇaṭalamiṭṭup pūcikkappaṭiṉ atu acalacalaliṅkam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
pañcapūta liṅkaṅkaḷ
mikat toṉmiyum naṉmiyum vāynta aintu pūta vaṭivāṉa iṟivaḻipāṭu tamiḻakattil uṇṭu. ivi tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ aintu puṉita nakarkaḷil naṭipeṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. avi aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
1. tiruvārūr allatu kāñciyil appuliṅkam (maṇvaṭivāṉa iliṅkam)
2. tiruvaṇṇāmaliyil tēyuliṅkam (neruppu vaṭivāṉa iliṅkam)
3. tiruvāṉikkāvil appuliṅkam (nīrvaṭivāṉa iliṅkam)
4. tirukkāḷattiyil vāyuvu liṅkam (kāṟṟu vaṭivāṉa iliṅkam)
5. tiruccitamparam pāṇa liṅkam (veḷivaṭivāṉa iliṅkam)
ivi civa camaya ñāṉikaḷāl pañca pūta talaṅkaḷ eṉṟu ciṟappittuk kūṟappaṭum.
aṟuvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷ
āriṭam, irākkatam, ācuram, tēvatam, māṉiṭam, cuyampu eṉṟu aṟuvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷ uḷa eṉṟu ciṟpanūṟkaḷ ciṟappittuk kūṟum.
1. muṉivarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu āriṭaliṅkam. 2. acurar kaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu irākkataliṅkam. 3. nāṉmukaṉ (piramaṉ) intiraṉ pōṉṟa tēvarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu ācuraliṅkam. 
4. kāñciyil kāmāṭci eṉṉum tēviyāl niṟuvap peṟṟu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu tēvataliṅkam. 5. maṇṇulakil makkaḷāl niṟuvappeṟṟu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟatu māṉiṭaliṅkam. 6. kaltaccaṉ uḷi koṇṭu cetukkappaṭātu iyaṟkiyil eḻuntaruḷic ciṟu pāṟi vaṭivilum kuṉṟaṉiya periya vaṭivilum tāṉē tōṉṟi niṟkum iliṅkam cuyampu liṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.
ēḻuvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷ
iliṅka vakikaḷil eḻuvakiliṅkaṅkaḷ ciṟappāṉavi eṉak kūṟappaṭum.
cuyampu liṅkam, tēviliṅkam, tivviya liṅkam, ārca liṅkam, māṉuṣa liṅkam, irāṭcacaliṅkam, ācura liṅkam eṉa eḻu vaki liṅkaṅkaḷ uḷa.
tāṉē tōṉṟiyatu cuyampuliṅkam (itu malikaḷiṉ taṉmiyuṭiyatu); tēviyiṉāl pūcikkappaṭṭatu tēvi liṅkam. tēvarkaḷāl niṟuvappeṟṟatu tivviya liṅkam (teyvīka liṅkam). muṉivarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu ārcaliṅkam, irāṭcatarkaḷāl pūcikkappaṭṭatu irāṭcacaliṅkam. acurarkaḷāl niṟuvappaṭṭatu ācuraliṅkam. pāṇam eṉappaṭum nīrilē uṟpattiyāṉati pāṇaliṅkam eṉpar. īṇṭu pāṇācuraṉāl pūcikkappaṭṭatē pāṇaliṅkam eṉappaṭukiṉṟatu.
upalaṭcaṇiyiṉāl piḷḷiyār, murukaṉ pōṉṟavar kaḷāl niṟuvappeṟṟatu karaṇaliṅkam eṉappaṭum.1
aṣṭaliṅkaṅkaḷ
iliṅka vakikaḷil aṣṭaliṅkam ciṟappumikka iṟivaṉ eṇkuṇattāṉ eṉṟu tamiḻmaṟi nūlkaḷil ciṟappittuk kūṟap paṭukiṉṟāṉ. aṭṭa mūrttiyāṉāy pōṟṟi - tēvāram pōṉṟa tutivētaṅkaḷ iṟivaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟiva ṉuṭiya eṭṭu mūrttaṅkaḷ (aṣṭamūrttaṅkaḷ) civamūrttaṅkaḷil ciṟappāṉavi eṉṟu ēṟṟip pōṟṟi vaḻipaṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nilam, nīr, tī, vaḷi, veḷi, katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ, ātavaṉ (āṉmā) eṉṉum eṇporuḷ vaṭiviṉaṉ civaperumāṉ eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa. iṟivaṉ eṅkum niṟintiruppatu paṟṟi eṇṭiciyum civaṉukku eṇkiyākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉāl nikaṇṭil eṇṭōḷaṉ eṉṟu pukaḻntu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, tī eṉṉum muccuṭarum civaperumāṉukku mukkaṇṇākak kūṟappaṭum. itaṉāl civaperumāṉ mukkaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu aḻikkappaṭukiṟār. iṟivaṉukku mūṉṟāvatu kaṇ neṟṟiyil iruppatāl nutal viḻi nāṭṭattiṟivaṉ eṉṟu pulavarkaḷ pukaḻntu pāṭiyuḷḷaṉar. kaṇṇutaṟ kaṭavuḷum kaḻaka mōṭamarntu eṉṟu maṉōṉmaṇiyam eṉṉum nūlil cuntaram piḷḷi avarkaḷ tamiḻ vaṇakkam pāṭiyuḷḷār. ivaṟṟāl iṟivaṉuṭiya eṇ poruḷ vaṭivil aṣṭa liṅkaṅkaḷ - eṇvaki iliṅkaṅkaḷāka amittu vaḻipaṭuvatu eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
paṉṉiru jōti liṅkaṅkaḷ
1. curāṣṭirattil (pirapā paṭṭaṉ) cōmanātaliṅkam,
2. ciricilattil  mallikārccuṉa liṅkam, 3. uccayiṉiyil avanti makākāṇaliṅkam, 4. ōṅkāram (vintiya ōṅkārēcuvarar - amalēc cuvaraliṅkam, 5. imācalam - pattirikētāra liṅkam, 6. ṭākṉi tikiḷiyūr  - pīmāṅkaraliṅkam, 7. vāraṇāci nācik -  vivēcuraliṅkam, 8. kōtāvari nācik  -  tiriyampaka liṅkam, 9. citāpuram  - vittiyanāta liṅkam, 10 cētu irāmēcuvara  - irāmaliṅkam, 11. tāruvaṉam auṭā-  nākēcuvaraliṅkam, 12. civālayam - kulamēcaliṅkam (ellōrā kukiyiṉ aṇmiyil amintuḷḷatu) eṉa uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kōṭiruttira camhiti kūṟukiṉṟatu. 
paṉṉiru oḷikkuṟikaḷil jōtiliṅkaṅkaḷum vaḻipāṭṭiṟku uyarntaṉa eṉṟu maṟinūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. jōtiliṅkattiṉ tirunāmaṅkaḷi kālip poḻutil eḻuntu, nīrāṭi, uṭaṟṟūymi ceytu, puṉitamāṉa āṭipuṉintu, uḷattūymi peṟṟup pūcikkuriya puṉitap pūkkaḷik koytu, iliṅka uruviṟku arppaṇam ceytu, tēvāram, tirumaṟi kūṟum pākkaḷip pāṭi arccaṉi ceytu, karppūram, cāmpirāṇi mutaliya naṟumaṇap puki kāṭṭi vaḻipaṭṭāl (pū ceypurintāl) uḷḷam puṉitam aṭiyum. cānti nilavum, paḻampāvaṅkaḷum pōm. ellāc cittikaḷum peṟa muṭiyum.
civaṉ ellōrkkum tāyākavum tantiyākavum uḷāṉ. anta uṇmic civaliṅkattiṉ mūlam ulakiṟku uṇarttap peṟṟuḷḷatu. avaṉ ennāṭṭiṉarkkum enniṟattārkkum, eppālārkkum iṟivaṉ. avaṉ aṉpu vaṭivāṉavaṉ. avaṉi añcātu ciṟuvarkaḷum, ciṟumikaḷum aṇukalām. avarkaḷ aṉivariyum aṉpuṭaṉ taḻuvi aravaṇittu aruḷpuriyum aruḷāḷaṉ; karuṇākaraṉ. avaṉi appar aṭikaḷ, ammi yākavum, appaṉākavum, ammāṉākavum attiyākavum uṟaviṉa rākavum kaṇṭār.
appaṉ nī ammi nī aiyaṉum nī
aṉpuṭiya māmaṉum māmiyumnī
oppuṭiya mātarum nī oṇporuḷum nī
oru kulamum cuṟṟamum ōrūrum nī
tuyppaṉavum uyppaṉavum tōṟṟuvāy nī
tuṇiyāyeṉ neñcantuṟappippāy nī
ippoṉ nī immaṇinī, immuttum nī,
iṟivaṉnī ēṟūrnta celvaṉ nīyē
eṉṟu avar pāṭiyuḷḷatu iṟivaṉukkum makkaḷukkum uḷḷa toṭarpiyuṇarttuvatākum. cuntarar iṟivaṉi uṟṟa tōḻaṉākak kaṇṭār.
ēḻiciyāy icippayaṉāy iṉṉamutāy eṉṉuṭiya
tōḻaṉumāy
eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu iṟivaṉuṭaṉ makkaḷ koḷḷa vēṇṭiya nerukkamāṉa aṉpi toṭarpi uṇarttum. mēlum māṇikka vācakar civaperumāṉi,
tāyāy, muliyit taruvāṉē, tārā toḻintāl cavaliyāy
nāyēṉ kaḻintu ...
...
eṉṟu uḷḷam nekiḻntu uṇarvu uruka iṟivaṉit tāyākki nammic cēyākkukiṟār. civaperumāṉum ñāṉacampantarukkut tāyākat tōṉṟi,  pālūṭṭi,  uyirkaḷiṉ mēl vittuḷḷa piḷḷip pācatti yuṇarttiyuḷḷār. campantar civaperumāṉi tāyavaṉ ulakukku eṉṟu uḷḷam urukappāṭi avaṉukku āṭpaṭṭār.
iliṅka vaṭiviṉ ēṟṟam
civaliṅkam tamiḻnāṭṭil arumpi  ciṟcila ciṟiya māṟṟaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ pāraṉittum paraviyuḷḷa oru ariya teyvīkac ciṉṉamākum. ciṟpakkali vaḷarcci peṟuvataṟku muṉ kal ūḻiyil karaṭu muraṭāṉa uruvil tōṉṟi oru nirantaramāṉa amippip peṟṟiruntatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil arumpiya irumpu ūḻiyil malarnta eḵkup paṇpāṭṭil, eḵkiṉāl uḷikaḷ ceyyappaṭavē civaliṅka vaṭivil pala māṟṟaṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ātiyil, aṭiyil vaṭṭavaṭivāṉa amippil āvuṭiyārum ataṉ mēṟpakutiyil vaṭṭavaṭivāṉa tūṇ uruvil civa iliṅkamum amikkappaṭṭaṉa. civaliṅkam tūṇ vaṭivilum āṟupaṭṭikaḷ, eṭṭup paṭṭikaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭatāyp putturuvam peṟṟeḻuntatu. appāl civaliṅkattiṉ mēl uḷḷa nīḷ uruva vaṭivilē palaciṟu iliṅka uruvaṅkaḷ cetukkap peṟṟu palvēṟu peyarkaḷāl vaḻipaṭap paṭṭu vantaṉa. avi aṭiyil varumāṟākum.
palavaki iliṅka pūcikaḷ
puṟappūciyil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum iliṅkaṅkaḷ pala tiṟappaṭum. pacci maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭa iliṅkattiṉum, cuṭṭa maṇṇāl uruvākkappaṭṭa vaṭivam patiṉmaṭaṅku uyarntatu. cuṭṭamaṇ liṅkattiṉum tāruliṅkam patiṉmaṭaṅku mēlāṉatu. tāruliṅkattiṉum, cilaliṅkam paṉmaṭaṅku ciṟantatu. cila liṅkattiṉum tātuliṅkam nūṟumaṭaṅku mēṉmiyāṉatu. ataṉiṉum irattiṉa liṅkam aṉanta maṭaṅku atikac ciṟappuṭiyatu. irattiṉa liṅkattiṉum maṇiliṅkam pallāyiram maṭaṅku ciṟantatu. ataṉiṉum varṇa liṅkam palakōṭi maṭaṅku ciṟantatu. ataṉiṉṟu iraca liṅkam caṅkam aḷavukku  uyarntatu. aṉittiṉum cuyampu liṅkam oppuyarvaṟṟatu.
pāṇācuraṉ oru uḻuntu mutal muḻam īṟāka uḷḷa iliṅkaṅkaḷip peṟṟu mantiraṅ koṇṭu pūcittamiyāl avliṅkam pāṇa liṅkam eṉap peṟṟatu. inta iliṅkaṅkaḷil āvākaṉam vēṇṭātu civacānnittiyam uḷatu eṉṟu nūlkaḷ ōtukiṉṟaṉa.
pāṇācuraṉ mantiramali (mahēntirakiri) nēpāḷam uḷḷaṭakki amarēcuvaram, iliṅkāttiri, kāḷikārttam cirikiri, kaṉṉikācciramam, kaṉṉiyā tīrttam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil pūcittu nalam peṟṟāṉ.
ātiyil nelliyil tōṉṟiya ciṉṉañciṟu civaliṅkam tilliyilum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum kuṉṟeṉak kāṭci tarum civaliṅkam pōṉṟaṉa - cuyampu liṅkam ākum. kacci ēkampattil kāmāṭci ammiyāl pūcikkappaṭṭa ēkāmparanāta makāliṅkam tēviliṅkam eṉavum, tirucceṅkāṭṭaṅkuṭiyil vināyakap perumāṉāl niṟuvappeṟṟu pūcikkap peṟṟa iliṅkam kaṇapatīccura makāliṅkam eṉavum, tiruccēyaññalūril cuppiramaṇiyar uruvam niṟuvi vaḻipaṭṭa cattikirīcuvara makāliṅkam karṇaliṅkam eṉavum nāṉmukaṉāl cīkāḻiyil nilināṭṭi vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu pirama purīmakāliṅkam eṉavum - tirumāṟ pēṟṟil tirumālāl pūcikkap peṟṟa māl vaṇaṅkīcuvara makāliṅkamum, viṣṇu vāmaṉa avatāram purintu tirumāṇikkuḻiyil niṟuvi pūcitta māṇikka varatēcuvara makāliṅkamum tirukkaṇṇār kōvilil intiraṉ tāpittu pūcitta kaṇṇāyirēcuvara makāliṅkamum (teyvīka liṅkamum) tiruk kuṟṟālattil akattiyarāl niṟuvip pūcikkap peṟṟa kuṟumpalāvīcura makāliṅkamum (āriṭaliṅkamum)  - iṭumpāvaṉattil iṭumpaṉāl niṟuvap peṟṟu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟa carkkuṇa nātēcuvara makāliṅkam (irāṭca caliṅkam) tiruōṇa kāntaṉṟaḷiyil ōṇaṉ, kāntaṉ eṉṟum acurarāl pūcikkap peṟṟa ōṇakān tēcuvara makāliṅkam (ācuraliṅkam) ākiyaṉavum varalāṟṟup pukaḻ peṟṟavi.
iliṅkattiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcci
āriyarkaḷ, intiyāvil pukuntapoḻutu tirāviṭarkaḷ vaḻipaṭṭa civaliṅka vaḻipāṭṭiṉ tattuvatti aṟiyātu ati aṟiyum āḻnta tattuva aṟiviṉṟi civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu ciciṉtēvar vaḻipāṭu eṉṟaṉar. appāl paṉṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟu āriyarkaḷ - ciṟappākat tamiḻnāṭṭup pārppaṉar - civaṉi muḻu
mutaṟ kaṭavuḷ eṉpati ēṟṟuk koḷḷātu civaṉ uruttiraṉ eṉṉum mummūrttiyil oruvarē eṉṟu civavaḻipāṭu ceyyum kālattum civaliṅkam āṇ peṇ kuṟi vaḻipāṭu eṉṟu kūṟi vantaṉar. iṉṟu pārppaṉar civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟi varukiṉ ṟaṉar. tirunīṟaṇintum, civaṉaṭiyārkaḷ pōl ceṅkāvi uṭi yaṇintum, tamiḻit tāy moḻiyākap payiṉṟum varukiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē ivarkaḷ iyaṟṟiya nūlip payiṉṟa veḷḷiyar cilar civaliṅka vaḻipāṭu āṇkuṟi peṇkuṟi vaḻipāṭu eṉṟu eḻuti vantaṉar.  āriyarkaḷ cumārtta camayattic cārntavarkaḷ avarkaḷ civaṉip peyaraḷavil teyvamāka  ciṟu teyvamāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭirukkiṟārkaḷēyoḻiya uṇmiyil civaṉi muḻu mutaṟkaṭavuḷāka ēṟṟuk koḷḷavilli; civa tīṭci peṟavilli. pārppaṉarāyiṉum paḷḷarkaḷāyiṉum civatīṭci peṟṟu, civa camaya maṟinūṟkaḷi payiṉṟavarkaḷē anta camayattip paṟṟip pēca arukati uḷḷavarkaḷ eṉpati civa camayac caṉṟōrkaḷ uṟutiyākat terivikkavēṇṭum. āriyarkaḷiṟ palar cumārtta camayattil cērntu pūṇūl iṭṭu kāyattiri mantiram colli civaṉ (uruttiraṉ) piramaṉ, viṣṇu, intiraṉ mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭu varupavarkaḷ eṉṟu uṇartta vēṇṭum. civattiṟkum cumārttattiṟkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭu malikkum maṭuviṟkum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭākum. civam intu matattiṟkuḷ iṇiyātu. iṇittup pēcuvatu tavaṟu. mēlum campantar, māṇikkavācakar pōṉṟa palar civam taḻuvi civarkaḷāl civaṉip pōla pōṟṟappaṭṭu varupavarkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmiyi nām maṟappataṟkō maṟuppataṟkō illi. 
civaliṅkam ātikālattil arumpiya niliyiṉiṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāy vaḷarntu vantuḷḷatu. tamiḻnāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil aṭiyil uṭukki pōṉṟa vaṭivamum ataṉ mītu vaṭṭamāṉa vaṭivil, nīḷ uruṇṭi vaṭivāṉa oru kuṟiyum atic cuṟṟip paḷḷamāṉa kuḻiyum, iliṅkattiṉ mītu nīrviṭṭāl atu veḷiyē cellat takkatāṉa amippē civaliṅkam eṉṟa vaṭivamippāka iruntu vantatu. putiya kal ūḻiyit  toṭarntu  tamiḻnāṭṭil eḻunta irumpu ūḻiyil eḵkiṉāl ceyta kūriya uḷikaḷum ēṉiya kallic cetukkum karuvikaḷ palavum eḻuntatiṉ payaṉāka iliṅkam eṭṭuppaṭṭi vaṭivil ceyyappaṭṭatu. eṭṭup paṭṭi vaṭivam patiṉāṟu paṭṭi vaṭivamāki puttaraliṅkamākip piṉ liṅkam eṉṟa peyarip peṟṟatu. piṉṉar  ciṟu ciṟu  māṟṟaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ iliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷ cetukkappeṟṟu aṣṭōttira liṅkam eṉṟum, cakaraliṅkam eṉṟum kūṟappeṟṟaṉa. ivikaḷ ātiyil maṇṇālum, piṉṉar cuṭumaṇṇālum marattālum, kallālum, poṉṉālum, paṭikat tālum, māṇikkattālum, marakatattālum ceyyap peṟṟaṉa.
appāl irumpu āyutaṅkaḷiṉ perukkattālum makkaḷiṉ aṟivu vaḷarcciyālum, ciṟpikaḷiṉ paṭṭaṟivālum, iliṅka vaṭivu civaperumāṉiṉ mārpiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa mukavaṭivamākac cetukkap paṭṭu orumukaliṅkam (ēkamukaliṅkam) eṉṟu tirukkōyilil iṭampeṟṟu vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. appāl tuvimuka liṅkamum, tirimuka liṅkamum, caturmukaliṅkamum (caturppujaliṅkamum) piṉṉar pañcamukaliṅkamum uruvākkap peṟṟaṉa. pañcamukaliṅkam eṉpatu iṟivaṉ nāṉku ticikaḷi maṭṭumalla viṇṇiyum maṇṇi yum maṇṇiṉ aṭippuṟattiyum pārkkavallavaṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil  amintuḷḷatu eṉṟu viḷakkam aḷikkap peṟṟatu. ivvāṟē ovvoru mukaliṅkattiṟkum poruttamāṉa viḷakkam tarappaṭṭatu. pañcamuka liṅkam iṟivaṉiṉ aintu kōlaṅkaḷi atāvatu vāmatēva, taṟpuruca, akōra,  caṭiyōcaṭa,  īcāṉa eṉṉum tirukkōlaṅkaḷi viḷakkum tiruuruvakam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
tirukkōyilil uḷḷa tiruuṇṇāḻikiyil (karppak kirakattil) oru vāyil maṭṭum iruntāl ēkamukaliṅkam iṭam peṟalām. iruvāyilkaḷ iruntāl tuvimuka liṅkamum (irumuka liṅkamum) mūṉṟu vāyilkaḷ iruntāl tirimukaliṅkamum (mummuka liṅkamum) nāṉku vāyilkaḷ iruntāl caturppujaliṅkamum (nāṉkumukaliṅkamum) irukka vēṇṭum. nāṉku vāyilkaḷ uḷḷa kōyilkaḷil pañcamukaliṅkam iṭam peṟalām eṉṟu iṟi nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. inta iliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷil taṟpurucamūrttam kiḻakku nōkkiyum, akōra mūrttam teṟku nōkkiyum, caṭiyōcaṭamūrttam mēṟku nōkkiyum, vāmatēva mūrttam vaṭakku nōkkiyum īcāṉa mūrttam viṇṇi nōkkiyum irukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
mukaliṅka vaḷarccikkup piṉ ciṟpavaḷarcciyāl  ki tērnta ciṟpiyāl iliṅkōṟpa mūrtti vaṭivam  ākamavitippaṭi, ciṟpanūl vitikaḷit taḻuvi iliṅka vaṭivattiṉ mītu civaperumāṉiṉ muḻaṅ kālukku mēṟpaṭṭa uruvam cetukkappaṭṭatu. itaṉ poruḷ civaperumāṉiṉ aṭiyum muṭiyum tēvarkaḷum kāṇamuṭiyātatu eṉṟu viḷakkam tarappaṭṭatu.
orukālattil tirumālum nāṉmukaṉum taṅkaḷil yār periyavaṉ eṉṟu vivātatti eḻuppiṉar. iṟutiyāy civa perumāṉiṭam ceṉṟu itaṟku muṭivu kūṟumpaṭi vaṇaṅkik kēṭṭaṉar. civaṉ cirittu uṅkaḷil yār eṉatu aṭiyum muṭiyum kaṇṭu mutalil vantu terivikkiṟārkaḷō avarkaḷ tāṉ periya varkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟa, nāṉmukaṉ taṉ aṉṉa vākaṉattil amarntu viṇṇiṟ ceṉṟu civaperumāṉiṉ muṭiyiṉ elliyik kaṇṭuvarac ceṉṟār. tirumāl paṉṟi vaṭivam tāṅki maṇṇik kiḻittuk koṇṭu civaperumāṉiṉ aṭiyik kāṇac ceṉṟār. pala ūḻikaḷ ceṉṟum civaperumāṉiṉ aṭiyik kāṇātu avar muṉṉarvantu eṉṉāl umatu aṭiyik kāṇa muṭiyavilli. tōlviyuṟṟēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi niṉṟār. nāṉmukaṉ viṇṇiṟ kēkiyil civaperumāṉiṉ tirumuṭimītiruntu iḻintu vīḻnta tāḻampūvik kaṇṭu nī eṅkiruntu varukiṟāy? eṉṟu kēṭka, nāṉ civaperumāṉ muṭi mītiruntu iḻintu maṇṇi nōkkip pala kōṭiyukaṅkaḷāy vantu koṇṭirukkiṟēṉ. avar muṭiyiṉ uyaram evarum aṟiya muṭiyātatu eṉṟu colliyatu. nāṉmukaṉ tāḻampūviṭam, nāṉ civaperumāṉiṉ muṭiyik kaṇṭu tirumpiya tāka, nī oru poy colli eṉakku utava vēṇṭum eṉṟu kūṟa, tāḻampū iṇaṅkiyatu. nāṉmukaṉ civaperumāṉiṭam ceṉṟu nāṉ umatu muṭiyik kaṇṭu tirumpiyuḷḷēṉ. itaṟku umatu caṭimuṭiyi ṉiṉṟu iḻintu varum tāḻampūvē cāṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟit tāṉ tirumāli viṭa uyarntavaṉ eṉṟār. civaperumāṉ cāṭciyi vicārittu, poyc cāṭci eṉṟu kaṇṭu poyc cāṭci coṉṉa tāḻampū iṉi eṉatu caṭimuṭiyil iṭam peṟa muṭiyātu atu pūcikkuriya malarāka iṉi evarum koḷḷār. nāṉmukaṉ eṉṉiṭam poy pukaṉṟu pōṭṭiyil tōlviyuṟṟatāl iṉi avaṉ uruvam eṉatu kōyilil - ēṉ nāṭṭiṉil eṅkum iṭam peṟalākātu eṉṟu kūṟiyatōṭu tirumāl uṇmiyi urittu taṉ tōlviyi oppuk koṇṭatāl avaṉ nāṉmukaṉi viṭap periya teyvam eṉṟu tīrppaḷittār eṉpatu purāṇak kati1. intap purāṇak katiyi viḷakka eḻuntatu iliṅkōṟpa mūrtti yiṉ tiruuruvam iliṅkaṅkaḷiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyil iṟivaṉiṉ taliyāya mūrttamāy viḷaṅkum taṭcaṇā mūrttattiṟkum, iliṅkōṟpa mūrttiyiṉ tiruuruvattiṟkum, iṭippaṭṭa iliṅka corūpa mūrttiyiṉ vaṭivam nīṇṭa kālamāy kāṇa muṭiyātiruntatu. cila āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ civaperumāṉiṉ muḻuvaṭivamum iliṅkattōṭu iṇintu niṟkum tiruuruvam oṉṟu āntira nāṭṭil uḷḷa kuṭimallam eṉṉum iṭattil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. attiru uruvam kiṟittuva ūḻikku muṉ cetukkappaṭṭa oru toṉmiyāṉa vaṭivam. atu iliṅka vaṭiviṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatil oru kuṟipāṭāka - uṇmiyi viḷakkik kāṭṭum oru caṅkilit toṭarpil viṭupaṭṭa vaḷiyamāka (Missing links)  eṇṇap paṭṭu vantati nivartti ceytuḷḷatu. iṉṟu iliṅka vaṭivattiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcci aṟupaṭāta caṅkilit toṭarpāka amintuḷḷatu makiḻccikkuriya tāka niṟivu peṟṟuḷḷatu. inta iliṅka vaṭiviṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyiṉ aṭippaṭiyilē namatu nūliṟku aṭippaṭiyāṉa aṣṭamūrttiyiṉ vaṭiva amippukaḷ amintuḷḷaṉa. aṣṭa mūrttiyiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷil taṭcaṇā mūrttiyiṉ tiruuruvam mutalil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. 
tirukkōyi luḷḷirukkum tirumēṉi taṉṉic
	civaṉeṉavē kaṇṭavarkkuc civaṉ uṟivaṉaṅkē
urukkōli mantirattāleṉa niṉiyumavarkkum
	uḷaṉeṅku milaṉiṅku muḷa ṉeṉpārkkum
viruppāya vaṭivāki yintaṉatti ṉeri pōl
	mantirattiṉ vantutittu mikuñcurapik keṅku
urukkāṇa voṇṇāta pāṉmulip pāl vimmi
	yoḻukuvatu pōl veḷippaṭ ṭaruḷuvaṉaṉ parkkē					(civañāṉa cittiyār)
civaliṅkattiṉ tattuvam
kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu. atu āṇum alla peṇṇum alla, aliyum alla eṉṟu civam eṉṉum teyvattiṟku tattuvam tanta meyyaṟivu peṟṟa makkaḷ tamiḻar. avarkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷ āyntu tampaṭṭaṟivil kaṇṭa teyvattaṉmiyi avarkaḷ tattuva nūlākiya civañāṉapōtam viḷakkukiṟatu. tamiḻar kaṇṭa civatattuvam, cumārttam, puttam, camaṇam, ekōvāyicam  kiṟittavam, iculām pōṉṟa camayaṅkaḷ karuvil uruppeṟṟu eḻuvataṟkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ maṉita camukattiṉ toṭakkak kālattil tōṉṟi iṉṟum poṉṟātu ceṟivuṟṟup polivuṭaṉ tikaḻkiṟatu. ataṉi uṇarāta  iṟi aṟivu eḷḷaḷavu peṟṟa nēṟṟut tōṉṟiya tam camayattik kūṭa naṉku payilāta ciṟṟaṟiviṉar- civa camaya tattuva nūlip payilum vāyppum aṟivum, moḻip payiṟciyum peṟātu tattuva viyākkiyāṉam ceyvatu  varuntaṟkuriyatu.
kaṭavuḷ oṉṟu uṇṭu. atu piṟavātatu, iṟavātatu.  atu `avatāram eṭuttu viṇṇiṉiṉṟu maṇṇiliṟaṅkiyatu eṉpatu kaṭṭuk kati eṉak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. tamiḻakattil tōṉṟiya cāṉṟōrkaḷ teyvattaṉmiyil aruḷiyum aṉpiyum kaṇṭaṉar. avaṟṟi āṇ peṇ taṉmiyatākak koṇṭaṉar. āṇ peṇ taṉmikaḷi viḷakkum aṟikuṟiyāka āṇ, peṇ vaṭivil iru aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷi (ciṉṉaṅkaḷi) vittu vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. itu paṇṭiya tamiḻ makkaḷ kal ūḻiyil tāy tantiyar āṭciyil uruppeṟṟa ammi appaṉ vaḻipāṭṭil eḻunta ciṉṉam. ivviru ciṉṉaṅkaḷum iṇintu orē vaṭivilāṉa ammiyappaṉ eṉum teyvam pirikka muṭiyātatu eṉṟu viḷakkam aḷittaṉar. kaṭavuḷ āṇ, peṇ iyalpiṉaṉ eṉṉum paṇpil vaḻipāṭu iyaṟṟap peṟṟu vantatu.
uḷḷam (maṉam) oru ellikkuṭpaṭṭatu. atu cañcalat taṉmi vāyntatu. attakiya calaṉam niṟinta uḷḷattāl viṇṇum maṇṇum - ēṉ ataṟkappālum eṅkum niṟintilaṅkum pēroḷiyāy eṇṇiṟat telliyillāta kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruuruvic cintikka - tiyāṉikka  - upācikka uḷam kuvintu cintaṉi yaṟṟu orumukappaṭṭāl tāṉē cāttiyamākum. maṉam, moḻi (vākku) meykaḷ (karaṇam) mūṉṟum orumippaṭa vēṇṭum. avvariya taṉmiyi aṭiya oru kuṟiyīṭu iṉṟiyamiyātatu. antak kuṟiyīṭu tāṉ civaliṅkam. atu (kaṭavuḷ)ḷa civam,  aruḷāḷaṉ, aṉpuṭiyōṉ eṉpati aṟivuṟuttukiṟatu. piṟkālattil tāy (māyōḷ) vaṭivilum tanti vaṭivilum piḷḷi vaṭivilum (murukaṉ vaṭivilum) vaḻipaṭṭa tuṇṭu. itaṉic cōmakanta cukācaṉa vaṭivam uṇarttum. māyōḷukku āṇ vaṭivam kaṟpitta poḻutu māyōṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ. (māyōṉ - tirumāl)

paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇam
ciṟpakkali
makkaḷ ulakil pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka vāḻntu uṇarnta paṭṭaṟivāl teḷḷat teḷinta aṟivuc ceṟivum, uḷḷaṟkariya uyariya uḷḷap paṇpum, naṉiciṟanta nalla nākarikamum peṟṟu uyarniliyi aṭintuḷḷaṉar. makkaḷ ulakil makattāṉa muṉṉōṭikaḷāka viḷaṅkum tamiḻar kaṟkaṇṭāyk kaṇṭa kaviṉuṟum nuṇkalikaḷil aṟputamāṉa ciṟpakkaliyum oṉṟu. intap poṟpuṟum ciṟpakkaliyil - kaṭṭiṭak kali ōviyakkali paṭimakkali ākiya mūṉṟum aṭaṅkum.
kaṭṭiṭak kaliyil vīṭu, kōyil, māḷiki, araṇmaṉi, maṇṭapam, kōṭṭi, kottaḷam iṉṉōraṉṉa palavum aṭaṅkum. ōviyak kaliyil (cittirakkaliyil) cuvar ōviyam, kiḻi ōviyam - tuṇiccīli (Canvas) yil tīṭṭum (cittiram) paṭam, puṉiyā ōviyam (kōṭukaḷāl tīṭṭappaṭṭa paṭam), vaṇṇa ōviyam (Colour pinting), neyvaṇṇa ōviyam (Oil Painting) eṉap palavaki uḷa. paṭam eṉpatu tamiḻccol. paṇṭik kālattil tuṇiyil tīṭṭappaṭṭa ōviyam paṭam, allatu paṭām eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭatu. paṭam eṉpatu tuṇiyil eḻutappaṭṭa ōviyam eṉappaṭum. iṉṟu paṭam eṉṉum patam tāḷilum tuṇiyilum cuvarilum tīṭṭappaṭṭa ōviyattik kuṟikkum peyarākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṟatu. ōviyam muṟkālattil eḻuttu eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭatu. ōviyar, kaṇṇuḷ viṉiñar eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭaṉar. ōviyam makkaḷ, teyvam, vilaṅku, paṟavi, maram, ceṭi, koṭi iyaṟkik kāṭci iṉṉō raṉṉa palvēṟu tōṟṟaṅkaḷākac cuvar, tuṇi, tāḷ mutaliyavikaḷil kariyālum, miyālum, vaṇṇak kalaviyālum, neyyālum iṉṟu tīṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. neyvaṇṇa ōviyam nīṇṭa kālam maṟiyātu nilittu niṟkum. paṭimakkali eṉpatu teyvaṅkaḷ, muṉivarkaḷ, arakkarkaḷ, maṉitarkaḷ, vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavikaḷ, ūrvaṉa, maram, ceṭi, koṭi pōṉṟavikaḷi maṇṇilum, marattilum, kallilum tātup poruḷkaḷilum maṇikaḷilum vaṭivamippatākum. ivikaḷil kallil cetukkappaṭuvatu cili (Sculpture) eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. cempu poṉ, veṇkalam pōṉṟa tātup poruḷkaḷil (ulōkaṅkaḷil) ceyyappaṭuvatu paṭimam (Bronze) eṉappaṭum. kaṟkaḷil cilikaḷ ceyvativiṭat tātup poruḷkaḷil paṭimam ceyvatu ciramam. āṉāl nuṇṇiya vēlippāṭukaḷ aṉittum paṭimaṅkaḷiltāṉ eḻiluṟa ēṟṟamuṟac ceyya muṭiyum. tamiḻarkaḷ veṇkalap paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyvatil tērcci vāyntavarkaḷ; kivaṇṇam vāyntavarkaḷ.
ciṟpak kaliyiṉ ilakkaṇamākavē ākamaṅkaḷum, cey vitiyākac ciṟpa nūṟkaḷum eḻuntaṉa. ciṟpa ilakkaṇa vitippaṭi kaṭṭiṭaṅkaḷum paṭimaṅkaḷum cilikaḷum ōviyaṅkaḷum ceytāl tāṉ avikaḷ ciṟappuṟṟu viḷaṅkum. vitippaṭi aḻakuṟa aminta paṭimaṅkaḷ tām kaṇṇiyum karuttiyum kavarvaṉavāy viḷaṅkum. avaṟṟil teyvat taṉmi poliyum eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ.
tamiḻnāṭṭuc ciṟpikaḷ iṉṟikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṉpiruntē kalivallunarkaḷāy viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa cintuveḷic ciṟpakkali viyattaku vēlippāṭuṭiyatu. itu tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ ciṟpa vēlip pāṭṭiṉ nuṭpatti eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatāy irukkiṟatu. tamiḻakat til intac ciṟpakkaliyil īṭupaṭṭuḷḷavarkaḷ tapatikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka ittoḻilil īṭupaṭṭup paṇpaṭṭavarkaḷ. kivaṇṇam kivarap peṟṟavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ tantiyiṭamiruntum kuruviṭamiruntum ikkaliyip payiṉṟu varupavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ ākamaṅkaḷiyum, ciṟpanūṟ kaḷiyum, ceymuṟikaḷiyum paḻutaṟap payiṉṟu vitikaḷukku immiyaḷavum māṟṟamiṉṟi ciṟpaṅkaḷi amippārkaḷ. ciṟpam poṭikaḷ, kal, cuti (naṟcāntu), maram, ulōkaṅkaḷ, maṇ, mutal taramāṉa cukkāṉ, tantam ākiya eṭṭuvakip poruḷkaḷāl ākkappaṭalām eṉṟu māṉacāram eṉṉum ciṟpanūl kūṟukiṟatu.
kallum ulōkamum ceṅkalum maramum
maṇṇum cutiyum tantamum vaṇṇamum
kaṇṭa carukkariyum meḻukum eṉṟivi
pattē ciṟpat toḻiṟ kuṟup pāvaṉa
eṉṟu namatu nikaṇṭu kūṟukiṟatu.
vaḻuvaṟu maraṉum maṇṇum kallum
eḻutiya pāviyum
eṉṟum,
maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum marattiṉum cuvariṉum
kaṇṇiya teyvam kāṭṭunar vakukka
eṉṟum
viḷippaṟi pōkātu meypuṟat tiṭūum
paḷikkaṟi maṇṭapa muṇṭata ṉuḷḷatu
eṉṟum maṇimēkaliyil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
namatu ciṟpa uruvaṅkaḷ muṟkālattil iruvakiyākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. oru vaki muḻuttiruvuruvaṅkaḷ maṟṟoru vaki puṭicciṟpat tiruuruvaṅkaḷ, muḻucciṟpat tiruuruvam eṉpatu uruvattiṉ muṉpakkam piṉpakkam mutaliya ellāp pakkamum teriyumpaṭi tiruuruvatti amittal. puṭic ciṟpam1 eṉpatu uruvattiṉ oru pakkam - atāvatu muṉpuṟamō piṉpuṟamō maṭṭum teriyumāṟu palakikaḷilō, cuvarkaḷilō, kaṟkaḷilō, cuṇṇāmpuk kaṟkaḷilō, ulōkat takaṭukaḷilō tantattilō ceyyappaṭuvatu. ivviruvakiyāṉa ciṟpat tiruuruvaṅkaḷum namatu tirukkōyilkaḷilē irukkiṉṟaṉa. namatu ciṟpaṅkaḷil kaṟpaṉiyil eḻunta teyva uruvaṅkaḷ mutal iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. maṉita tatrūpa ciṟpuruvaṅkaḷ mēṉāṭṭip pōl muḻuvaḷarcci peṟavilli. kāraṇam namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ aḻintu pōkum maṉita uṭalukku aḻiyāta kal allatu ulōkattil uruvam amittu vippatip peritum virumpātatēyākum.
uruva amippiṟkuriya upakaraṇaṅkaḷ
namatu teyvat tiruuruvaṅkaḷi amippataṟku tapati kaḷukku iṉṟiyamiyāta mūṉṟu mukkiya upakaraṇaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. aviyāvaṉa: 1. tiyāṉa culōkaṅkaḷ, 2. ilakkaṇa vitikaḷ, 
3. aḷavup piramāṇaṅkaḷ eṉappaṭum.
tiyāṉa culōkaṅkaḷ
tiyāṉa culōkaṅkaḷ eṉṟāl ovvoru teyvattiṟkum irukka vēṇṭiya aṅka ilaṭcaṇaṅkaḷi viḷakkum ariya vaṭamoḻi mantiram ākum. itaṟkum teyvaṅkaḷit tutittup pāṭum piṟamantiraṅkaḷukkum vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. tapatikaḷ paṭimaṅkaḷic ceyya ārampikkummuṉ avarkaḷ uḷḷattil kuṟippiṭṭa teyva uruvik kaṟpaṉi ceytu koḷḷuvataṟku ētuvāka avarkaḷāl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum mantiramē tiyāṉa culōkam eṉappaṭum. tiyāṉa culōkaṅkaḷi uḷḷattil uruppōṭṭuk koḷḷum poḻutu, tapati ceyyap pōkum tiruuruvattit tam uḷḷattil mutalil kāṇkiṉṟār. ataṉ piṟaku, atit tam kiyāl tūla vaṭivil uruvākkukiṉṟār. itu ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil, tiruuruvaṅkaḷi iyaṟṟum ciṟpikaḷ at tiruuruvaṅkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi (ākamaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟiyavāṟu) tiyāṉippatil mikavum paṟṟuṭiyavarāy iruttal vēṇṭum. attiyāṉa neṟiyāl aṉṟik kāṭci mutaliya vēṟev vaḻiyāṉum ittiruuruva ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ amiyātu eṉṟu kūṟappaṭ ṭuḷḷatu.1
tapati maṟikaḷi matippavarāyum, ākamaṅkaḷi muṟip paṭi aṟintavarāyum, maram, ceṅkal, karuṅkal, ulōkam, maṇ, cāntu, cukkāṉkal, tantam, maṇikaḷ mutaliyavikaḷil tiruuruvaṅ kaḷi amikkum aṟivu āṟṟal uṭiyavarāyum, tarppip pulmōtirattit tarittavarāyum taliyil muṭiyuṭiyavarāyum ariyil pītāmparam aṇintavarāyum cantaṉattip pūciyavarāyum civapūci purikiṟavarāyum iruttal vēṇṭum. tiyāṉa citti kivarap peṟṟa ciṟpikaḷ amikkum uruvaṅkaḷ āyiram āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟum makkaḷ nākarika mutircci peṟṟa piṉṉarum aḻakuṭiya tāy uḷḷattik kavarvaṉavāyk kāṭciyaḷittuvaruvati maṟappataṟkilli.
tiruuruviṉ māṉamum tiraviyamum, rēṭṭam, mattipam, atamam eṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭum. ellāt tiraviyaṅkaḷilum teyvat taṉmi orē vitamākak kāṇappaṭuvatilli.
kaḷar nilam (ūcā pūmi), vaḷanilam, kiḷar nilam (aḻutta millāta meṉmiyāṉa pūmi) ākiyavikaḷil taṉittaṉi nilattiṟkēṟṟa maraṅkaḷip payir ceytāl nalla palaṉi nalkum. palaṉkaḷ palavitamāy nilavēṟṟumikaḷiṉāl eḻukiṉṟaṉa. eṉavē maraṅkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumikaḷ maṭṭum palaṉkaḷiṉ ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷi aḷippatilli; iṭaṅkaḷiṉ vēṟṟumikaḷukkēṟpavē palaṉkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. atupōlavē ceyyap peṟṟa teyva uruvap poruḷkaḷukkēṟpa uttama mattipa atamap palaṉik koṭukkak kāṇalām.
eṉavē namatu muṉṉōrkaḷ viruppamākat tātuppaṭimaṅ kaḷiyē ceytuvantaṉar. namatu nāṭṭil muṟkālattil atikamāka veḷḷi kiṭikkavilli ataṉāl atikamāṉa veḷḷip paṭimaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭavilli. cempu ērāḷamākak kiṭittatāl cempup paṭimaṅkaḷic ceytaṉar. cempu kiṭṭattaṭṭa poṉ pōṉṟa niṟamum atip pōṉṟa kuḻivum uṭiyatāy irunta tāl ceppu uruvaṅkaḷ atikamākac ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. poṟ paṭimaṅkaḷ ciṟappāṉatu eṉṟālum atu atika viliyēṟap peṟṟatāy iruntatāl makkaḷ ceppuppaṭimaṅkaḷip peritum virumpiṉar. eṉṟālum ceppup paṭimaṅkaḷil taṅkaḷ caktikkēṟṟa aḷavil poṉṉik kalantu tiruuruvaṅkaḷic ceytu vantaṉar. ataṉāl tiruuruvaṅkaḷ eḻil peṟṟilaṅkiṉa. periya nilakkiḻār kaḷum maṉṉarkaḷum, poṉṉāl tiruuruvaṅkaḷ ceytu vaḻipaṭṭu vantaṉar. irāvaṇaṉ poṉṉāl ceyyap peṟṟa civaliṅkattit tāṉ pōkum iṭaṅkaḷukkellām eṭuttuc ceṉṟu nāṭōṟum vaḻipaṭṭu vantāṉ eṉṟu namatu purāṇak katikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
poṉṉāṟ ceyyap peṟṟa tiruuruvattik kaṇṇāl uṟṟup pārttuk koṇṭiruppatum, avvuruviṟkuk kuṭamuḻukku ceyyum nīriyum pāl paḻam pōṉṟa apicēkap poruḷiyum nām uṇpatum kaṇṇukkum uṭalukkum uṟuti aḷikkiṉṟaṉa; uḷḷattiṟku uvaki ēṟpaṭukiṟatu eṉṟu aṟiñarkaḷ kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ. 	
itupōl maṇikaḷālum, paṭimaṅkaḷ ceytu apicēkam ceytu vaḻipaṭuvatil, naṟpayaṉ viḷikiṟatu eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭukiṟatu.
tiruuruva amippu vitikaḷ
namatu teyvat tiruvuruviṉic ceyvataṟku mikap paḻamiyāṉa kālattilē ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi ākkiyuḷḷaṉar. paṭimaṅkaḷi mūṉṟu pirivāka vakuttu ilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar. am mūvakiyāvaṉa : acalam, calam, calācalam eṉappaṭum. maṇ, maṇal, cuti ivikaḷāl uruvākkappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷ acalam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. kal, maṇikaḷ, maram, tātup poruḷkaḷāl ceyyap paṭum cili calam eṉappaṭum. ulōkaṅkaḷāl uruvākkappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷ calācalam eṉappaṭum.
itilum artta cittiram, cittiram, cittirāpācam eṉa mūṉṟu pirivukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. pāti aṅkaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum tiruvuruvi artta cittiram eṉṟum muḻu uruvaṅkaḷōṭu tikaḻum uruvic cittiram eṉṟum tuṇi, cuvar, palaki ivikaḷil mēṭu paḷḷaṅ kaḷuṭaṉ kaṇṇiṟkuk kāṭci aḷikkum uruvic cittirāpācam eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
cutiyiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa uruvam artta cittiramākum. maṟṟap poruḷkaḷiṉāl ceyyappaṭum uruvam cittiramākum. cittira karma vitippaṭi tātukkaḷālē ceyyappaṭum uruvam cittirāpācamākum. cittirāpācam tēvarkaḷukku ukantatu. ivi uttamam mattiyamam atamam eṉa mūṉṟu vakippaṭum.
ceṅkaṟkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa uruvam ciṟantatu. ataṉiṉum uyarntatu artta cittiram. ativiṭac ciṟantatu maṇṇāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa uruvamākum. maṇṇālum marattālum ceyyap peṟṟatu mēlāṉatu. marattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭativiṭak kalliṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu mika mēlāṉatu kalliṉum ulōkattāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa paṭimamē mika mika uyarntatu.
ulōkaṅkaḷil uyarntatu poṉ. naṭuttara ulōkam veḷḷi. kaṭittara ulōkam cempu. ivikaḷil ceyyappeṟṟa tiruuruvaṅkaḷ muṟiyē uttama, mattima, atamap payaṉkaḷi nalkak kūṭiyavi eṉṟu ākamaṅkaḷ aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
tiruuruvam amikkum cātakaṅkaḷ 
maṇṇil uruvam amittal :
ātikālattil tamiḻakattil maṇṇāl tiruuruvam amit taṉar. irumpuk kālam eḻunta piṉ mutaṉ mutalāka marattāl tiruuruvam ceytaṉar. piṉṉar kallil aḻakiya tiruuruvaṅkaḷic cetukkiṉar. iṟutiyil ulōka kālattil poṉ, cempu mutaliyavikaḷāl tiruuruvaṅkaḷi vārkka muṟpaṭṭaṉar. 
mutaṉ mutalāka maṇṇāl tiruuruvaṅkaḷ amikkap paṭṭatum avi eḷitil maḻi, kāṟṟu mutaliyavikaḷāl cētamuṟātu avikaḷic cūḷiyil vittuc cuṭṭu veḷḷi, civappu, mañcaḷ,  pacci, nīlam mutaliya vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tīṭṭi aḻakūṭṭi vantaṉar. iṉṟum avvuruvaṅkaḷ ciṟṟūrkaḷilum nakaraṅkaḷil ēḻikaḷ vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷilum tāyt teyvaṅ kaḷākiya kāḷi, kūḷi, piṭāri, pēcci, icakki, elli, nīli, māri mutaliya peyarkaḷāl kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil perun teyvaṅkaḷ kūṭac cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭum tiruuruvaṅkaḷukku namatu ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
teyva uruvaṅkaḷ ceyyap payaṉpaṭum maṇ cāṅkalam, anūpam, micciram eṉa mūṉṟu vakiyāyp pirikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpati mutalil nām aṟintirukka vēṇṭum.
nilattiṉiṉṟu eḷitil veṭṭi eṭukka muṭiyātu uṟutiyāka irukkum maṇṇiṟkuc cāṅkalam eṉṟu peyar. ciṟu maṇal kalantu atikac ciramam illātu veṭṭi eṭukkappaṭum maṇṇāṉatu mūṉṟu muḻa āḻam variyil naṉṟāka iruntāl ammaṇ anūpam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa iruvaki maṇkaḷum kalanta niliyil kāṇappaṭum maṇṇiṟku micciram eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
maṇ, eṉṟum nīrvaṟṟāmalum cuttamākavum makiḻccikara mākavum kāṇappaṭum āṟu, kuḷam ākiyavikaḷiliruntu eṭukkap paṭa vēṇṭum.
maṇ, veḷuppu, mañcaḷ, civappu, karuppu eṉa nāṉku vakip paṭum.  maṇṇi nilattiṉiṉṟu tōṇṭi eṭuttu ati nīril kalakkip pututtuṇiyiṉāl vaṭikaṭṭi eṭukka vēṇṭum. piṉ ati maṭpāṇṭattil nīr cuṇṭum vari vittirukka vēṇṭum. appāl ammaṇṇip pattiramākak kāppāṟṟa vēṇṭum. piṟaku nāṟpāl marappaṭṭiyiṉ cāṟṟiyum allatu kacāyattiyum viṭṭu anta maṇṇi nalla pāttirattiliṭṭup piciya vēṇṭum. ati uruṇṭiyākkak kūṭiya vakiyil maṟupaṭiyum nīric cuṇṭa vikka vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṟaku tirupalōṣit nīri (kaṭukkāy, tāṉṟikkāy, nellikkāy ākiyavikaḷiṉ tūḷiṉi nīril ūṟa vittu anta nīri) viṭṭu maṇṇi naṉṟākap picintu ati mīṇṭum nīr  cuṇṭumpaṭi ceyya vēṇṭum. nīr muḻutum cuṇṭāmal uruṇṭi piṭikkum aḷaviṟku maṇṇikkāyavittu uruṇṭiyākac ceyyavēṇṭum. ammaṇ uruṇṭiyāṉatu uḷḷaṅkiyil vittu aḻuttiṉāl irēkikaḷ patiyak kūṭiya pakkuvattil irukka vēṇṭum. anta maṇṇi eṇ kōṇa vaṭivamāṉa uruṇṭiyākak kiyiṉālē ceyya vēṇṭum. anta uruṇṭiyiṉ cuṟṟaḷavāṉatu 11 aṅkulam irukka vēṇṭum. immaṇ oru paṭi aḷavuṭiyatāy irukka vēṇṭum.
piṉ, poṭimaṇaliyum karuṅkal (kūḻāṅkal) poṭiyiyum camaaḷavu cērttu ati ¼ paṅku eṭuttu maṇṇuṭaṉ kalantu tirupalā kacāyattiviṭṭu naṉṟākap picintu 7 mutal 10 nāḷ vari nāḷtōṟum picintu vikka vēṇṭum.
appāl ravi, kōtumi, uḷuntu, kāyām pū allatu ili (allatu kāyampū niṟamāṉa uḷuntu) ivikaḷit tūḷ ceytu maṇṇil 1/8 pākam eṭuttu maṇṇōṭu kalantu muṟṟiṉa tēṅkāy nīri (maṭṭiyuṭaṉ  iṭittup piḻinta cāṟu allatu iḷanīri) viṭṭup pāttirattil pōṭṭuk kiyiṉāl naṉṟāyp picintu 10 nāṭkaḷ vittirukka vēṇṭum. (allatu 10 nāḷ naṉṟāka mitittu vara vēṇṭum)
cirivēṭṭakam (tiruvaṭṭam piciṉ) kukkulam (kukkil) kunturukkam (pūṉikkaṇ kuṅkuliyam) carccaracam (kuṅkuliyam) ivikaḷip poṭi ceytu 1/16 paṅku, maṇṇuṭaṉ kalantu tayirip pāttirattil viṭṭu ellāvaṟṟiyum cērttup piciya vēṇṭum.
ataṟkup piṟaku cukku, miḷaku, tippili, mañcaḷ ivikaḷiṉ tūḷic camaaḷavu cērttu ati maṇṇil 1/10 paṅku kalantu tēṉ, pāl, ney ivikaḷi viṭṭup pāttirattil kalakka vēṇṭum.
viḷāmpiciṉ, kōṭṭam, kirikam (naṟceṅkal allatu kāvikkaṭṭi) tāḷakam, cantaṉam, akil, paccik kaṟpūram, kōrōcaṉi ākiyavikaḷip poṭi ceytu camaaḷavākak kūṭṭi maṇṇil 1/30 paṅku kalantu atacīcu nēkam (kāyāñceṭi) allatu vitiyiṉ eṇṇey allatu kuḻampi  viṭṭuk kalantu naṉṟākap piciya vēṇṭum.
poṉ, veḷḷi ākiyavikaḷiṉ poṭiyik kiṭitta aḷavu anta maṇṇuṭaṉ cērttup piciya vēṇṭum. puṟṟu, mali, āṟu, vayal, naṇṭuvaḷi, eṉṟum nīrvaṟṟāta ēri, kaṭal, yāṉit tantam, pacu allatu kāḷiyiṉ kompu, kalappiyiṉ koḻu ākiya pattu iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭum maṇṇiṟkut tacamirukki eṉṟu peyar. immaṇṇik kiṭitta variyil campātittu avikaḷuṭaṉ nalla maṇapporuḷkaḷik kalantu viḷāmpiciṉ allatu tēṉviṭṭuk kuḻappa vēṇṭum.
piṟaku, mutirnta tēṅkāyi maṭṭiyuṭaṉ iṭittuc cāṟṟi eṭuttut taca mirukkiyil, mēlē 1, 1½ allatu, 2, 2½ aṅkulam niṟkumpaṭi 5 nāḷ vari vittirukka vēṇṭum.
antat tacamirukkiyi maṇṇil ¼ paṅku kalantu kuḻittu ati iraccupantaṉattiṟku mēlē pūca vēṇṭum. ataṟku mēlē mīṇṭum iraccupantaṉañ ceyya vēṇṭum. mutal iraccupantaṉat tiṟku mēlē mirullēpami ceytu mīṇṭum ceyyappaṭum iraccupantaṉattiṉ kaṉamāṉatu uṟuppukaḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu 1, 2 allatu 3 aṅkulam irukka vēṇṭum. piṉ maṇṇip pūci ularavittu atu ularumuṉ kavaṉittu avaciyamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil mella mella maṇṇip pūca vēṇṭum.
(irumuṟi iraccupantaṉam ceytapiṉ) cūlattiṟku veḷiyē yuḷḷa kaṉatti nāṉku paṅkukaḷākac ceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. anta nālu paṅkukaḷil oru paṅku kaṉam maṇṇip pūcuvataṟku anūpam eṉṟu peyar. ataṟku mēlē iraṇṭu paṅkukkaṉam maṇṇip pūcuvataṟku micciram eṉṟu peyar. ataṟku mēl oru paṅku maṇṇip pūcuvataṟkuc cāṅkalam eṉṟu peyar. immaṇ pūccukkaḷiantanta vakimaṇṇikkalantu poruttamākac ceyvikka vēṇṭum.
kīḻē kūṟappaṭṭa mūṉṟu pākatti nīkki, nālāvatu pākatti (4vatu pākamākap pūca vēṇṭiya maṇṇuṭaṉ) caṇal mutaliyavi (pañcattukaḷ)kaḷik kalkattuṭaṉ naṉku kalantu cērttu antak kalkattiṉālē kālakkiramattil ilēpaṉam ceyya vēṇṭum. itaṉi mirut camcukāram eṉpar. itaṉpiṉ āṟu, kuḷam, ēri, vayal pōṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil uppunīr iṉṟi nalla nīruḷḷa iṭattil nīruṭaṉ kalantirukkum perumaṇal allatu cukkāṉil tēṟiya cukkip pōṉṟa niṟamuḷḷatākap pārttu eṭuttu atikkaḻuvi naṉku ularavittup piṉ ati naṉṟāyp poṭi ceyya vēṇṭum. piṉ appoṭiyit tirupalā kacāyatti viṭṭuk kalantu piṇṭamākac ceytu ulara vikka vēṇṭum.
viḷāmpiciṉ nīr mūṉṟu vitamākum. avi muṟiyē piciṉukku 5 paṅku allatu 5½, 6 paṅku nīruṭaṉ kūṭiyavikaḷākum. ivikaḷukku muṟiyē curuvam, cuvaccam, cēkam eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭum. mutalil cuvaccam, cēkam ākiya iruvita nīriyum viṭṭu mirut piṇṭatti veṇṇeyip pōl piciya vēṇṭum. piṟaku paruttip pañcut tukaḷkaḷi maṇṇuṭaṉ kalantu naṉku karittu vittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
intak kalkatti oṭittāl oṭittavāyāṉatu oṭitta vāḻit taṇṭiṉ oṭipaṭṭa iṭattip pōl irukka vēṇṭum. ittakiya kalkattiyē mutalil pūca vēṇṭum.
mattiya pantakalkamāṉatu mūṉṟu vitappaṭum. avi muṟiyē piciṉukku 3, 3½ allatu 4 paṅku nīruṭaṉ kūṭiyavikaḷākum. ivikaḷil pintiya iraṇṭu nīr vakikaḷi viṭṭu maṇṇi naṉṟākap piciya vēṇṭum. appāl ammaṇṇi 2 allatu 3 yavikaṇattiṟku mella mellap pūca vēṇṭum. cūlattil iṭiveḷiviṭāmalum iṭukkukaḷ illāmalum naṉku kavaṉittup pārttuc cuttamākap pūca vēṇṭum. ivvitam miruttu lēpaṉam ceytapiṉ ati 2, 1½, allatu 1 mātam kāya vikka vēṇṭum.
appāl kūrccatti vittu (maṇṇuṭaṉ) aṇṭa nīrvakikaḷik kalantu piciya vēṇṭum. piṉ eñciya nīriviṭṭuk kalantu picintu antak kalkatti (kalaviyi) uṟuppukkaḷil piramāṇantamākap pūca vēṇṭum.
uṟuppukkaḷiṉ piramāṇaṅkaḷi naṉku kavaṉittut takuntavāṟu amikka vēṇṭum. aṅkam, upāṅkam, pirattiyāṅkam ākiyavikaḷ eḻiluṭaṉ tikaḻumāṟu tīṭṭa vēṇṭum.
uṟuppukkaḷ (aṅkaṅkaḷ) nāṉku vakippaṭum. avi 1. makā aṅkam, 2. aṅkam, 3. pirattiyāṅkam, 4. upāṅkam eṉappaṭum. talikku makā aṅkam eṉṟum kaḻuttu, irutayam, vayiṟu, mēṭaram, aṭaṅkiya uṭaṟpakutikku aṅkam eṉṟum, kāl, ki ākiya pakuti kaḷukku upāṅkam eṉṟum aṇikaḷukkup pirattiyāṅkam eṉṟum peyarākum. ivikaḷuṭaṉ caṭaṅkaṅkaḷiyum kalpikka vēṇṭum.
aimpoṟikaḷum ki, kāl mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷum ataṉataṉ ceyalkaḷi āṟṟat takkatāy amikka vēṇṭum.
viḷām piciṉukku 2½ allatu 3 paṅku nīrviṭṭu naṉṟākap picintu annīril pututtuṇiyi naṉṟāka naṉittu attuṇi yiṉāl paṭimattip pōrtta vēṇṭum. ataṟku mēl kalkalēpaṉam ceytu 10 nāṭkaḷ vittirukka vēṇṭum. kalkalēpaṉamāṉatu yaviyil 1/8 paṅku kaṉam irukka vēṇṭum. 10 nāṭkaḷ ceṉṟu kūrccattiṉāl (kūrccam - tūriki atāvatu vaṇṇantīṭṭum karuviyiṉāl) piciṉ nīri eṭuttu ellā uṟuppukkaḷiyum naṉku kavaṉittup pārttup pūca vēṇṭum. ataṟku mēl aṇipeṟac ceyya vēṇṭum. iṉṉum oru muṟi kalkalēpaṉam ceytu ataṟku mēl veḷḷi tīṭṭa vēṇṭum.
itaṟku mēl vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiya vitaṅkaḷiyum avikaḷiṉ pirivukaḷiyum, atit tiruuruvil tīṭṭa vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷiyum virivākak kāciyapam, ciṟparattiṉam pōṉṟa nūṟkaḷil kāṇalām. makkaḷ iṉṟu cātāraṇamāka veḷḷi, karuppu, civappu, mañcaḷ, pacci, nīlam, ūtā pōṉṟa niṟaṅkaḷit tāṉ aṟivārkaḷ. āṉāl āṅkilattil iṉṟu nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tamiḻakattil āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē namatu ciṟpanūṟkaḷ cumār 40kku mēṟpaṭṭa vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kuṟippiṭṭiruppatōṭu anta vaṇṇam etip pōṉṟu irukkum eṉṟum namakku eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ āṅkilēyarkaḷ nāṉku vaṇṇaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷik kūṭa aṟiyārkaḷ.
cilikaḷ amittal
kallilē amikkum tiruuruvattic cili eṉpar. ciliyi amikkum ciṟpi mali, kaṭal, āṟu, nakar, kāṭu ākiya iṭaṅkaḷil maraṅkaḷiṉ arukilum kōyil pakkaṅkaḷilum āṟṟaṅkari, kuḷattaṅkari arukil irukkum kaṟkaḷit tōṇṭi eṭuttuc cili amippārkaḷ. ēṉiya iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kaṟ kaḷic cilā caṅkirahaṇam ceyyak kūṭātu eṉṟu ciṟpanūṟkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
cili ceyyum kaṟkaḷ nāṉku vakippaṭum. avi : 
1. veṇmi, 2. civappu, 3. mañcaḷ, 4. karuppu eṉappaṭum.
1. veḷḷik kal
veṇmi niṟak kallāṉatu pacuviṉ pāl, caṅku, mullippū, nilavu, muttu, paḷiṅku (paṭikam), mallikippū pōṉṟa ēḻu poruḷkaḷukku oppāṉatāka irukkum.
2. ceṅkal
civappu niṟamāṉa kal, cemparattampū, puracampū, paṭṭuppūcci (intirakōpam), cātiliṅkam, muyal utiram, mātuḷampū, pantūka malar (civappup pūvuṭaṉ kūṭiya perumaruntuppū pōṉṟa ēḻu vitap poruṭkaḷiṉ vaṇṇaṅkaḷi ottirukkum.
3. mañcaḷ kal
mañcaḷ vaṇṇak kallāṉatu poṉ, carakkoṉṟi, ceṇpakappū, mañcaḷ,  mañcaḷ niṟakkuṟiñcip pū1, elumiccam paḻam, aritāram ākiya ēḻu niṟam uṭiyatāy irukkum.
4. karuṅkal
karuṅkal pūṉikkaṇ (makicāṭcam), kuṅkiliyam, añcaṉak kal, nīlōtpalam, karuṅkuḷavi, mayilkaḻuttu, macipattiram, irāca varttam (karumiyāṉa tēṉ niṟam), karuppup payiṟu, kuṟumpayaṟu pōṉṟa pattu vaṇṇaṅkaḷi ottirukkum.
veṇmi, civappu, mañcaḷ, karuppu vaṇṇac cilikaḷ muṟiyē antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, uḻavar kulaṅkaḷukku uriyavi kaḷākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi ovvoṉṟum aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu ākiyavikaḷi aḷikkum.
antanta iṉattiṟkuriya cilikaḷ antanta iṉattiṟku uyarntatākum. antaṇarkaḷ karuppu, mañcaḷ mutaliya niṟaccili kaḷi vaṇaṅkalām. āṉāl uḻavarkaḷ vaṇikarkaḷ ākiyōrkaḷ uyarnta kulattiṟkuriya cilikaḷi vittup pūcikkak kūṭātu.
ācāriyaṉ, ciṟpi ākiya iruvarum veṇmiyāṉa āṭi kaḷi aṇintu veṇcantaṉam pūci veḷḷip pūkkaḷālāṉa mālikaḷi aṇintu pañcāṅkap pūcaṉaṅkaḷ pūṇṭu kōṭari, kaṭappāri, uḷi mutaliya āyutaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kal irukkum iṭat tiṟkup pōy maṉa amitiyuṭaṉ akkalli valam vantu kuṟṟam illāta kallik kaṇṭu ciṟappittu akkalliṉ taṉmiyi niccayittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.
eppoḻutum maḻiyil naṉintu koṇṭiruppatu allatu veyilil kāyntu koṇṭiruppatu, tīyiṉ nāṟṟamuḷḷatu, (kal tīyil pocuṅkiyatu), uppu nīr paṭintatu, keṭṭa iṭattil iruppatu, nīrkkariyil iruppatu, vēṟu kāriyattiṟkup payaṉ paṭṭatu, maṇal kalantiruppatu, cēṟu paṭintiruppatu, citivuṟṟatu (aḻuttam viciṟip pōṉatu),  pārkkap payaṅkaramāṉatu, kōṭukaḷ, puḷḷikaḷ, kaḷaṅkaṅkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiyatu pōṉṟa kaṟkaḷ cilikaḷ ceyyak kūṭātavikaḷākum.
kuṟṟam uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ
1. irēki
1. kayiṟu viḻuntatu (kalliṉ naṭuvē kayiṟu pōṉṟa irēki yuḷḷatu) allatu kayiṟu pōṉṟa tōṟṟam uṭiyatu. 2. pakalavaṉ (katiravaṉ) katirkaḷ pāyntatu 3. maḻikkāl pōṉṟa uruvam uṭiyatu eṉa mūṉṟu vita irēkikaḷ uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ.
2. pintu
1. nāvalpaḻam pōṉṟa uruvam uṭiyatu. 2. tirāṭci allatu muntirip paḻam allatu kāy pōṉṟa vaṭivam vāyntatu. 3. vēṟu enta vitamāṉa vaṭṭa vaṭivamuḷḷatu. immūṉṟum pintu uṭiyatu eṉak kūṟappaṭum.
3. kaḷaṅkam
1. irumpu niṟam uṭiyatu. 2. karuṅkuḷavi pōṉṟa niṟam uṭiyatu. 3. mayil iṟaku pōṉṟa niṟamuṭiyatu eṉak kaḷaṅkam mūṉṟu vitamākum.
ittakiya kuṟikaḷ (tōcaṅkaḷ) uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ tiruuruvaṅkaḷ ceyvataṟkut takkaviyalla.
1. pērīcci ili pōṉṟa kōṭuṭiyatu. 2. arukaṅkaṭṭi pōṉṟa kōṭuṭiyatu. 3. mūṅkil muḷi pōṉṟa kōṭuṭiyatu. 4. eṭṭukkāl pūcciyiṉ kālkaḷip pōṉṟa nīṇṭa kōṭukaḷi yuṭiyatu. 5. veḷḷi niṟam illāta ēṉiya niṟamuḷḷa kallil veḷḷi irēki uḷḷatu ākiya aintu vakik kaṟkaḷāl  āṉa cilikaḷ ellāc celvaṅkaḷiyum nalkum. (eṉavē mutalil kūṟappaṭṭa 4 vitak kōṭukaḷum veḷḷik kaṟkaḷukkuk kūṟappaṭṭavikaḷām)
karuppuk kallil karuppuk kōṭum veḷḷik kallil karuppuk kōṭum aḻivi uṇṭu paṇṇum. eṉavē aṭiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa kuṟṟamaṟṟa kaṟkaḷik koṇṭu cili ceyvatu ciṟantatu.
nalla kaṟkaḷ
karuppu niṟamāṉa kallum pacci niṟamāṉa kallum (allatu iru niṟamum kalanta kallum) kirakikkatakkatām. veḷḷik kal ciṟappuṭaṉ koḷḷat takkatu. pērīcci iliyiṉ niṟamuṭiya kallum arukaṅkaṭṭiyiṉ niṟamuṭiya kallum nalla kaṟkaḷ. naṟpayaṉ nalkattakkaṉa.
mikavum karuppāṉa niṟamuṭiya kal naṟpayaṉi nalkātu eṉa nūṟkaḷ nuvalkiṉṟaṉa. kaḷaṅkam uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ nōyi uṇṭākkum; nāṭu naliyum.
pintu (puḷḷi), tōcam (kuṟṟam) uḷḷa kaṟkaḷ makkaḷukku aḻiviyum celva nācattiyum uṇṭākkum. 
eṉavē kaṟkaḷiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷiyum kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum ārāyntu kuṟṟamuḷḷa kaṟkaḷi akaṟṟik kuṇamaḷḷa aḻakiya vaṇṇamuṭiya kaṟkaḷit tēṭik kaṇṭupiṭittuc cilikaḷ ceyya vēṇṭum.
oli, uruvam iṉṉōraṉṉa pētaṅkaḷāl pāla, yuvati, virutti eṉṟum āṇkal, peṇkal, alikkal eṉṟum ciṟpa viṟpaṉṉarkaḷ kaṟkaḷip palavitamākap pirittirukkiṉṟārkaḷ.
kalli eṭukka nilattit tōṇṭum poḻutu cattamum, kuṭṭiyāṉa uruvamum uṭiya kal kiṭittāl atip pālakkal eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
paṉiyip pōṉṟa ōciyum maṇiyip pōṉṟa oliyum nīṇṭa vaṭivamum vāynta kallip peṇ kal eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
iriccalāṉa oliyit tarum kal viruttak kal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
maṇiyōciyip pōl atika olitarum nīṇṭa vaṭivam vāynta kal āṇkal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.
paḷapaḷappiṉṟi uṟutiyiṉṟik karaṭumuraṭāy oliyillāmal irukkum kal alikkal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum.  
iḵtaṉṟi uruvapētattik koṇṭum kalli āṇ, peṇ, ali, pāla, virutta eṉa aintu vitamākap pirittum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
caturamākavum eṇkōṇamākavum uḷḷa kal, peṇkal ākum. nīṇṭa caturamākavum vaṭṭavaṭivamākavum pattuk kōṇa mākavum paṉṉiraṇṭu kōṇamākavum iruppatu āṇ kal eṉavum nalla vaṭṭa vaṭivamāka iruppatu alikkal eṉavum pattuk kōṇamāka iruppatu pālakkal eṉavum paṉṉiraṇṭu kōṇamāka iruppatu viruttak kal eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. pālakkal, viruttakkal ākiya iruvitak kaṟkaḷum cilikaḷ ceyvataṟku ēṟṟaviyalla.
āṇkallil iliṅkam ceyya vēṇṭum. peṇ kallāl pīṭatti (piṇṭikiyi)c ceyya vēṇṭum. alikkallāl pātā tāratti (pātātāram - iliṅkattiṉ aṭittalattiṉ ātāram) ceyyavēṇṭum. inta vitikaḷukku māṟākac cilikaḷ ceytāl cilikaḷ ceyyum ciṟpikku nācam uṇṭākum.
tēcikaṉ, kiḻakku nuṉiyākavō, vaṭakku nuṉiyākavō irukkum kallik kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum. kiḻakku nuṉiyāṉa kallil mēṟkup pakkam aṭippākamākum. vaṭakku nuṉiyāṉa kallil teṉpakkam aṭippākamākum. kiḻakkup pakkamum vaṭakkup pakkamum iliṅkattiṉ nuṉippakkam (talippakkam) ākum. kalliṉ aṭippakkam mukam (muṉ pakkam) ākum. mēlpakkam piṉpakka mākum.
ivvāṟu kalli naṉku cōtittup pārttu antak kalli irutaya mantiram cepittup pacumpāliṉāl naṉṟāka alampic cuttam ceyya vēṇṭum. piṉ antak kallit tūkki niṟuttic cattiya mantirattiṉālē cantaṉam pū mutaliyavikaḷ koṇṭu vaḻipaṭa vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ īcāṉiya tikkil cānti ōmam ceyya vēṇṭum.
akkiṉi, ātānam mutaliya evvāvaṟṟiyum muṟippaṭi ceyya vēṇṭum. irutaya mantirattāl camittukaḷiyum mūla mantirattāl neyyiyum akōra mantirattāl karuviyum (aṉṉattiyum) puruṭa mantirattāl taṇṭulattiyum (ariciyi yum) īcāṉamantirattāl eḷḷiyum, kavaca mantirattāl kaṭukiyum ōmam ceyya vēṇṭum. piṉ camittu ney, aṉṉam, arici, eḷ, kaṭuku ākiyavi ovvoṉṟiyum muṟiyē 100, 50 allatu 25 taṭavi ōmam ceyya vēṇṭum. aknē mantirattāl cuviṭṭakiruta ōmattic ceytu piṉ pūraṇā hūti ōmattic ceyya vēṇṭum.
piṉ akkiṉiyi utvācaṉam ceytu viṭṭu, pūci ceyta nirmāliyappū mutaliyavikaḷi eṭuttuk kaḷiya vēṇṭum. kallic cetukkum āyutattit tavira maṟṟavikaḷi mantirat tiṉāl pūci ceyya vēṇṭum. apacarṇa mantirattic collik kalli uṭikka vēṇṭum. piṉ ciṟpi ceyya vēṇṭiya kāriyattic ceytu muṭikka vēṇṭum.
tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ (karppakirukattiṉ) aḷavu, ataṉ vāyiṟpaṭiyiṉ aḷavu, uṇṇāḻikiyiṉ tūṇ maṭṭam (tūṇ aḷavu), talamāṉam (uṇṇāḻikiyiṉ pū maṭṭattiṉ pakkattiṉ aḷavākum. veḷippirākārattiṉ veḷippakkattiṉ nīḷamum talamāṉamākak kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu. ituvē pātamāṉam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṟatu) attamāṉam, talamāṉam, aṅkulamāṉam eṉṟu iliṅkamāṉatu ēḻuvita māṉaṅkaḷāl niccayikkap paṭṭirukkiṟatu. inta ēḻuvita māṉaṅkaḷilum palavita aḷavukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
iḵtaṉṟi uṇṇāḻikiyiṉ aḷavil 3/5 paṅku uyaram irukkum tiruuruvam uttamaliṅkam eṉṟum, 1/2 paṅku uyaram iruppatu mattimaliṅkam eṉṟum, inta iraṇṭu aḷaviṟkum mattiyil uḷḷa aḷavi atāvatu 1/2kku mēl 1-variyuḷḷa mattiyatti 9-pākaṅkaḷākac ceyya vēṇṭum. avikaḷil ovvoru aḷavum taṉittaṉiyē 9 vitamāṉa iliṅkamāṉamākum. inta oṉpatu vitamāṉa iliṅka māṉaṅkaḷil atika aḷavuḷḷa mūṉṟum uttamam eṉṟum mattiyiluḷḷa mūṉṟum mattiyamamākum eṉṟum kuṟinta aḷavuḷḷa mūṉṟum atamam ākum eṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvitamākat tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ aḷaviṉāl kiṭikkum iliṅkattiṉ uyaram oṉpatu vitamākum.
tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ vāyil uyarattiṟkuc camamāṉa tiruuruvam uttamaliṅkamākum. 2/3 paṅku uyaram uḷḷatu atamaliṅkamākum. ivvāṟu palvēṟu vitamāṉa aḷavi muṟi kaḷi vakuttut tiruuruvam camikka vēṇṭiya muṟikaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
aḷavi muṟi
tiruuruvaṅkaḷi amikkum muṟikaḷ ākamaṅkaḷilum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷilum teḷivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭimaṅkaḷic ceyyum tapatikaḷ inta nūṟkaḷi aṉucarittu tiruuruvaṅkaḷi amikkiṟārkaḷ. civaṉ kōyilkaḷil perumpālum tiru uṇṇāḻikiyil piratiṭṭi ceyyappaṭṭiruppatu iliṅkamē yākum.2 iliṅkam 7 vakippaṭum 1. cuyampu, 2. tēvikam, 3. teyvīkam, 4. irāṭcacam, 5. māṉiṭam, 6. ārcam, 7. pāṇam eṉappaṭum. iḵtaṉṟiyum palapirivukaḷ uḷa. ivi ovvoṉṟilum pala uṭpirivukaḷum uḷa.
āṉāl paṭimaṅkaḷic ceypavarkaḷ iliṅkaṅkaḷip potuvāka niṭkaḷam, cakaḷaniṭkaḷam, cakaḷam eṉṟu pirit tuḷḷārkaḷ. ivi namatu paṭimaṅkaḷiṉ pariṇāma vaḷarcciyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu pirikkappaṭṭavikaḷākum. niṭkaḷam eṉpatu uruvamiṉṟi ellāk kōyilkaḷilum viḷaṅkum cātāraṇa iliṅkamākum. cakaḷaniṭkaḷam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvatu mukaliṅkamākum. atāvatu āvuṭiyārukku mēl uruṇṭiyākat tūṇvaṭivil kāṇappaṭum vaṭivattil kaṇ, mūkkuc cevi, vāy, neṟṟi, tali, makuṭam mutaliyavikaḷ maṭṭum kāṇappaṭuvatākum. cakaḷaliṅkam eṉpatu mukam, ki, kāl, pātam, mārpu mutaliya ellā uṟuppu kaḷum aminta mūrttikaḷiṉ uruvamākum. atāvatu teṉmukak kaṭavuḷ tirumaṇamūrtti (kalyāṇa cuntarar) piṭcāṭaṉar pōṉṟa muḻuuruvam oḷirum mūrttamākum. aṭṭa mūrttaṅkaḷum makātēva mūrttaṅkaḷum, civamūrttaṅkaḷum niṭkaḷaliṅkattiṉiṉṟē pariṇa mittavi eṉṟu civākamaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
mūrttikaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ ākamaṅkaḷi aṉucarittu aṇu vaḷavum picakātu ceyyappaṭum. aḷavikaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷit taḻuvic ceyyappaṭum. tapatikaḷ kiyāḷum aḷavikaḷ aṅkulam tālam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. mukattiṉ nīḷam tālam eṉappaṭum. oru tālattiṉ paṉṉiraṇṭil oru paṅku aṅkulam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. inta aṅkulam nāṉku vakippaṭum. avi pērāṅkulam, māṉāṅkulam, māttirāṅkulam, tēkalaptāṅkulam eṉpaṉa. pērāṅkulamāvatu uruvattiṉ viral akalatti aṅkulamākak koḷḷutalākum. māṉāṅkulam maṭṭappalakiyil poṟikkap paṭṭa niliyāṉa aḷavākum. atāvatu 3/4 aṅkulam 1 iñcu ākum. māttirāṅkulam paṭimattic ceyyum tapatiyiṉ naṭuviralil uḷḷa iraṇṭāvatu veḷiyiṉ nīḷamākum. tēkāptaṅkulam eṉpatu paṭimattiṉ uruvatti 124 allatu 120 allatu 116 pākamākap piritta oru pakutiyākum. itu tālaṅkaḷukkut takkavāṟu māṟutal peṟum. tamiḻakattil aḷavu kōlkaḷ 24 viralkaḷ (aṅkulam) koṇṭaviyāka irukkiṉṟaṉa.
nūṉeṟi marapi riṅka maḷakkuṅ
kōleṉa virupattu nāl viralāka
eṉṟu tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil kūṟappaṭuvatu namatu karutti valiyuṟuttuvatākum.1
iḵtaṉṟic cila aṟiñarkaḷ tālam eṉṟa vaṭamoḻikku uḷḷaṅki eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟukiṟārkaḷ. itaṉi ātarittu cila tapatikaḷ uḷḷaṅkiyiṉ kuṟukkaḷavit tālam eṉṟu kūṟuki ṟārkaḷ. āṉāl akarātikaḷil tālam eṉpatu oru cāṇ allatu 9 aṅkulam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vīṭukaḷil ciṟu cantiṉ vaḻiyākak katiravaṉiṉ kiraṇaṅkaḷ vīṭṭiṟkuḷ varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟil mikac ciṟiya tūcikaḷ pōṉṟa tūḷikaḷ iruppatip pārkkiṉṟōm. mika nuṇṇiya oru tūḷi paramāṇu eṉappaṭum. intap paramāṇukkaḷ oru tūḷi paramāṇu eṉappaṭum. intap paramāṇukkaḷ palacērntu oru irēṇu ākiṟatu. ivvāṟu pala irēṇukkaḷ cērntāl urōmkali ākiṟatu. ivvāṟē cīkā, yūkam, yavam eṉṟu aḷavikaḷ vaḷarntu koṇṭē pōkiṉṟaṉa. yavam eṉṟāl kōtumi. oru kōtumiyiṉ mattiyiṉ kuṟukkaḷaviṟku yavam eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. yavam - atāvatu kōtumi ariciyiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu eṭṭuk koṇṭatu oru aṅkulam ākum. paṉṉiraṇṭu aṅkulam oru tālamākum. iraṇṭu tālaṅkaḷ koṇṭatu oru ciṣṭu - katam. irupattēḻu aṅkulam koṇṭatu oru taṉurkkiraham.
ivvāṟē muḻam ciṣṭṭu, pirapattiyam, taṉur muṣṭṭi, taṉurkirakam eṉṟu nāṉku vakiyākac ciṟpa aḷavikaḷ kaṇak kiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cayaṉam, ācaṉam ākiyavikaḷic ciṣṭṭu aḷavilum pirakāram, ampalam, maṇṭapam, illaṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷip pirapattiyam eṉṟa aḷavilum araṇmaṉi, kuḷam, kāḷi kōyil mutaliyavikaḷit taṉurmuṣṭi aḷavilum āṟu, vāykkāl, tōṇṭuvatu, cālikaḷ amippatu pōṉṟavikaḷit taṉurk kiraka aḷavilum ceyya vēṇṭum. inta muṟi māṉāṅkulam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. itu kāraṇākamattil virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iḵtaṉṟi, ellāp poruḷkaḷukkum oru potuvāṉa aḷavi muṟiyum ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu aṭiyil   varumāṟu :
8 paramāṇu  = 1 vālākkiram
8 vālakkiram  = 1 ilīkkiyi (ilīṭci)
8 ilīkkiyi  = 1 iyūki
8 iyūki  = 1 iyavi
8 iyavi  = 1 mānāṅkulam
24 aṅkulam = 1 ṣṭṭu (2 muḻam)
ciṟpiyiṉ valakki naṭuviraliṉ naṭukkaṇuviṉ nīḷam allatu akalamāṉatu māttirāṅkulam eṉappaṭum.
paṭimattiṉ uyaratti 124 kūṟukaḷākac ceyyatu avikaḷil oru kūṟu tēkalaptāṅkulamākum.
māttiram - mōṭcam. amparam - viṣṇu - muktam ic coṟkaḷ ōr aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷ.
paṭcam - aṭci - kōlakam - acuvinī - vihakam - kalā - tuvijaṉ - yukamam - atyuktiyam - vāyu caraṇam ippataṅkaḷ 2 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷākum.
īcāṭci - mattiyam - kālam - kuṇam - akkiṉi ākiya pataṅkaḷ 3 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷākum.
karaṇam - jāti - varṇam, piratiṭṭi, vētam, tōyam, yukamatturiyam - apaja jāṉaṉam, pākam, muṣṭi iccoṟkaḷ 4 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷ.
vicayam - intiriyam, pūtam, icu, pirutivī, cuppiratiṣṭitā (īcāṉaṉam - karmāṅkam) iccoṟkaḷ 5 aṅkulattiṉ peyar kaḷākum.
camayāṉaṉam, karmā, aṅkam, kucikam, irutu, kumāraṉ, kiruttiki, iracam, kāyattiri, tūmam ippataṅkaḷ 6 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷ.
ampōniti, pātāḷam lōkam, uṣṇik, urōhiṇi, nakam, māturu, tātu, tūvīpam, muṉikic coṟkaḷ 7 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷām.
pantam aṉuṣṭṭup vacu, taṇam, urakātipar, tikkacam, lōkapālar ākiya coṟkaḷ 8 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷām.
nava, nantar kirakam, irattiṉam, pirukati, tiri tiri, cūttirakam, mārkkam, attuvā, kati ākiya pataṅkaḷ 9 aṅkulattiṉ peyar kaḷākum.
pirāturpavam, nāṭi, tarumam, paṅkati, tikaruviti, cōtaṉam, caṉmakam ākiya iccoṟkaḷ 10 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷām.
tiruṣrup, uruttiraṉ ākiya coṟkaḷ 11  aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷām.
iyamam, irāci, mukam, tālam, vitatira piratēci nī, tiripākam ākiya iccoṟkaḷ 12 aṅkulattiṉ peyarkaḷām.
māṉam, piramāṇam, uṉmāṉam, parimāṇam, upamāṇam, ilampamāṉam eṉa māṉam (aḷavu) āṟu vakippaṭum.
uruvaṅkaḷiṉ uyaram akalam iraṇṭiṟkum potuvāka māṉam eṉṟu collappaṭum.
viṣkampam, vicālam eṉṉum pataṅkaḷ piramāṇattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum. (ivi potuvāka akalattiṟkup payaṉ paṭuttappaṭum coṟkaḷākum.)
uccirayam, tuṅkam, uccirāyam, uṉṉatam, utayam, utcetam, uccam, arttam ākiya pataṅkaḷ utmāṉam eṉappaṭum. (ivi uyarattiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum pataṅkaḷ)
pariṇāmam, nākam, viruti, āviruti, āvirutam ākiyavikaḷ pariṇāham eṉappaṭum. (ivi cutta aḷaviṟkup payaṉpaṭuttap paṭum pataṅkaḷ.)
nīppiram, vivaram, antaram ākiya pataṅkaḷ upamāṉam eṉappaṭum. (ivikaḷ mattiyamāṉatti velattiyik kūṟum pataṅkaḷ)
uruvaṅkaḷiṉ pakkaṅkaḷ muṉpakkam (piṉpakkam) ivi kaḷil kayiṟukaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu akkayiṟukaḷiliruntu uruvaṅ kaḷi aḷaviṭuvataṟku ilampamāṉam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum. 
amaram eṉṉum nūlil peruviralil iruntu muṟiyē cuṭṭuviral naṭuviral aṇiviral variyuḷḷa aḷaviṟku muṟiyē pirātēcam, tālam kōkarṇam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ciṟpa rattiṉam eṉṉum nūl peruviralil iruntu cuṭṭuviral mutaliya 
4 viralkaḷ variyuḷḷa aḷaviṟku muṟiyē pirātēcam, tālam, kōkarṇam, vitati eṉṟu kūṟukiṟatu.
oru paṭimattiṉ uyaratti antanta vaṭivattiṟkuriya tālak kaṇakkil pirittu aḷavu kāṇpatē tēkāṅkulam eṉappaṭum. inta aḷavu muṟikaḷil paṭimaṅkaḷip palavita aḷavu muṟiyil ceykiṉṟārkaḷ. paṭimattiṉ uyaratti aḷaviṭuvatu māṉam eṉappaṭum. cuṟṟaḷavi aḷaviṭuvatu piramāṇam eṉavum, kaṉa aḷavik kaṇakkiṭuvatu nāka māṉam eṉavum neṟṟi nācākkaram toṭṭup pātam vari aḷaviṭuvatu ilampamāṉam eṉavum piratiṭṭi ceyyappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷ, pīṭaṅkaḷ mutaliyavikaḷi aḷaviṭuvatu uḷmāṉam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
oru paṭimam 7 tā uyaramiruntāl vāmaṉam eṉavum 8 tāḷa uyaramiruntāl māṉuṭam eṉavum, 9 tāla uyaramiruntāl teyvīkam eṉavum, 10 tāla uyaramiruntāl irākkatam eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
mūrttikaḷiṉ aḷavikaḷ
ciṟpikaḷ tam viruppappaṭi allatu maṉṉaṉ allatu makkaḷ viruppappaṭi tiruuruvaṅkaḷukku aḷavukaḷ kaṟpikkak kūṭātu. ataṟku ākamaṅkaḷum ciṟpanūṟkaḷum cila vitikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mūrttikaḷiṉ uyaratti aṭiyiṟ kaṇṭa patiṉoru vakikaḷil nirṇayam ceyyalām eṉṟu cakaḷātikāram kūṟukiṟatu. cila ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil oṉṟiraṇṭu vitikaḷ kūṭṭiyum kuṟittum kūṟiyiruk kalām. tapatikaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil ētāvatu oṉṟip piṉ paṟṟavēṇṭum. akattiyam aruḷiya cakaḷātikāram eṉṉum nūlil mūrttikaḷiṉ aḷavip paṟṟi naṉku kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
1. 	tiruuṇṇāḻikiyil uḷḷa iliṅkattiṉ aḷavik koṇṭum.
2. 	tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ uṭpuṟa aḷavik koṇṭum
3. 	tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ vāyil aḷavik koṇṭum
4. 	tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ atiṭṭāṉam allatu aṭippaṭi yiṉ aḷavik koṇṭum
5. 	pātam atāvatu aṭippaṭiyiṉ mītu kaṭṭappaṭum pakutiyiṉ uyarattik koṇṭum
6. 	attam eṉṉum muḻu aḷavālum
7. 	tālam eṉṉum cāṇ aḷavālum
8. 	kōyilik kaṭṭa muṉ vantuḷḷa talivaṉuṭiya uyarattik koṇṭum.
9.	mūlap pērāṅkulam atāvatu uṇṇāḻikiyiṉ uyarattit tālamāṉa vitippaṭi ataṟkuriya aṅkulat tokiyāl vakuttuk kiṭitta aṅkula aḷavālum
10.	māṉāṅkulam - atāvatu yavi, nel mutaliya tāṉiyaṅ kaḷiṉ aḷaviṉāl nirṇayikkappaṭṭa aṅkula aḷavālum
11. 	māttirāṅkulam - atāvatu tirukkōyili amikka muṉ vantirukkum talivaṉuṭiya viraliṉ naṭuppakutiyiṉ nīḷam allatu akalamāka nirṇayikkappaṭṭa aṅkulattālum muṭivu ceyyalām.
ivi ovvoṉṟum pala vakippaṭum. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka mutalāvatākak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa uṇṇāḻikiyil uḷḷa iliṅkam aḷavik koṇṭu mūrttiyiṉ uyaratti nirṇayam ceyyum muṟiyi ārāyvōm.
iliṅkattiṉ pūjāmcam - atāvatu pūci ceyyum mēlpākattiṉ utayam eṉṟu kūṟum uyaram allatu tāram eṉṉum kuṟukkaḷavu (viṭṭam allatu nāham) eṉṉum cuṟṟaḷavu oṉṟip patiṉāṟu paṅku ceytu, antap paṅkukaḷil 16 - paṅkukaḷiyum appaṭiyē vittuk koṇṭō allatu 3, 5, 7, 9 allatu 16 paṅkukaḷik kūṭṭiyō kuṟittō mūrttikaḷiṉ uyaratti muṭivu ceyyalām. 16 paṅkukaḷōṭu 3, 5, 7, 9, 11 paṅkukaḷik kūṭṭum poḻutu 19, 21, 23, 25 allatu 27 paṅkukaḷ uḷḷa aintu uyaraṅkaḷ kiṭikkum. kuṟikkum pōtu 13, 11, 9, 7, 5 intap pākaṅkaḷ uḷḷa aintu uyaraṅkaḷ kiṭikkum. kūṭṭāmalum kuṟikkāmalum vittuk koṇṭāl 16 paṅkukaḷ uḷḷa uyaram kiṭikkum. āka 11 - vaki uyaraṅkaḷ kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu cuyampu liṅkaṅkaḷ - atāvatu tāmāka uṇṭāṉa iliṅkaṅkaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷik koṇṭum putitāka nirmāṇikkappaṭṭa iliṅkaṅ kaḷiṉ aḷavukaḷik koṇṭum pūcāmcattik koṇṭum1 tārattik koṇṭa aḷavukaḷum nākattik koṇṭa aḷavukaḷu māka 33 aḷavukaḷ kiṭikkum.
ivvāṟē tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ uṭpuṟa aḷavu, tiruuṇṇāḻikiyiṉ vāyil aḷavu ākiyavikaḷi vevvēṟu vitamāka kūṟu ceytu mūrttikaḷiṉ uyaratti nirṇayikkum muṟikaḷ ciṟpa nūṟkaḷil virivākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

taca tāla ilakkaṇam (aḷavi muṟi)
tiruuruvam
putiya kal ūḻi tēyntu māyntatum, tamiḻakattil irumpu ūḻi arumpu tuḷirttatu. maliyip piḷantu cilikaḷ vaṭikkum kali putturuvam peṟṟu eḻat toṭaṅkiyatu. civaliṅkat tirumēṉi yil civaperumāṉ mukammaṭṭum mutalil uruppeṟṟu paṭippaṭi yāy civaperumāṉ muḻuuruvamum civaliṅkattōṭu iṇittuc ceyyap peṟṟup piṉṉar civaperumāṉuṭiya muḻuuruvam taṉiyāka uruvākkap peṟṟatu. avvuruvam civaperumāṉuṭiya aruḷ kōlam eṉappaṭṭatu. ikkōlam mūrttam eṉak kūṟappaṭum. mūrttam (kōlam) palavāṟāka eṇṇilāteḻuntaṉa.
tiruuruvaṅkaḷ ātiyil maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭṭup piṉ atu cūḷiyiliṭṭu cuṭappaṭṭu uṟutiyuṭiyatāy ākkap peṟṟatu. iṉṟum icakki ammaṉ, māriyammaṉ, pēcci ammaṉ pōṉṟa tāyt teyva vaṭivaṅkaḷ ciṟṟūrkaḷil cuṭumaṇṇāl ceyyap paṭṭu vaḻipaṭap peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. appāl teyva uruvaṅkaḷ marattālum, kallālum, paṭikattālum, aimpoṉṉālum ceyyap peṟṟut tiruuruvamākkum kali vaḷarcci eytiyatu.
ciṟpa viti muṟikaḷ
tiruuruvaṅkaḷip paṭippataṟku ākamaṅkaḷum, ciṟpa nūlkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟil cilikaḷ allatu tātup paṭivaṅkaḷiṉ uyaram, tōṟṟam, mukapāvam, uṟuppukkaḷ, kikaḷil irukka vēṇṭiya āyutaṅkaḷ, maṟṟum poruṭkaḷ, kālkaḷiṉ amippu, pīṭaṅkaḷ, tiruvācikaḷiṉ amippu ākiya pala ariya ceytikaḷ viḷakkappaṭṭuc caṭṭamākkap peṟṟaṉa.
ciṟpanūlilṉpaṭi, teyva uruvaṅkaḷ maṇṇāl ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟāl mutalil cūlatāpaṉam ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum. cilajamāṉāl mutalil cilācaṅkaraṇam ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum. tātup poruṭkaḷil paṭimam ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭumāṉāl mutalil tēṉ meḻukil uruvam amikkappaṭavēṇṭum. (matuc ciṣṭam ceyya vēṇṭum) ulōkaṅkaḷil uruvaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟāl mutaṉ mutalākat tirukkōyiliṉ muka maṇṭapattil naṭuvaṇ campānellip parappa vēṇṭum. appāl ataṉ mītu tāmari malarkaḷiyum ēṉiya malarkaḷiyum parappa vēṇṭum. ataṉ mēlākat tarppaṅkaḷip parappa vēṇṭum. piṉṉar, palakiyip parappiṉ mītu vittu arukē ācāriyaṉ, tapati, āṇiyāḷar ākiya mūṉṟu pērkaḷum amarntiruntu āka vēṇṭiya maṅkala kāriyattik kaṇikka vēṇṭum. uruvākka vēṇṭiya tiruuruvaṅkaḷukku (māṉattiṟkēṟpa) uṟuppukkaḷi maṇṇil allatu marappalakiyil tīṭṭa vēṇṭum. appaṭic ceyyappaṭum vaṭivam kiḻakkē taliyum mēlē mukamum irukkumpaṭi palaki mītu vikka vēṇṭum.
itaṉ teṉpuṟam civākkiṉiyi vaḷarttu mūla mantiratti ōtik koṇṭu, anta neruppiṉ naṭuvē camittu, cōṟu, ācayam ākiyavikaḷāl 108 - taṭavi ōmam ceyya vēṇṭum. piṉṉar viyākirutikaḷāl ōmam ceytu appāl pūraṇāhutiyic ceyya vēṇṭum. anta neruppiṉ naṭuvē uruvattit tiyāṉam ceyya vēṇṭum. avvuruvi iṟivaṉ tiruvaṭivāka eṇṇit tūpam kāṭṭa vēṇṭum. (irutaya mantiram colli neruppiṉiṉṟu ātma corūpattip pimpattil pāvaṉi ceyya vēṇṭum.)
akkirapākattiliruntu (anta neruppiṉiṉṟu tiru uruvatti) muṟippaṭi (aṅkaṅkaḷi) aṉucantāṉam ceyya vēṇṭum.
mutalil īcāṉa mantirattāl ucciyiyum, kavaca mantirattāl makuṭattiyum (taliyaṇiyiyum) attira mantirattāl ki kaḷiyum, cattiya mantirattāl pātaṅkaḷiyum aṉucantāṉam ceyya vēṇṭum. ataṉpiṉ tiruuruviṉ ellā uṟuppukkaḷiyum (aṅkaṅkaḷiyum) aṉucantāṉam ceytu aṅkacampūrttiyik kaṟpittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. uṟuppukkaḷi niṟivuṭiyatākap pāvaṉi ceytal vēṇṭum. piṉṉar tuṇi uṟuppukkaḷiyum (upāṅkaṅkaḷiyum) ataṉ piṟaku pirattiyāṅkaṅkaḷiyum (utavi yāka iṇikkappaṭṭa uṟuppukaḷiyum) pāvaṉi ceytu kaṟpittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṉṉar aṭṭa tirimcat (38) kālānyācatti antanta uṟuppukkaḷil vikka vēṇṭum.
āṭi aṇikaḷ, āyutaṅkaḷ, pūṇūl ākiyavi upāṅkat tuṭaṉ cērntavikaḷākum. antat tiruvuruvaṅkaḷukku aṭiyāḷa māka attiramāṉatu (āyutamāṉatu) pirattiyāṅkattil cērntatākac ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum.1
uttama taca tālam
tamiḻakattil teyva uruvaṅkaḷ, ciṟpanūṟkaḷilum ākamaṅ kaḷilum kūṟappaṭṭa vitimuṟikaḷi aṉucarittu aṭiyil kūṟap paṭum tāla aḷavi muṟiyiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu amikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tacatālam (10-tālam) mutal tiri (3) tālam variyil ovvorutālamum uttamam, mattiyamam, atamam eṉa mūṉṟu vakiyākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. uttama tacatālam 124 aṅkulam ākum. mattiyama tacatālam 120 aṅkulamākum. atamatacatālam 112 aṅkulamākum. mattiyama navatālam 108 aṅkulamākum. atamanava tālam 104 aṅkulamākum. uttama aṭṭatālam mutaliya maṟṟat tālaṅkaḷ yāvum muṟiyē 4, 4 aṅkulam kuṟinta aḷavu.
uttama tacatālam eṉpatu tiruuruvi ucci mutal uḷḷaṅ kālvari nūṟṟiruppattu nāṉku pākaṅkaḷāka (tēkāṅkulamāka)c ceyya vēṇṭum. naraṉ, nārāyaṇaṉ, tirumāl, naraciṅkaṉ, nāṉmukaṉ, makēcuvaraṉ ākiyavarkaḷiṉ tiruuruvaṅkaḷum, vāṇācuraṉ, makāpali, intiraṉ, aruccuṉaṉ, kaṅkāḷar, taṭcaṇā mūrtti mutaliyavarkaḷiṉ tiruuruvaṅkaḷum tacatāla (pattuttāla) aḷavu uyaram uḷḷatāka irukka vēṇṭum.
mattiyama tacatālam
mattiyama taca tālam eṉpatu uruvam ucci mutal uḷḷaṅkāl vari nūṟṟirupatu tēkāṅkulamākac ceyya vēṇṭum. inta aḷavil umātēvi, ucātēvi, kalimakaḷ, pūtēvi, turkki, tirumakaḷ, captamātarkaḷ, cēṭṭātēvi, caṇṭikā tēvi, piravar, vētāḷam, varākam, ayakkirivar, irāmaṉ, vēṇu kōpālaṉ, kāḷi, urukkumaṇi, murukaṉ mutaliyōr uruvaṅkaḷ ittāla aḷavil ceyyalām.
atama taca tālam
ittāla aḷaviṉpaṭi uruvaṅkaḷ aṭimutal nuṉivari nūṟṟup patiṉāṟu tēkāṅkulamākap pirikkappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. ivvaḷavil murukaṉ, arukkaṉ, cantiraṉ, intiraṉ, paṉṉiraṇṭu ātittiyarkaḷ, patiṉoru uruttirarkaḷ, navakkirakaṅkaḷ, aṭṭavacukkaḷ ākiyavarkaḷiṉ tiruuruvaṅkaḷ ceyyalām.
navatālam
uttama navatālam
ivvaḷavuppaṭi uruvaṅkaḷ uccimutal uḷḷaṅkāl vari nūṟṟup paṉṉiraṇṭu tēkāṅkulamākap pirikkappaṭa vēṇṭum. inta aḷavil civaperumāṉiṉaṭṭa mūrtta uruvaṅkaḷum, tuvārapālakar, pālakiruṭṭiṉaṉ, vittiyātiparkaḷ, lōka pālarkaḷ, tēvarkaḷ palarāmaṉ, cīti, vaḷḷi mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷum amikkalām.
mattiyama navatālam
inta aḷavuppaṭi uruvatti nūṟṟeṭṭut tēkāṅkulamākap pirikka vēṇṭum. inta aḷavil yaṭcarkaḷ, apcaracukaḷ, attira mūrttikaḷ, maruttukaṇaṅkaḷ mutaliya uruvaṅkaḷ amikkalām.
atama navatālam
inta aḷavuppaṭi uruvaṅkaḷi aṭi mutal muṭi vari nūṟṟu nālu tēkāṅkulamākap pirikka vēṇṭum. acurarkaḷ, cittarkaḷ, kantarvarkaḷ, piturukkaḷ, civaṉaṭiyārkaḷ uruvaṅkaḷ inta aḷavi aṉucarittuc ceyyalām.
ēṉiya tālaṅkaḷ
aṭṭa tālattāl cāttiyarkaḷiyum, captatālattāl vētāḷam picācu mutaliyavaṟṟiyum caṣṭatālattāl kupajā (kūṉar)kaḷiyum, pañcatālattāl piḷḷiyār, kiruṭṭiṉaṉ ākiyavarkaḷiyum caturt tālattāl pāla pērattiyum tiritālattāl kiṉṉararkaḷiyum tuvitālattāl kimpuruṭar kaḷiyum ēkatālattāl kūrmattiyum ceyya vēṇṭum eṉṟu cirikā ciyalacilpa cāttiram kūṟukiṟatu.
tāla aḷavikaḷ uruvaṅkaḷiṉ uṟuppukkaḷ oṉṟuk koṉṟu poruttamāṉa aḷavil amikkat tēkāṅkulaṅ kaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. uruvaṅkaḷiṉ uyaram piṉvarum aḷavi muṟikaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu amikkap paṭukiṟatu.
